   560	| 0.1.0 | 2026-07-15 | Established the Step 1–5-aligned recommendation architecture outline. | ADR-0003 |
     1	# Volume X — Research Automation
     2	
     3	Status: Reserved outline; not implemented by this document
     4	Version: 0.1.0
     5	Last revised: 2026-07-15
     6	
     7	## 1. Purpose
     8	
     9	Define a future controlled automation layer for proposing and coordinating
    10	bounded research while preserving explicit policy, budgets, audit, and human
    11	authority.
    12	
    13	## 2. Scope
    14	
    15	### 2.1 In scope
    16	
    17	Future campaign goals, proposal policies, budgets, stopping conditions,
    18	selection/evaluation loops, audit, and operator control.
    19	
    20	### 2.2 Out of scope
    21	
    22	Unbounded autonomy, self-modifying production code, implicit experiment
    23	creation, live trading, or bypassing scheduler/lifecycle ownership.
    24	
    25	### 2.3 Current implementation status
    26	
    27	Reserved. Existing recommendation and continuation capabilities do not form an
    28	autonomous research agent and MUST NOT be composed as one informally.
    29	
    30	## 3. Responsibilities
    31	
    32	### 3.1 Owned responsibilities
    33	
    34	Future automation owns declared campaign policy and budget accounting only.
    35	
    36	### 3.2 Dependencies
    37	
    38	Consumes advisory evidence from Volumes VI/VIII/IX and requests work only
    39	through accepted Volume VII/XI interfaces.
    40	
    41	### 3.3 Prohibited responsibilities
    42	
    43	MUST NOT bypass review, invent authorization, exceed budgets, mutate model math,
    44	or directly control workers.
    45	
    46	## 4. Architecture
    47	
    48	### 4.1 Components
    49	
    50	Potential components are campaign policy, planner, budget ledger, proposal
    51	adapter, decision/audit repository, operator controls, and scheduler adapter.
    52	
    53	### 4.2 Control flow
    54	
    55	Explicit campaign → bounded evidence snapshot → proposal → required review or
    56	policy gate → lifecycle request → scheduler execution → evidence update → stop.
    57	
    58	### 4.3 Ownership boundaries
    59	
    60	Automation proposes and accounts. Domain services validate. Experiment
    61	lifecycle creates. Scheduler claims and runs. Operators retain pause/stop power.
    62	
    63	## 5. Data model
    64	
    65	### 5.1 Authoritative entities
    66	
    67	Future campaign, policy version, budget, proposal, decision, execution request,
    68	evidence snapshot, and stop reason.
    69	
    70	### 5.2 Provenance and versions
    71	
    72	Every decision records exact inputs, policy canonical text/version, budgets,
    73	software version, and downstream IDs.
    74	
    75	### 5.3 Invariants and legacy data
    76	
    77	Budget consumption is monotonic and auditable. Missing authority or provenance
    78	blocks action. Advisory status never becomes implicit authorization.
    79	
    80	## 6. Transactions
    81	
    82	### 6.1 Read paths
    83	
    84	Planning consumes named immutable evidence snapshots.
    85	
    86	### 6.2 Write paths
    87	
    88	Decision, budget reservation, and execution request require an explicit atomic
    89	or compensating protocol before implementation.
    90	
    91	### 6.3 Failure semantics
    92	
    93	Uncertain budget or request state fails closed and requires reconciliation.
    94	
    95	## 7. Concurrency
    96	
    97	### 7.1 Conflict domain
    98	
    99	Same campaign budget, proposal, or execution request.
   100	
   101	### 7.2 Locking and serialization
   102	
   103	Future ledgers use row locks/conditional updates and authoritative request IDs.
   104	
   105	### 7.3 Winner, loser, and retry outcomes
   106	
   107	At most one accepted request consumes a reservation; retries return the same
   108	request only when authoritative idempotency identity matches.
   109	
   110	## 8. CLI
   111	
   112	### 8.1 Commands and validation
   113	
   114	Future start/pause/stop/status commands require explicit campaign IDs, limits,
   115	and confirmation for mutations.
   116	
   117	### 8.2 Machine output
   118	
   119	Events expose policy, budget, evidence, decision, request, and stop reason.
   120	
   121	### 8.3 Human output
   122	
   123	Summaries state remaining budget, current authority, and whether any work was
   124	actually requested or queued.
   125	
   126	## 9. Testing
   127	
   128	### 9.1 Pure tests
   129	
   130	Policy, budgets, stopping, selection, and deterministic planning.
   131	
   132	### 9.2 Persistence and migration tests
   133	
   134	Ledger monotonicity, request idempotency, audit immutability, and permissions.
   135	
   136	### 9.3 Concurrency and integration tests
   137	
   138	Budget races, duplicate requests, pause/stop races, and scheduler isolation.
   139	
   140	### 9.4 Regression boundaries
   141	
   142	Recommendation, lifecycle, continuation, and scheduler semantics remain owned
   143	by their volumes.
   144	
   145	## 10. Operational safety
   146	
   147	### 10.1 Runtime isolation
   148	
   149	Automation defaults disabled and cannot run from documentation or mere schema
   150	presence.
   151	
   152	### 10.2 Permissions and destructive operations
   153	
   154	Least-privilege roles, explicit budgets, operator stop controls, and audit are
   155	mandatory before deployment.
   156	
   157	### 10.3 Observability and recovery
   158	
   159	Durable campaign status, reservations, requests, outcomes, and reconciliation
   160	must survive process failure.
   161	
   162	## 11. Future extensions
   163	
   164	### 11.1 Approved extension points
   165	
   166	None are executable yet; interfaces named here are architectural placeholders.
   167	
   168	### 11.2 Deferred capabilities
   169	
   170	Campaign planning, automated conversion, adaptive search, and autonomous
   171	experimentation.
   172	
   173	### 11.3 Required decisions
   174	
   175	Multiple ADRs are required for authority, budgets, safety, policy identity,
   176	conversion, scheduler capacity, and shutdown before implementation.
   177	
   178	## 12. References
   179	
   180	- [Volume I §17.4](Volume_I_Foundation.md)
   181	- [Volume VII](Volume_VII_Experiment_Lifecycle.md)
   182	- [Volume VIII](Volume_VIII_Recommendation_Engine.md)
   183	- [Volume XI](Volume_XI_Scheduler.md)

   185	## 13. Revision history
   186	
   187	| Version | Date | Change | ADR |
   188	|---|---|---|---|
   189	| 0.1.0 | 2026-07-15 | Reserved the research-automation architecture and safety gates. | — |
     1	Phase 5 Recommendation Campaign Outcome Assessment
     2	==================================================
     3	
     4	Purpose and boundary
     5	--------------------
     6	
     7	Step 5a defines a pure, immutable, point-in-time domain assessment of the
     8	scientific outcomes associated with an exact recommendation-campaign
     9	materialization.  It is read-only, non-persistent, non-authoritative, and does
    10	not declare campaign success or authorize follow-up work.
    11	
    12	Lifecycle, consistency, and comparison
    13	--------------------------------------
    14	
    15	Lifecycle is explicit (``not_terminal``, ``succeeded``, ``failed``,
    16	``cancelled``, or ``unknown``).  Consistency is independent
    17	(``consistent`` or ``inconsistent``), and inconsistent downstream evidence is
    18	retained with deterministic diagnostic codes.  Callers can also carry a known
    19	inconsistent lifecycle or workflow observation without changing or discarding
    20	the exact materialized-member identity.  Scientific comparisons are derived
    21	only for consistent succeeded members.  Member outcomes distinguish not-ready,
    22	comparable success, context-changed success, metric-gap success, terminal
    23	failure, terminal cancellation, and inconsistency.
    24	
    25	Multi-metric evidence
    26	---------------------
    27	
    28	Source and result evidence contain metric collections.  Collections reject a
    29	duplicate metric identity and are canonicalized by metric identity, so input
    30	order does not affect output or assessment identity.  A comparison is derived
    31	for every metric identity in the source/result union.  Member outcome counts
    32	remain separate from metric-comparison and delta counts.
    33	
    34	Provenance and deterministic identity
    35	-------------------------------------
    36	
    37	Each materialized member may identify its expected downstream experiment.
    38	Succeeded-result evidence is checked against that exact experiment.  Terminal
    39	members require an expected experiment, and expected experiments, observed
    40	result experiments, result models, and typed result identities may not be
    41	reused across members.  Unexpected result evidence is retained and diagnosed
    42	rather than discarded.  Provenance defects remain inconsistent members when
    43	top-level materialization membership is trustworthy; invalid or ambiguous
    44	member identity fails the assessment.  Campaign and materialization inputs
    45	must agree on both the persisted campaign-approval ID and its canonical
    46	identity hash.
    47	
    48	Canonical text and hashing bind lifecycle, consistency, diagnostics, canonical
    49	source and result metrics, comparisons, exact provenance, and aggregate member
    50	and metric summaries.  Authoritative campaign and materialization canonical
    51	texts are length-framed into the assessment identity alongside their compact
    52	hashes.  Canonical number formatting is locale-independent.  ``observed_at``
    53	is intentionally excluded from evidence identity.
    54	
    55	Non-goals
    56	---------
    57	
    58	Step 5a does not provide a repository, database access, persistence, service,
    59	CLI, scheduler, worker, filesystem behavior, campaign-success policy, or
    60	follow-up authorization.
    61	
    62	Step 5b authoritative-evidence integration
    63	------------------------------------------
    64	
    65	Step 5b supplies the read-only integration without changing the Step 5a domain
    66	boundary.  One PostgreSQL ``REPEATABLE READ``, ``READ ONLY`` transaction loads
    67	authoritative persisted evidence: the exact materialization through the Phase
    68	4D production loader, its exact campaign approval, and the evidence underlying
    69	lifecycle and workflow consistency from the existing Phase 5 campaign-status
    70	projection.  Step 5b builds a non-authoritative, point-in-time assessment from
    71	that evidence.  It does not reconstruct a second lifecycle classifier.
    72	
    73	The Step 5a campaign identity is the persisted approval canonical text and
    74	hash.  Its materialization input receives the materialization row's persisted
    75	``approval_identity_hash`` unchanged, so Step 5a verifies the approval-to-
    76	materialization binding.  Exact materialization member ordinal, member ID,
    77	ranking-member ID, recommendation ID, source-experiment ID, proposal ID, and
    78	the execution-linked expected experiment ID flow from the validated status
    79	snapshot.  A status member reported as inconsistent is passed to Step 5a as
    80	``inputConsistency=inconsistent``; Step 5b neither repairs nor normalizes the
    81	upstream evidence.
    82	
    83	Scientific evidence
    84	-------------------
    85	
    86	Source evidence follows the materialized recommendation's persisted source
    87	experiment, model, and analysis identities.  Source final-inference selection
    88	uses the immutable source invocation canonical text captured by the exact
    89	persisted Phase 4C proposal; it never substitutes the source experiment's
    90	current configuration.  Result evidence follows the exact conversion-
    91	execution experiment, its current linked model, and its final inference and
    92	final analysis rows.  The comparison context is taken from the selected
    93	persisted final inference identity: canonical symbol, prediction horizon,
    94	threshold, persisted window size, the versioned
    95	``inference_eval_result_label_v1`` representation of the
    96	``label_rule_id``/``target_type`` pair, and canonical ``YYYY-MM-DD`` inference
    97	range.
    98	
    99	The version-1 Step 5b mapping carries ``inference_accuracy`` and
   100	``leader_score`` with ``NumericDelta`` support.  The source values are the
   101	immutable values captured on the recommendation; result values are the final
   102	analysis values.  Missing analysis or metric evidence remains absent or null,
   103	and changed contexts remain non-comparable.  No metric is recomputed and no
   104	campaign-success threshold is applied.
   105	
   106	Other persisted analysis fields were deliberately not mapped in this version:
   107	the immutable recommendation does not retain a directly corresponding value
   108	with the same scientific meaning.  In particular, Step 5b does not reconstruct
   109	predicted-neutral proportions or evidence totals from result counts.  Such
   110	values therefore cannot be mistaken for a valid numeric delta.
   111	
   112	CLI and output
   113	--------------
   114	
   115	::
   116	
   117	   LSTM_Release \
   118	     --recommendation-campaign-outcome-assessment=MATERIALIZATION_ID
   119	
   120	The separated option form is also accepted.  The command is intrinsically
   121	read-only, so ``--yes`` and ``--dry-run`` are rejected.  Output begins with one
   122	``RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_OUTCOME_ASSESSMENT`` record and then one
   123	``RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_OUTCOME_ASSESSMENT_MEMBER`` record per exact
   124	materialized ordinal.  Records expose contract and identity hashes, the exact
   125	Step 5a aggregate classification and counts, lifecycle, separate input and
   126	final consistency, diagnostics, provenance, contexts, metrics, comparisons,
   127	explicit evidence/context-presence booleans and ``null`` values,
   128	percent-encoded variable text, the materialization's persisted campaign-
   129	approval hash, and explicit no-write/no-scheduler/no-success/no-follow-up
   130	safety fields.
   131	
   132	No schema migration or privilege change is required.  The assessment remains
   133	point-in-time and non-persistent; Step 5b inserts, updates, deletes, activates,
   134	queues, retries, and launches nothing and authorizes no follow-up.
   135	
   136	Step 5c pure outcome policy
   137	----------------------------
   138	
   139	Step 5c adds only a database-free policy and interpretation contract over one
   140	immutable Step 5a assessment.  It does not reopen source or result evidence,
   141	recompute a metric or delta, or change any Step 5a lifecycle, consistency,
   142	member-outcome, comparison, diagnostic, or aggregate classification.  Evidence
   143	classification and policy judgment remain separate fields.
   144	
   145	Policy contract version 1 has an explicit canonical identity.  Its default
   146	minimum comparable-member evidence coverage is one ``succeeded_comparable``
   147	member, and its required metric rules are ``inference_accuracy`` and
   148	``leader_score``, each with the explicit ``higher_is_favorable`` direction.
   149	Required rules are sorted by metric identity and duplicates are rejected.  A
   150	caller may construct another version-1 policy with a different positive
   151	minimum or deterministic set of metric-direction rules; changing the policy
   152	changes both policy and decision identity.  The canonical policy binds the
   153	contract version, coverage minimum, sorted metric identities and directions,
   154	fixed all-member consistency, terminal, and successful comparability
   155	requirements, favorable-interpretation requirement for operator-review
   156	eligibility, and fixed non-authorizing semantics.
   157	
   158	``minimumComparableMemberCount`` is only an advisory evidence-coverage
   159	threshold.  It is not statistical significance, confidence, causal evidence,
   160	profitability evidence, a guarantee of repeatability, or campaign success.  No
   161	materiality threshold or statistical, causal, profitability, or weighted
   162	campaign score is inferred.
   163	
   164	For every exact member and required metric, the policy output preserves the
   165	Step 5a comparison classification and delta as optional evidence, then records
   166	a separate ``favorable``, ``neutral``, ``unfavorable``, or ``not_evaluable``
   167	judgment.  It never subtracts result and source values again.  Positive and
   168	negative meaning is determined only by the versioned metric-direction rule;
   169	an exact zero remains neutral.
   170	
   171	Evidence sufficiency is separate from campaign interpretation.  Inconsistent,
   172	not-ready, cancelled, context-changed, metric-gap, missing-source,
   173	missing-result, unavailable, unsupported, missing-required-metric, and
   174	minimum-comparable-member conditions remain explicit conservative reasons.
   175	A terminal failure is adverse lifecycle evidence rather than a scientific
   176	metric gap.  Thus an all-failed campaign is interpreted ``unfavorable`` even
   177	though it lacks the minimum comparable-member evidence coverage; a failed
   178	member mixed with complete favorable evidence is ``mixed``.  Cancellation,
   179	inconsistency, unfinished lifecycle, or non-comparable successful evidence
   180	makes the campaign interpretation ``inconclusive``.  Complete comparable
   181	metric judgments yield ``favorable``, ``neutral``, ``unfavorable``, or
   182	``mixed`` without declaring the campaign successful.
   183	
   184	Follow-up eligibility is only ``eligible_for_operator_review`` when every
   185	exact member is a comparable successful outcome, the minimum evidence coverage
   186	is met, all required metrics are comparable, and the campaign interpretation
   187	is ``favorable``.  Neutral, unfavorable, mixed, and inconclusive campaigns are
   188	``not_eligible``; sufficient evidence alone is not enough.  Failure,
   189	cancellation, incompleteness, inconsistency, context change, or a metric gap
   190	also leaves it ``not_eligible``.  This is an advisory screening result, not a
   191	recommendation, approval, or authorization: the immutable result always has
   192	``follow_up_authorized=false`` and ``follow_up_authorizing=false``.
   193	
   194	Decision contract version 1 binds the complete policy canonical text and hash,
   195	the exact Step 5a assessment canonical text and hash, complete campaign
   196	approval and materialization identities, ordered member identities including
   197	the expected experiment, assessment lifecycle/consistency/outcome/diagnostics,
   198	evidence classifications, metric judgments, interpretations, reasons, counts,
   199	and explicit non-authorization result.  The complete upstream assessment
   200	canonical already binds Step 5a provenance; the direct fields are deliberately
   201	included as well because the decision carries and exposes those identities and
   202	assessment classifications.  ``observed_at`` remains display metadata and is
   203	excluded through the upstream assessment identity.  Unsupported policy or
   204	assessment contracts, invalid enums, duplicate policy metrics, duplicate or
   205	ambiguous top-level member identities, malformed identity/hash pairs, and
   206	invalid counts fail closed.
   207	
   208	Step 5c adds no repository, service, formatter, CLI, migration, privilege,
   209	table, sequence, lock, write, scheduler call, worker behavior, experiment or
   210	campaign mutation, persistence, operator-review event, follow-up generation,
   211	approval, queueing, activation, retry, continuation, or automatic action.  The
   212	existing Step 5b CLI continues to report only the non-authoritative assessment
   213	built from authoritative persisted evidence and does not silently apply outcome
   214	policy.

   243	    ResultEvidenceMissing,

   245	    ResultExperimentIdentityMismatch,
   246	    ResultModelIdentityMissing,

   248	    ResultIdentityMissing,
   249	    ResultContextMissing,
   250	    ResultExperimentReused,
   251	    ResultIdentityReused,
   252	    NumericDeltaNonFinite
   253	};

   403	struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember
   404	{

   407	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentConsistencyState consistency;
   408	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberOutcome outcome;
   409	    // Empty for consistent members; otherwise sorted in fixed code order.
   410	    const std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentDiagnosticCode>
   411	        diagnostics;
   412	    const std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence>
   413	        sourceEvidence;
   414	    const std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence>
   415	        resultEvidence;
   416	    // One entry per metric identity in the source/result union, sorted by identity.
   417	    const std::vector<
   418	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparison>
   419	        comparisons;
   420	
   421	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember(
   422	        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember&) = default;
   423	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember(
   424	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember&&) = default;
   425	    bool operator==(
   426	        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember&) const = default;
   427

   430	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity identity,
   431	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleState lifecycle,
   432	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentConsistencyState consistency,
   433	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberOutcome outcome,
   434	        std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentDiagnosticCode>
   435	            diagnostics,
   436	        std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence>
   437	            sourceEvidence,
   438	        std::optional<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence>
   439	            resultEvidence,
   440	        std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparison>
   441	            comparisons);
   442	    friend struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentBuilder;
   443	};

    63	    }

    66	}

    95	std::vector<Metric> CanonicalMetrics(std::vector<Metric> metrics)
    96	{
    97	    std::vector<const Metric*> ordered;
    98	    ordered.reserve(metrics.size());
    99	    for (const auto& metric : metrics) ordered.push_back(&metric);
   100	    std::sort(ordered.begin(), ordered.end(), [](const Metric* left,
   101	                                              const Metric* right)
   102	    {
   103	        return left->identity < right->identity;
   104	    });
   105	    for (std::size_t index = 1; index < ordered.size(); ++index)
   106	        if (ordered[index - 1]->identity == ordered[index]->identity)
   107	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   108	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_metric_identity_duplicate");
   109	    std::vector<Metric> canonical;
   110	    canonical.reserve(ordered.size());
   111	    for (const auto* metric : ordered) canonical.push_back(*metric);
   112	    return canonical;
   113	}
   114	
   115	std::vector<ResultIdentity> CanonicalResultIdentities(
   116	    std::vector<ResultIdentity> identities)
   117	{
   118	    std::vector<const ResultIdentity*> ordered;
   119	    ordered.reserve(identities.size());
   120	    for (const auto& identity : identities) ordered.push_back(&identity);
   121	    std::sort(ordered.begin(), ordered.end(),
   122	        [](const ResultIdentity* left, const ResultIdentity* right)
   123	    {
   124	        return std::tie(left->kind, left->id) <
   125	            std::tie(right->kind, right->id);
   126	    });
   127	    for (std::size_t index = 1; index < ordered.size(); ++index)
   128	        if (*ordered[index - 1] == *ordered[index])
   129	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   130	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_result_identity_duplicate");
   131	    std::vector<ResultIdentity> canonical;
   132	    canonical.reserve(ordered.size());
   133	    for (const auto* identity : ordered) canonical.push_back(*identity);
   134	    return canonical;
   135	}

   138	{
   139	    return std::to_string(value.size()) + ":" + value;
   140	}

   152	void AppendContext(std::ostringstream& out, const Context& context)
   153	{
   154	    out << LengthPrefixed(context.symbol) << ',' << context.predictionHorizon
   155	        << ',' << CanonicalRecommendationDouble(context.threshold) << ','
   156	        << context.windowSize << ',' << LengthPrefixed(context.labelDefinition)
   157	        << ',' << LengthPrefixed(context.inferenceRangeStart) << ','
   158	        << LengthPrefixed(context.inferenceRangeEnd);
   159	}
   160	
   161	void AppendOptionalContext(
   162	    std::ostringstream& out,
   163	    const std::optional<Context>& context)
   164	{
   165	    if (!context)
   166	    {
   167	        out << "absent";
   168	        return;
   169	    }
   170	    out << "present,";
   171	    AppendContext(out, *context);
   172	}

   183	{

   185	    for (const auto& metric : collection.metrics)

   187	        out << ',';

   192	void AppendSourceEvidence(
   193	    std::ostringstream& out,
   194	    const std::optional<
   195	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence>& evidence)
   196	{
   197	    if (!evidence)
   198	    {
   199	        out << "absent";
   200	        return;
   201	    }
   202	    out << "present," << evidence->sourceExperimentId << ','
   203	        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->sourceModelId) << ','
   204	        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->sourceAnalysisId) << ",context=";

   210	void AppendResultEvidence(
   211	    std::ostringstream& out,
   212	    const std::optional<
   213	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence>& evidence)
   214	{
   215	    if (!evidence)
   216	    {
   217	        out << "absent";
   218	        return;

   220	    out << "present," << OptionalLongLong(evidence->experimentId) << ','
   221	        << OptionalLongLong(evidence->modelId)

   223	    for (const auto& identity : evidence->resultIdentities)

   225	    out << ",context=";
   226	    AppendOptionalContext(out, evidence->context);
   227	    out << ",metrics=";
   228	    AppendMetrics(out, evidence->metrics);

   231	void AppendComparison(std::ostringstream& out, const Comparison& comparison)
   232	{

   239	        << ",context_difference_count="

   244	                   difference);

   292	std::string AssessmentCanonicalText(
   293	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity& campaign,
   294	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity&
   295	        materialization,
   296	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary& summary,
   297	    const std::vector<Member>& members)
   298	{

   302	        << ";assessment_contract_version="
   303	        << kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion
   304	        << ";campaign_approval_id=" << campaign.campaignApprovalId
   305	        << ";campaign_identity="
   306	        << LengthPrefixed(campaign.identityCanonical)
   307	        << ";campaign_identity_hash=" << LengthPrefixed(campaign.identityHash)
   308	        << ";materialization_id=" << materialization.materializationId
   309	        << ";materialization_contract_version="

   315	    for (const auto& member : members)
   316	    {

   318	            << member.identity.materializationMemberId << ','
   319	            << member.identity.rankingMemberId << ','
   320	            << member.identity.recommendationId << ','
   321	            << member.identity.sourceExperimentId << ','
   322	            << member.identity.proposalId << ','
   323	            << OptionalLongLong(member.identity.expectedExperimentId)
   324	            << ",lifecycle="
   325	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleStateText(
   326	                   member.lifecycle)
   327	            << ",consistency="
   328	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentConsistencyStateText(
   329	                   member.consistency)
   330	            << ",outcome="
   331	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberOutcomeText(
   332	                   member.outcome)
   333	            << ",diagnostic_count=" << member.diagnostics.size();
   334	        for (const auto diagnostic : member.diagnostics)
   335	            out << ','
   336	                << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentDiagnosticCodeText(
   337	                       diagnostic);
   338	        out << ",source=";
   339	        AppendSourceEvidence(out, member.sourceEvidence);
   340	        out << ",result=";
   341	        AppendResultEvidence(out, member.resultEvidence);
   342	        out << ",comparison_count=" << member.comparisons.size();
   343	        for (const auto& comparison : member.comparisons)
   344	        {
   345	            out << ',';
   346	            AppendComparison(out, comparison);
   347	        }
   348	    }
   349	    AppendSummary(out, summary);
   350	    return out.str();
   351	}
   352	
   353	std::vector<Difference> ContextDifferences(
   354	    const Context& source,
   355	    const Context& result)
   356	{
   357	    std::vector<Difference> differences;
   358	    if (source.symbol != result.symbol)
   359	        differences.push_back(Difference::Symbol);
   360	    if (source.predictionHorizon != result.predictionHorizon)
   361	        differences.push_back(Difference::PredictionHorizon);
   362	    if (source.threshold != result.threshold)
   363	        differences.push_back(Difference::Threshold);
   364	    if (source.windowSize != result.windowSize)
   365	        differences.push_back(Difference::WindowSize);
   366	    if (source.labelDefinition != result.labelDefinition)
   367	        differences.push_back(Difference::LabelDefinition);
   368	    if (source.inferenceRangeStart != result.inferenceRangeStart ||
   369	        source.inferenceRangeEnd != result.inferenceRangeEnd)
   370	        differences.push_back(Difference::InferenceRange);
   371	    return differences;
   372	}
   373	
   374	void CanonicalizeDiagnostics(std::vector<Diagnostic>& diagnostics)
   375	{
   376	    std::sort(diagnostics.begin(), diagnostics.end(),
   377	        [](Diagnostic left, Diagnostic right)
   378	    {
   379	        return static_cast<int>(left) < static_cast<int>(right);
   380	    });
   381	    diagnostics.erase(
   382	        std::unique(diagnostics.begin(), diagnostics.end()),
   383	        diagnostics.end());
   384	}

   392	struct MutableSummary
   393	{
   394	    int memberCount = 0;
   395	    int notTerminalLifecycleCount = 0;
   396	    int succeededLifecycleCount = 0;
   397	    int failedLifecycleCount = 0;
   398	    int cancelledLifecycleCount = 0;
   399	    int unknownLifecycleCount = 0;
   400	    int consistentMemberCount = 0;
   401	    int inconsistentMemberCount = 0;
   402	    int notReadyMemberCount = 0;
   403	    int succeededComparableMemberCount = 0;
   404	    int succeededContextChangedMemberCount = 0;
   405	    int succeededMetricGapMemberCount = 0;
   406	    int terminalFailedMemberCount = 0;
   407	    int terminalCancelledMemberCount = 0;
   408	    int inconsistentOutcomeMemberCount = 0;
   409	    std::size_t metricComparisonCount = 0;
   410	    std::size_t comparableMetricCount = 0;
   411	    std::size_t contextChangedMetricCount = 0;
   412	    std::size_t missingSourceMetricCount = 0;
   413	    std::size_t missingResultMetricCount = 0;
   414	    std::size_t metricValueUnavailableCount = 0;
   415	    std::size_t unsupportedMetricCount = 0;
   416	    std::size_t positiveDeltaCount = 0;
   417	    std::size_t zeroDeltaCount = 0;
   418	    std::size_t negativeDeltaCount = 0;
   419	};
   420	
   421	AggregateOutcome DeriveAggregateOutcome(const MutableSummary& counts)
   422	{
   423	    if (counts.inconsistentMemberCount > 0)
   424	        return AggregateOutcome::Inconsistent;
   425	    if (counts.terminalFailedMemberCount > 0)
   426	        return AggregateOutcome::HasFailures;
   427	    if (counts.terminalCancelledMemberCount > 0)
   428	        return AggregateOutcome::HasCancellations;
   429	    if (counts.notReadyMemberCount > 0)
   430	        return AggregateOutcome::NotReady;
   431	    if (counts.succeededContextChangedMemberCount > 0)
   432	        return AggregateOutcome::SucceededContextChanged;
   433	    if (counts.succeededMetricGapMemberCount > 0)
   434	        return AggregateOutcome::SucceededMetricGap;
   435	    return AggregateOutcome::SucceededComparable;
   436	}
   437	
   438	void CountMember(MutableSummary& counts, const Member& member)
   439	{
   440	    ++counts.memberCount;
   441	    switch (member.lifecycle)
   442	    {
   443	        case Lifecycle::NotTerminal:
   444	            ++counts.notTerminalLifecycleCount;
   445	            break;
   446	        case Lifecycle::Succeeded:
   447	            ++counts.succeededLifecycleCount;
   448	            break;
   449	        case Lifecycle::Failed:
   450	            ++counts.failedLifecycleCount;
   451	            break;
   452	        case Lifecycle::Cancelled:
   453	            ++counts.cancelledLifecycleCount;
   454	            break;
   455	        case Lifecycle::Unknown:
   456	            ++counts.unknownLifecycleCount;
   457	            break;
   458	    }
   459	    if (member.consistency == Consistency::Consistent)
   460	        ++counts.consistentMemberCount;
   461	    else
   462	        ++counts.inconsistentMemberCount;
   463	
   464	    switch (member.outcome)
   465	    {
   466	        case MemberOutcome::NotReady:
   467	            ++counts.notReadyMemberCount;
   468	            break;
   469	        case MemberOutcome::SucceededComparable:
   470	            ++counts.succeededComparableMemberCount;
   471	            break;
   472	        case MemberOutcome::SucceededContextChanged:
   473	            ++counts.succeededContextChangedMemberCount;
   474	            break;
   475	        case MemberOutcome::SucceededMetricGap:
   476	            ++counts.succeededMetricGapMemberCount;
   477	            break;
   478	        case MemberOutcome::TerminalFailed:
   479	            ++counts.terminalFailedMemberCount;
   480	            break;
   481	        case MemberOutcome::TerminalCancelled:
   482	            ++counts.terminalCancelledMemberCount;
   483	            break;
   484	        case MemberOutcome::Inconsistent:
   485	            ++counts.inconsistentOutcomeMemberCount;
   486	            break;
   487	    }
   488	
   489	    for (const auto& comparison : member.comparisons)
   490	    {
   491	        ++counts.metricComparisonCount;
   492	        switch (comparison.classification)
   493	        {
   494	            case Classification::Comparable:
   495	                ++counts.comparableMetricCount;
   496	                if (*comparison.delta > 0.0)
   497	                    ++counts.positiveDeltaCount;
   498	                else if (*comparison.delta < 0.0)
   499	                    ++counts.negativeDeltaCount;
   500	                else
   501	                    ++counts.zeroDeltaCount;
   502	                break;
   503	            case Classification::ContextChanged:
   504	                ++counts.contextChangedMetricCount;
   505	                break;
   506	            case Classification::MissingSourceMetric:
   507	                ++counts.missingSourceMetricCount;
   508	                break;
   509	            case Classification::MissingResultMetric:
   510	                ++counts.missingResultMetricCount;
   511	                break;
   512	            case Classification::MetricValueUnavailable:
   513	                ++counts.metricValueUnavailableCount;
   514	                break;
   515	            case Classification::Unsupported:
   516	                ++counts.unsupportedMetricCount;
   517	                break;
   518	        }
   519	    }
   520	}
   521	
   522	} // namespace

   537	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_identity_missing");
   538	    if (identityHash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(identityCanonical))

   540	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_identity_invalid");
   520	}

   734	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparison::
   735	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparison(
   736	        std::string metricIdentityValue,
   737	        Classification classificationValue,
   738	        std::optional<double> sourceValueValue,
   739	        std::optional<double> resultValueValue,
   740	        std::optional<double> deltaValue,
   741	        std::vector<Difference> contextDifferencesValue)
   742	    : metricIdentity(std::move(metricIdentityValue)),
   743	      classification(classificationValue),
   744	      sourceValue(sourceValueValue),
   745	      resultValue(resultValueValue),
   746	      delta(deltaValue && *deltaValue == 0.0
   747	              ? std::optional<double>(0.0)
   748	              : deltaValue),
   749	      contextDifferences(std::move(contextDifferencesValue))
   750	{
   751	    RequireText(metricIdentity,
   752	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_comparison_metric_identity_invalid");
   753	    if ((sourceValue && !std::isfinite(*sourceValue)) ||
   754	        (resultValue && !std::isfinite(*resultValue)) ||
   755	        (delta && !std::isfinite(*delta)))
   756	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   757	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_comparison_number_nonfinite");
   758	    const bool changed = classification == Classification::ContextChanged;
   759	    if (changed != !contextDifferences.empty())
   760	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   761	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_comparison_context_invalid");
   762	    if (classification == Classification::Comparable)
   763	    {
   764	        if (!sourceValue || !resultValue || !delta)
   765	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   766	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_comparable_invalid");
   767	    }
   768	    else if (delta)
   769	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   770	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_noncomparable_delta_invalid");
   771	}
   772	
   773	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary::
   774	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary(
   775	        AggregateOutcome outcomeValue,
   776	        int memberCountValue,
   777	        int notTerminalLifecycleCountValue,
   778	        int succeededLifecycleCountValue,
   779	        int failedLifecycleCountValue,
   780	        int cancelledLifecycleCountValue,
   781	        int unknownLifecycleCountValue,
   782	        int consistentMemberCountValue,
   783	        int inconsistentMemberCountValue,
   784	        int notReadyMemberCountValue,
   785	        int succeededComparableMemberCountValue,
   786	        int succeededContextChangedMemberCountValue,
   787	        int succeededMetricGapMemberCountValue,
   788	        int terminalFailedMemberCountValue,
   789	        int terminalCancelledMemberCountValue,
   790	        int inconsistentOutcomeMemberCountValue,
   791	        std::size_t metricComparisonCountValue,
   792	        std::size_t comparableMetricCountValue,
   793	        std::size_t contextChangedMetricCountValue,
   794	        std::size_t missingSourceMetricCountValue,
   795	        std::size_t missingResultMetricCountValue,
   796	        std::size_t metricValueUnavailableCountValue,
   797	        std::size_t unsupportedMetricCountValue,
   798	        std::size_t positiveDeltaCountValue,
   799	        std::size_t zeroDeltaCountValue,
   800	        std::size_t negativeDeltaCountValue)
   801	    : outcome(outcomeValue),
   802	      memberCount(memberCountValue),
   803	      notTerminalLifecycleCount(notTerminalLifecycleCountValue),
   804	      succeededLifecycleCount(succeededLifecycleCountValue),
   805	      failedLifecycleCount(failedLifecycleCountValue),
   806	      cancelledLifecycleCount(cancelledLifecycleCountValue),
   807	      unknownLifecycleCount(unknownLifecycleCountValue),
   808	      consistentMemberCount(consistentMemberCountValue),
   809	      inconsistentMemberCount(inconsistentMemberCountValue),
   810	      notReadyMemberCount(notReadyMemberCountValue),
   811	      succeededComparableMemberCount(succeededComparableMemberCountValue),
   812	      succeededContextChangedMemberCount(
   813	          succeededContextChangedMemberCountValue),
   814	      succeededMetricGapMemberCount(succeededMetricGapMemberCountValue),
   815	      terminalFailedMemberCount(terminalFailedMemberCountValue),
   816	      terminalCancelledMemberCount(terminalCancelledMemberCountValue),
   817	      inconsistentOutcomeMemberCount(inconsistentOutcomeMemberCountValue),
   818	      metricComparisonCount(metricComparisonCountValue),
   819	      comparableMetricCount(comparableMetricCountValue),
   820	      contextChangedMetricCount(contextChangedMetricCountValue),
   821	      missingSourceMetricCount(missingSourceMetricCountValue),
   822	      missingResultMetricCount(missingResultMetricCountValue),
   823	      metricValueUnavailableCount(metricValueUnavailableCountValue),
   824	      unsupportedMetricCount(unsupportedMetricCountValue),
   825	      positiveDeltaCount(positiveDeltaCountValue),
   826	      zeroDeltaCount(zeroDeltaCountValue),
   827	      negativeDeltaCount(negativeDeltaCountValue)
   828	{
   829	    const int lifecycleTotal = notTerminalLifecycleCount +
   830	        succeededLifecycleCount + failedLifecycleCount +
   831	        cancelledLifecycleCount + unknownLifecycleCount;
   832	    const int consistencyTotal =
   833	        consistentMemberCount + inconsistentMemberCount;
   834	    const int outcomeTotal = notReadyMemberCount +
   835	        succeededComparableMemberCount + succeededContextChangedMemberCount +
   836	        succeededMetricGapMemberCount + terminalFailedMemberCount +
   837	        terminalCancelledMemberCount + inconsistentOutcomeMemberCount;
   838	    const std::size_t comparisonTotal = comparableMetricCount +
   839	        contextChangedMetricCount + missingSourceMetricCount +
   840	        missingResultMetricCount + metricValueUnavailableCount +
   841	        unsupportedMetricCount;
   842	    const std::size_t deltaTotal =
   843	        positiveDeltaCount + zeroDeltaCount + negativeDeltaCount;
   844	    if (memberCount <= 0 || lifecycleTotal != memberCount ||
   845	        consistencyTotal != memberCount || outcomeTotal != memberCount ||
   846	        comparisonTotal != metricComparisonCount ||
   847	        deltaTotal != comparableMetricCount)
   848	        throw std::logic_error(
   849	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_summary_invariant_failed");
   850	}
   851	
   852	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember::
   853	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember(
   854	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity identityValue,
   855	        Lifecycle lifecycleValue,
   856	        Consistency consistencyValue,
   857	        MemberOutcome outcomeValue,
   858	        std::vector<Diagnostic> diagnosticsValue,
   859	        std::optional<
   860	            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence>
   861	            sourceEvidenceValue,
   862	        std::optional<
   863	            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence>
   864	            resultEvidenceValue,
   865	        std::vector<Comparison> comparisonsValue)
   866	    : identity(std::move(identityValue)),
   867	      lifecycle(lifecycleValue),
   868	      consistency(consistencyValue),
   869	      outcome(outcomeValue),
   870	      diagnostics(std::move(diagnosticsValue)),
   871	      sourceEvidence(std::move(sourceEvidenceValue)),
   872	      resultEvidence(std::move(resultEvidenceValue)),
   873	      comparisons(std::move(comparisonsValue))
   874	{
   875	    const bool inconsistent = consistency == Consistency::Inconsistent;
   876	    if (inconsistent != !diagnostics.empty() ||
   877	        inconsistent != (outcome == MemberOutcome::Inconsistent) ||
   878	        (inconsistent && !comparisons.empty()))
   879	        throw std::logic_error(
   880	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_invariant_failed");
   881	}

   883	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentIdentity::
   884	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentIdentity(
   885	        int contractVersionValue,
   886	        std::string canonicalTextValue,
   887	        std::string hashValue)
   888	    : contractVersion(contractVersionValue),
   889	      canonicalText(std::move(canonicalTextValue)),
   890	      hash(std::move(hashValue))
   891	{
   892	    if (contractVersion !=
   893	            kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion ||
   894	        hash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(canonicalText))
   895	        throw std::logic_error(
   896	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_identity_invariant_failed");
   897	    RequireText(canonicalText,
   898	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_identity_missing");
   899	}

   901	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment::
   902	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
   903	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentIdentity identityValue,
   904	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity
   905	            campaignIdentityValue,
   906	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity
   907	            materializationIdentityValue,
   908	        std::string observedAtValue,
   909	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary summaryValue,
   910	        std::vector<Member> membersValue)
   911	    : identity(std::move(identityValue)),
   912	      campaignIdentity(std::move(campaignIdentityValue)),
   913	      materializationIdentity(std::move(materializationIdentityValue)),
   914	      observedAt(std::move(observedAtValue)),
   915	      summary(std::move(summaryValue)),
   916	      members(std::move(membersValue))
   917	{
   918	    RequireText(observedAt,
   919	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_observed_at_invalid");
   920	    if (summary.memberCount != static_cast<int>(members.size()))
   921	        throw std::logic_error(
   922	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_summary_invariant_failed");
   923	}
   924	
   925	struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentBuilder
   926	{
   927	    static std::vector<Comparison> BuildComparisons(
   928	        const MemberEvidence& evidence,
   929	        bool& nonFiniteDelta)
   930	    {
   931	        const auto* sourceMetrics = evidence.sourceEvidence
   932	            ? &evidence.sourceEvidence->metrics.metrics
   933	            : nullptr;
   934	        const auto& resultMetrics = evidence.resultEvidence->metrics.metrics;
   935	        std::vector<Difference> differences;
   936	        if (evidence.sourceEvidence)
   937	            differences = ContextDifferences(
   938	                evidence.sourceEvidence->context,
   939	                *evidence.resultEvidence->context);
   940	
   941	        std::vector<Comparison> comparisons;
   942	        std::size_t sourceIndex = 0;
   943	        std::size_t resultIndex = 0;
   944	        while ((sourceMetrics && sourceIndex < sourceMetrics->size()) ||
   945	            resultIndex < resultMetrics.size())
   946	        {
   947	            const Metric* source = sourceMetrics &&
   948	                    sourceIndex < sourceMetrics->size()
   949	                ? &(*sourceMetrics)[sourceIndex]
   950	                : nullptr;
   925	struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentBuilder
   926	{
   927	    static std::vector<Comparison> BuildComparisons(
   928	        const MemberEvidence& evidence,
   929	        bool& nonFiniteDelta)
   930	    {
   931	        const auto* sourceMetrics = evidence.sourceEvidence
   932	            ? &evidence.sourceEvidence->metrics.metrics
   933	            : nullptr;
   934	        const auto& resultMetrics = evidence.resultEvidence->metrics.metrics;
   935	        std::vector<Difference> differences;
   936	        if (evidence.sourceEvidence)
   937	            differences = ContextDifferences(
   938	                evidence.sourceEvidence->context,
   939	                *evidence.resultEvidence->context);
   940	
   941	        std::vector<Comparison> comparisons;
   942	        std::size_t sourceIndex = 0;
   943	        std::size_t resultIndex = 0;
   944	        while ((sourceMetrics && sourceIndex < sourceMetrics->size()) ||
   945	            resultIndex < resultMetrics.size())
   946	        {
   947	            const Metric* source = sourceMetrics &&
   948	                    sourceIndex < sourceMetrics->size()
   949	                ? &(*sourceMetrics)[sourceIndex]
   950	                : nullptr;
   951	            const Metric* result = resultIndex < resultMetrics.size()
   952	                ? &resultMetrics[resultIndex]
   953	                : nullptr;
   954	            if (source && result && source->identity == result->identity)
   955	            {
   956	                if (!differences.empty())
   957	                    comparisons.push_back(Comparison(source->identity,
   958	                        Classification::ContextChanged, source->value,
   959	                        result->value, std::nullopt, differences));
   960	                else if (!source->value || !result->value)
   961	                    comparisons.push_back(Comparison(source->identity,
   962	                        Classification::MetricValueUnavailable, source->value,
   963	                        result->value, std::nullopt, {}));
   964	                else if (source->comparisonSupport != MetricSupport::NumericDelta ||
   965	                    result->comparisonSupport != MetricSupport::NumericDelta)
   966	                    comparisons.push_back(Comparison(source->identity,
   967	                        Classification::Unsupported, source->value,
   968	                        result->value, std::nullopt, {}));
   969	                else
   970	                {
   971	                    double delta = *result->value - *source->value;
   972	                    if (!std::isfinite(delta))
   973	                    {
   974	                        nonFiniteDelta = true;
   975	                        return {};
   976	                    }
   977	                    if (delta == 0.0) delta = 0.0;
   978	                    comparisons.push_back(Comparison(source->identity,
   979	                        Classification::Comparable, source->value,
   980	                        result->value, delta, {}));
   981	                }
   982	                ++sourceIndex;
   983	                ++resultIndex;
   984	            }
   985	            else if (source &&
   986	                (!result || source->identity < result->identity))
   987	            {
   988	                comparisons.push_back(Comparison(source->identity,
   989	                    Classification::MissingResultMetric, source->value,
   990	                    std::nullopt, std::nullopt, {}));
   991	                ++sourceIndex;
   992	            }
   993	            else
   994	            {
   995	                comparisons.push_back(Comparison(result->identity,
   996	                    Classification::MissingSourceMetric, std::nullopt,
   997	                    result->value, std::nullopt, {}));
   998	                ++resultIndex;
   999	            }
  1000	        }
  1001	        return comparisons;
  1002	    }
  1003	
  1004	    static MemberOutcome DeriveMemberOutcome(
  1005	        Lifecycle lifecycle,
  1006	        const std::vector<Diagnostic>& diagnostics,
  1007	        const std::vector<Comparison>& comparisons)
  1008	    {
  1009	        if (!diagnostics.empty()) return MemberOutcome::Inconsistent;
  1010	        switch (lifecycle)
  1011	        {
  1012	            case Lifecycle::NotTerminal:
  1013	            case Lifecycle::Unknown:
  1014	                return MemberOutcome::NotReady;
  1015	            case Lifecycle::Failed:
  1016	                return MemberOutcome::TerminalFailed;
  1017	            case Lifecycle::Cancelled:
  1018	                return MemberOutcome::TerminalCancelled;
  1019	            case Lifecycle::Succeeded:
  1020	                break;
  1021	        }
  1022	        if (std::any_of(comparisons.begin(), comparisons.end(),
  1023	                [](const Comparison& comparison)
  1024	        {
  1025	            return comparison.classification == Classification::ContextChanged;
  1026	        }))
  1027	            return MemberOutcome::SucceededContextChanged;
  1028	        if (comparisons.empty() ||
  1029	            std::any_of(comparisons.begin(), comparisons.end(),
  1030	                [](const Comparison& comparison)
  1031	        {
  1032	            return comparison.classification != Classification::Comparable;
  1033	        }))
  1034	            return MemberOutcome::SucceededMetricGap;
  1035	        return MemberOutcome::SucceededComparable;
  1036	    }
  1037	
  1038	    static RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment Build(
  1039	        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity&
  1040	            campaignIdentity,
  1041	        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity&
  1042	            materializationIdentity,
  1043	        const std::string& observedAt,
  1044	        const std::vector<MemberEvidence>& members)
  1045	    {
  1046	        RequireText(observedAt,
  1047	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_observed_at_invalid");
  1048	        if (materializationIdentity.campaignApprovalId !=
  1049	                campaignIdentity.campaignApprovalId ||
  1050	            materializationIdentity.campaignIdentityHash !=
  1051	                campaignIdentity.identityHash)
  1052	            throw std::invalid_argument(
  1053	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_materialization_mismatch");
  1054	        if (materializationIdentity.contractVersion !=
  1055	            kRecommendationCampaignMaterializationContractVersion)
  1056	            throw std::invalid_argument(
  1057	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_version_unsupported");
  1058	        if (materializationIdentity.memberCount !=
  1059	            static_cast<int>(members.size()))
  1060	            throw std::invalid_argument(
  1061	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_count_mismatch");
  1062	        if (members.empty() ||
  1063	            members.size() > static_cast<std::size_t>(
  1064	                                 kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers))
  1065	            throw std::invalid_argument(
  1066	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_count_invalid");
  1067	
  1068	        std::vector<const MemberEvidence*> ordered;
  1069	        ordered.reserve(members.size());
  1070	        for (const auto& member : members) ordered.push_back(&member);
  1071	        std::sort(ordered.begin(), ordered.end(),
  1072	            [](const MemberEvidence* left, const MemberEvidence* right)
  1073	        {
  1074	            return std::tie(left->identity.memberOrdinal,
  1075	                       left->identity.materializationMemberId,
  1076	                       left->identity.rankingMemberId,
  1077	                       left->identity.recommendationId,
  1078	                       left->identity.proposalId) <
  1079	                std::tie(right->identity.memberOrdinal,
  1080	                       right->identity.materializationMemberId,
  1081	                       right->identity.rankingMemberId,
  1082	                       right->identity.recommendationId,
  1083	                       right->identity.proposalId);
  1084	        });
  1085	
  1086	        std::set<long long> materializationMemberIds;
  1087	        std::set<long long> rankingMemberIds;
  1088	        std::set<long long> recommendationIds;
  1089	        std::set<long long> proposalIds;
  1090	        std::vector<WorkingMember> working;
  1091	        working.reserve(ordered.size());
  1092	        for (std::size_t index = 0; index < ordered.size(); ++index)
  1093	        {
  1094	            const auto& evidence = *ordered[index];
  1095	            if (evidence.identity.memberOrdinal != static_cast<int>(index) + 1 ||
  1096	                !materializationMemberIds
  1097	                     .insert(evidence.identity.materializationMemberId)
  1098	                     .second ||
  1099	                !rankingMemberIds.insert(evidence.identity.rankingMemberId)
  1100	                     .second ||
  1101	                !recommendationIds.insert(evidence.identity.recommendationId)
  1102	                     .second ||
  1103	                !proposalIds.insert(evidence.identity.proposalId).second)
  1104	                throw std::invalid_argument(
  1105	                    "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_identity_set_invalid");
  1106	
  1107	            WorkingMember item{&evidence, {}};
  1108	            if (evidence.inputConsistency == Consistency::Inconsistent)
  1109	                item.diagnostics.push_back(
  1110	                    Diagnostic::InputEvidenceInconsistent);
  1111	            if (evidence.sourceEvidence &&
  1112	                evidence.sourceEvidence->sourceExperimentId !=
  1113	                    evidence.identity.sourceExperimentId)
  1114	                item.diagnostics.push_back(
  1115	                    Diagnostic::SourceExperimentIdentityMismatch);
  1116	            if ((evidence.lifecycle == Lifecycle::Succeeded ||
  1117	                    evidence.lifecycle == Lifecycle::Failed ||
  1118	                    evidence.lifecycle == Lifecycle::Cancelled) &&
  1119	                !evidence.identity.expectedExperimentId)
  1120	                item.diagnostics.push_back(
  1121	                    Diagnostic::ExpectedExperimentIdentityMissing);
  1122	            if (evidence.lifecycle != Lifecycle::Succeeded &&
  1123	                evidence.resultEvidence)
  1124	                item.diagnostics.push_back(
  1125	                    Diagnostic::ResultEvidenceUnexpectedForLifecycle);
  1126	            if (evidence.lifecycle == Lifecycle::Succeeded)
  1127	            {
  1128	                if (!evidence.resultEvidence)
  1129	                    item.diagnostics.push_back(Diagnostic::ResultEvidenceMissing);
  1130	                else
  1131	                {
  1132	                    if (!evidence.resultEvidence->experimentId)
  1133	                        item.diagnostics.push_back(
  1134	                            Diagnostic::ResultExperimentIdentityMissing);
  1135	                    else if (evidence.identity.expectedExperimentId &&
  1136	                        evidence.resultEvidence->experimentId !=
  1137	                            evidence.identity.expectedExperimentId)
  1138	                        item.diagnostics.push_back(
  1139	                            Diagnostic::ResultExperimentIdentityMismatch);
  1140	                    if (!evidence.resultEvidence->modelId)
  1141	                        item.diagnostics.push_back(
  1142	                            Diagnostic::ResultModelIdentityMissing);
  1143	                    if (evidence.resultEvidence->resultIdentities.empty())
  1144	                        item.diagnostics.push_back(
  1145	                            Diagnostic::ResultIdentityMissing);
  1146	                    if (!evidence.resultEvidence->context)
  1147	                        item.diagnostics.push_back(
  1148	                            Diagnostic::ResultContextMissing);
  1149	                }
  1150	            }
  1151	            working.push_back(std::move(item));
  1152	        }
  1153	
  1154	        std::map<long long, std::vector<std::size_t>> expectedExperiments;
  1155	        std::map<long long, std::vector<std::size_t>> resultExperiments;
  1156	        std::map<long long, std::vector<std::size_t>> resultModels;
  1157	        std::map<std::pair<std::string, long long>, std::vector<std::size_t>>
  1158	            resultIdentities;
  1159	        for (std::size_t index = 0; index < working.size(); ++index)
  1160	        {
  1161	            const auto& expected =
  1162	                working[index].evidence->identity.expectedExperimentId;
  1163	            if (expected) expectedExperiments[*expected].push_back(index);
  1164	            const auto& result = working[index].evidence->resultEvidence;
  1165	            if (!result) continue;
  1166	            if (result->experimentId)
  1167	                resultExperiments[*result->experimentId].push_back(index);
  1168	            if (result->modelId)
  1169	                resultModels[*result->modelId].push_back(index);
  1170	            for (const auto& identity : result->resultIdentities)
  1171	                resultIdentities[{identity.kind, identity.id}].push_back(index);
  1172	        }
  1173	        for (const auto& [unused, indices] : expectedExperiments)
  1174	        {
  1175	            static_cast<void>(unused);
  1176	            if (indices.size() < 2) continue;
  1177	            for (const auto index : indices)
  1178	                working[index].diagnostics.push_back(
  1179	                    Diagnostic::ExpectedExperimentIdentityReused);
  1180	        }
  1181	        for (const auto& [unused, indices] : resultExperiments)
  1182	        {
  1183	            static_cast<void>(unused);
  1184	            if (indices.size() < 2) continue;
  1185	            for (const auto index : indices)
  1186	                working[index].diagnostics.push_back(
  1187	                    Diagnostic::ResultExperimentReused);
  1188	        }
  1189	        for (const auto& [unused, indices] : resultModels)
  1190	        {
  1191	            static_cast<void>(unused);
  1192	            if (indices.size() < 2) continue;
  1193	            for (const auto index : indices)
  1194	                working[index].diagnostics.push_back(
  1195	                    Diagnostic::ResultModelIdentityReused);
  1196	        }
  1197	        for (const auto& [unused, indices] : resultIdentities)
  1198	        {
  1199	            static_cast<void>(unused);
  1200	            if (indices.size() < 2) continue;
  1201	            for (const auto index : indices)
  1202	                working[index].diagnostics.push_back(
  1203	                    Diagnostic::ResultIdentityReused);
  1204	        }
  1205	
  1206	        std::vector<Member> assessedMembers;
  1207	        assessedMembers.reserve(working.size());
  1208	        for (auto& item : working)
  1209	        {
  1210	            CanonicalizeDiagnostics(item.diagnostics);
  1211	            std::vector<Comparison> comparisons;
  1212	            if (item.diagnostics.empty() &&
  1213	                item.evidence->lifecycle == Lifecycle::Succeeded)
  1214	            {
  1215	                bool nonFiniteDelta = false;
  1216	                comparisons = BuildComparisons(*item.evidence, nonFiniteDelta);
  1217	                if (nonFiniteDelta)
  1218	                {
  1219	                    item.diagnostics.push_back(
  1220	                        Diagnostic::NumericDeltaNonFinite);
  1221	                    comparisons.clear();
  1222	                }
  1223	            }
  1224	            CanonicalizeDiagnostics(item.diagnostics);
  1225	            const Consistency consistency = item.diagnostics.empty()
  1226	                ? Consistency::Consistent
  1227	                : Consistency::Inconsistent;
  1228	            const MemberOutcome outcome = DeriveMemberOutcome(
  1229	                item.evidence->lifecycle, item.diagnostics, comparisons);
  1230	            assessedMembers.push_back(Member(item.evidence->identity,
  1231	                item.evidence->lifecycle, consistency, outcome,
  1232	                item.diagnostics, item.evidence->sourceEvidence,
  1233	                item.evidence->resultEvidence, std::move(comparisons)));
  1234	        }
  1235	
  1236	        MutableSummary counts;
  1237	        for (const auto& member : assessedMembers) CountMember(counts, member);
  1238	        const AggregateOutcome aggregateOutcome =
  1239	            DeriveAggregateOutcome(counts);
  1240	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary summary(
  1241	            aggregateOutcome, counts.memberCount,
  1242	            counts.notTerminalLifecycleCount, counts.succeededLifecycleCount,
  1243	            counts.failedLifecycleCount, counts.cancelledLifecycleCount,
  1244	            counts.unknownLifecycleCount, counts.consistentMemberCount,
  1245	            counts.inconsistentMemberCount, counts.notReadyMemberCount,
  1246	            counts.succeededComparableMemberCount,
  1247	            counts.succeededContextChangedMemberCount,
  1248	            counts.succeededMetricGapMemberCount,
  1249	            counts.terminalFailedMemberCount,
  1250	            counts.terminalCancelledMemberCount,
  1251	            counts.inconsistentOutcomeMemberCount,
  1252	            counts.metricComparisonCount, counts.comparableMetricCount,
  1253	            counts.contextChangedMetricCount,
  1254	            counts.missingSourceMetricCount,
  1255	            counts.missingResultMetricCount,
  1256	            counts.metricValueUnavailableCount,
  1257	            counts.unsupportedMetricCount, counts.positiveDeltaCount,
  1258	            counts.zeroDeltaCount, counts.negativeDeltaCount);
  1259	        std::string canonical = AssessmentCanonicalText(campaignIdentity,
  1260	            materializationIdentity, summary, assessedMembers);
  1261	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentIdentity identity(
  1262	            kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion,
  1263	            canonical, RecommendationCanonicalHash(canonical));
  1264	        return RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(std::move(identity),
  1265	            campaignIdentity, materializationIdentity, observedAt,
  1266	            std::move(summary), std::move(assessedMembers));
  1267	    }
  1268	};
  1269	
  1270	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
  1271	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity&
  1272	        campaignIdentity,
  1273	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity&
  1274	        materializationIdentity,
  1275	    const std::string& observedAt,
  1276	    const std::vector<MemberEvidence>& members)
  1277	{
  1278	    return RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentBuilder::Build(
  1279	        campaignIdentity, materializationIdentity, observedAt, members);
  1280	}

  1291	    }

   524	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity::
   525	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity(
   526	        long long campaignApprovalIdValue,
   527	        std::string identityCanonicalValue,
   528	        std::string identityHashValue)
   529	    : campaignApprovalId(campaignApprovalIdValue),
   530	      identityCanonical(std::move(identityCanonicalValue)),
   531	      identityHash(std::move(identityHashValue))
   532	{
   533	    if (campaignApprovalId <= 0)
   534	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   535	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_id_invalid");
   536	    RequireText(identityCanonical,
   537	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_identity_missing");
   538	    if (identityHash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(identityCanonical))
   539	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   540	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_identity_invalid");
   541	}

   543	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity::
   544	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity(
   545	        long long materializationIdValue,
   546	        long long campaignApprovalIdValue,
   547	        std::string campaignIdentityHashValue,
   548	        int contractVersionValue,
   549	        int memberCountValue,
   550	        std::string identityCanonicalValue,
   551	        std::string identityHashValue)
   552	    : materializationId(materializationIdValue),
   553	      campaignApprovalId(campaignApprovalIdValue),
   554	      campaignIdentityHash(std::move(campaignIdentityHashValue)),
   555	      contractVersion(contractVersionValue),
   556	      memberCount(memberCountValue),
   557	      identityCanonical(std::move(identityCanonicalValue)),
   558	      identityHash(std::move(identityHashValue))
   559	{
   560	    if (materializationId <= 0 || campaignApprovalId <= 0)
   561	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   562	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_id_invalid");
   563	    if (memberCount <= 0 ||
   564	        memberCount > kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers)
   565	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   566	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_member_count_invalid");
   567	    RequireText(campaignIdentityHash,
   568	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_identity_hash_missing");
   569	    RequireText(identityCanonical,
   570	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_identity_missing");
   571	    if (identityHash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(identityCanonical))
   572	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   573	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_identity_invalid");
   574	}

   576	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity::
   577	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity(
   578	        int memberOrdinalValue,
   579	        long long materializationMemberIdValue,
   580	        long long rankingMemberIdValue,
   581	        long long recommendationIdValue,
   582	        long long sourceExperimentIdValue,
   583	        long long proposalIdValue,

   585	    : memberOrdinal(memberOrdinalValue),

   587	      rankingMemberId(rankingMemberIdValue),

   608	      value(valueValue && *valueValue == 0.0

   628	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentComparisonContext::

   636	        std::string inferenceRangeEndValue)
   637	    : symbol(std::move(symbolValue)),
   638	      predictionHorizon(predictionHorizonValue),

   642	      inferenceRangeStart(std::move(inferenceRangeStartValue)),

   713	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberEvidence::
   714	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberEvidence(
   715	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity identityValue,
   716	        Lifecycle lifecycleValue,
   717	        std::optional<
   718	            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence>
   719	            sourceEvidenceValue,
   720	        std::optional<
   721	            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence>
   722	            resultEvidenceValue,
   723	        Consistency inputConsistencyValue)
   724	    : identity(std::move(identityValue)),
   725	      lifecycle(lifecycleValue),
   726	      inputConsistency(inputConsistencyValue),
   727	      sourceEvidence(std::move(sourceEvidenceValue)),
   728	      resultEvidence(std::move(resultEvidenceValue))
   729	{
   730	    RequireValidLifecycle(lifecycle);
   731	    RequireValidConsistency(inputConsistency);
   732	}
   733	
   734	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparison::
   735	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparison(
   736	        std::string metricIdentityValue,
   737	        Classification classificationValue,
   738	        std::optional<double> sourceValueValue,
   739	        std::optional<double> resultValueValue,
   740	        std::optional<double> deltaValue,
   741	        std::vector<Difference> contextDifferencesValue)
   742	    : metricIdentity(std::move(metricIdentityValue)),
   743	      classification(classificationValue),
   744	      sourceValue(sourceValueValue),
   745	      resultValue(resultValueValue),
   746	      delta(deltaValue && *deltaValue == 0.0
   747	              ? std::optional<double>(0.0)
   748	              : deltaValue),
   749	      contextDifferences(std::move(contextDifferencesValue))
   750	{
   751	    RequireText(metricIdentity,
   752	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_comparison_metric_identity_invalid");
   753	    if ((sourceValue && !std::isfinite(*sourceValue)) ||
   754	        (resultValue && !std::isfinite(*resultValue)) ||
   755	        (delta && !std::isfinite(*delta)))
   756	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   757	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_comparison_number_nonfinite");
   758	    const bool changed = classification == Classification::ContextChanged;
   759	    if (changed != !contextDifferences.empty())
   760	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   761	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_comparison_context_invalid");
   762	    if (classification == Classification::Comparable)
   763	    {
   764	        if (!sourceValue || !resultValue || !delta)
   765	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   766	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_comparable_invalid");
   767	    }
   768	    else if (delta)
   769	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   770	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_noncomparable_delta_invalid");
   771	}

   773	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary::
   774	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSummary(
   775	        AggregateOutcome outcomeValue,
   776	        int memberCountValue,
   777	        int notTerminalLifecycleCountValue,
   778	        int succeededLifecycleCountValue,
   779	        int failedLifecycleCountValue,
   780	        int cancelledLifecycleCountValue,
   781	        int unknownLifecycleCountValue,
   782	        int consistentMemberCountValue,
   783	        int inconsistentMemberCountValue,
   784	        int notReadyMemberCountValue,
   785	        int succeededComparableMemberCountValue,
   786	        int succeededContextChangedMemberCountValue,
   787	        int succeededMetricGapMemberCountValue,
   788	        int terminalFailedMemberCountValue,
   789	        int terminalCancelledMemberCountValue,
   790	        int inconsistentOutcomeMemberCountValue,
   791	        std::size_t metricComparisonCountValue,
   792	        std::size_t comparableMetricCountValue,
   793	        std::size_t contextChangedMetricCountValue,
   794	        std::size_t missingSourceMetricCountValue,
   795	        std::size_t missingResultMetricCountValue,
   796	        std::size_t metricValueUnavailableCountValue,
   797	        std::size_t unsupportedMetricCountValue,
   798	        std::size_t positiveDeltaCountValue,
   799	        std::size_t zeroDeltaCountValue,
   800	        std::size_t negativeDeltaCountValue)
   801	    : outcome(outcomeValue),
   802	      memberCount(memberCountValue),
   803	      notTerminalLifecycleCount(notTerminalLifecycleCountValue),
   804	      succeededLifecycleCount(succeededLifecycleCountValue),
   805	      failedLifecycleCount(failedLifecycleCountValue),
   806	      cancelledLifecycleCount(cancelledLifecycleCountValue),
   807	      unknownLifecycleCount(unknownLifecycleCountValue),
   808	      consistentMemberCount(consistentMemberCountValue),
   809	      inconsistentMemberCount(inconsistentMemberCountValue),
   810	      notReadyMemberCount(notReadyMemberCountValue),
   811	      succeededComparableMemberCount(succeededComparableMemberCountValue),
   812	      succeededContextChangedMemberCount(
   813	          succeededContextChangedMemberCountValue),
   814	      succeededMetricGapMemberCount(succeededMetricGapMemberCountValue),
   815	      terminalFailedMemberCount(terminalFailedMemberCountValue),
   816	      terminalCancelledMemberCount(terminalCancelledMemberCountValue),
   817	      inconsistentOutcomeMemberCount(inconsistentOutcomeMemberCountValue),
   818	      metricComparisonCount(metricComparisonCountValue),
   819	      comparableMetricCount(comparableMetricCountValue),
   820	      contextChangedMetricCount(contextChangedMetricCountValue),
   821	      missingSourceMetricCount(missingSourceMetricCountValue),
   822	      missingResultMetricCount(missingResultMetricCountValue),
   823	      metricValueUnavailableCount(metricValueUnavailableCountValue),
   824	      unsupportedMetricCount(unsupportedMetricCountValue),
   825	      positiveDeltaCount(positiveDeltaCountValue),
   826	      zeroDeltaCount(zeroDeltaCountValue),
   827	      negativeDeltaCount(negativeDeltaCountValue)
   828	{
   829	    const int lifecycleTotal = notTerminalLifecycleCount +
   830	        succeededLifecycleCount + failedLifecycleCount +
   831	        cancelledLifecycleCount + unknownLifecycleCount;
   832	    const int consistencyTotal =
   833	        consistentMemberCount + inconsistentMemberCount;
   834	    const int outcomeTotal = notReadyMemberCount +
   835	        succeededComparableMemberCount + succeededContextChangedMemberCount +
   836	        succeededMetricGapMemberCount + terminalFailedMemberCount +
   837	        terminalCancelledMemberCount + inconsistentOutcomeMemberCount;
   838	    const std::size_t comparisonTotal = comparableMetricCount +
   839	        contextChangedMetricCount + missingSourceMetricCount +
   840	        missingResultMetricCount + metricValueUnavailableCount +
   841	        unsupportedMetricCount;
   842	    const std::size_t deltaTotal =
   843	        positiveDeltaCount + zeroDeltaCount + negativeDeltaCount;
   844	    if (memberCount <= 0 || lifecycleTotal != memberCount ||
   845	        consistencyTotal != memberCount || outcomeTotal != memberCount ||
   846	        comparisonTotal != metricComparisonCount ||
   847	        deltaTotal != comparableMetricCount)
   848	        throw std::logic_error(
   849	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_summary_invariant_failed");
   850	}
