   852	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember::
   853	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember(
   854	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity identityValue,
   855	        Lifecycle lifecycleValue,
   856	        Consistency consistencyValue,
   857	        MemberOutcome outcomeValue,
   858	        std::vector<Diagnostic> diagnosticsValue,
   859	        std::optional<
   860	            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSourceEvidence>
   861	            sourceEvidenceValue,
   862	        std::optional<
   863	            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultEvidence>
   864	            resultEvidenceValue,
   865	        std::vector<Comparison> comparisonsValue)
   866	    : identity(std::move(identityValue)),
   867	      lifecycle(lifecycleValue),
   868	      consistency(consistencyValue),
   869	      outcome(outcomeValue),
   870	      diagnostics(std::move(diagnosticsValue)),
   871	      sourceEvidence(std::move(sourceEvidenceValue)),
   872	      resultEvidence(std::move(resultEvidenceValue)),
   873	      comparisons(std::move(comparisonsValue))
   874	{
   875	    const bool inconsistent = consistency == Consistency::Inconsistent;
   876	    if (inconsistent != !diagnostics.empty() ||
   877	        inconsistent != (outcome == MemberOutcome::Inconsistent) ||
   878	        (inconsistent && !comparisons.empty()))
   879	        throw std::logic_error(
   880	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_invariant_failed");
   881	}

   883	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentIdentity::
   884	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentIdentity(
   885	        int contractVersionValue,
   886	        std::string canonicalTextValue,
   887	        std::string hashValue)
   888	    : contractVersion(contractVersionValue),
   889	      canonicalText(std::move(canonicalTextValue)),
   890	      hash(std::move(hashValue))
   891	{
   892	    if (contractVersion !=
   893	            kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion ||
   894	        hash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(canonicalText))
   895	        throw std::logic_error(
   896	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_identity_invariant_failed");
   897	    RequireText(canonicalText,
   898	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_identity_missing");
   899	}

   904	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity

   914	      observedAt(std::move(observedAtValue)),
   915	      summary(std::move(summaryValue)),
   916	      members(std::move(membersValue))
   917	{
   918	    RequireText(observedAt,
   919	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_observed_at_invalid");
   920	    if (summary.memberCount != static_cast<int>(members.size()))
   921	        throw std::logic_error(
   922	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_summary_invariant_failed");
   923	}
   924	
   925	struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentBuilder
   926	{
   927	    static std::vector<Comparison> BuildComparisons(
   928	        const MemberEvidence& evidence,
   929	        bool& nonFiniteDelta)
   930	    {
   931	        const auto* sourceMetrics = evidence.sourceEvidence
   932	            ? &evidence.sourceEvidence->metrics.metrics
   933	            : nullptr;
   934	        const auto& resultMetrics = evidence.resultEvidence->metrics.metrics;
   935	        std::vector<Difference> differences;
   936	        if (evidence.sourceEvidence)
   937	            differences = ContextDifferences(
   938	                evidence.sourceEvidence->context,
   939	                *evidence.resultEvidence->context);
   940	
   925	struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentBuilder
   926	{
   927	    static std::vector<Comparison> BuildComparisons(
   928	        const MemberEvidence& evidence,
   929	        bool& nonFiniteDelta)
   930	    {
   931	        const auto* sourceMetrics = evidence.sourceEvidence
   932	            ? &evidence.sourceEvidence->metrics.metrics
   933	            : nullptr;
   934	        const auto& resultMetrics = evidence.resultEvidence->metrics.metrics;
   935	        std::vector<Difference> differences;
   936	        if (evidence.sourceEvidence)
   937	            differences = ContextDifferences(
   938	                evidence.sourceEvidence->context,
   939	                *evidence.resultEvidence->context);
   940	
   941	        std::vector<Comparison> comparisons;
   942	        std::size_t sourceIndex = 0;
   943	        std::size_t resultIndex = 0;
   944	        while ((sourceMetrics && sourceIndex < sourceMetrics->size()) ||
   945	            resultIndex < resultMetrics.size())
   946	        {
   947	            const Metric* source = sourceMetrics &&
   948	                    sourceIndex < sourceMetrics->size()
   949	                ? &(*sourceMetrics)[sourceIndex]
   950	                : nullptr;
   951	            const Metric* result = resultIndex < resultMetrics.size()
   952	                ? &resultMetrics[resultIndex]
   953	                : nullptr;
   954	            if (source && result && source->identity == result->identity)
   955	            {
   956	                if (!differences.empty())
   957	                    comparisons.push_back(Comparison(source->identity,
   958	                        Classification::ContextChanged, source->value,
   959	                        result->value, std::nullopt, differences));
   960	                else if (!source->value || !result->value)
   961	                    comparisons.push_back(Comparison(source->identity,
   962	                        Classification::MetricValueUnavailable, source->value,
   963	                        result->value, std::nullopt, {}));
   964	                else if (source->comparisonSupport != MetricSupport::NumericDelta ||
   965	                    result->comparisonSupport != MetricSupport::NumericDelta)
   966	                    comparisons.push_back(Comparison(source->identity,
   967	                        Classification::Unsupported, source->value,
   968	                        result->value, std::nullopt, {}));
   969	                else
   970	                {
   971	                    double delta = *result->value - *source->value;
   972	                    if (!std::isfinite(delta))
   973	                    {
   974	                        nonFiniteDelta = true;
   975	                        return {};
   976	                    }
   977	                    if (delta == 0.0) delta = 0.0;
   978	                    comparisons.push_back(Comparison(source->identity,
   979	                        Classification::Comparable, source->value,
   980	                        result->value, delta, {}));
   981	                }
   982	                ++sourceIndex;
   983	                ++resultIndex;
   984	            }
   985	            else if (source &&
   986	                (!result || source->identity < result->identity))
   987	            {
   988	                comparisons.push_back(Comparison(source->identity,
   989	                    Classification::MissingResultMetric, source->value,
   990	                    std::nullopt, std::nullopt, {}));
   991	                ++sourceIndex;
   992	            }
   993	            else
   994	            {
   995	                comparisons.push_back(Comparison(result->identity,
   996	                    Classification::MissingSourceMetric, std::nullopt,
   997	                    result->value, std::nullopt, {}));
   998	                ++resultIndex;
   999	            }
  1000	        }
  1001	        return comparisons;
  1002	    }
  1003	
  1004	    static MemberOutcome DeriveMemberOutcome(
  1005	        Lifecycle lifecycle,
  1006	        const std::vector<Diagnostic>& diagnostics,
  1007	        const std::vector<Comparison>& comparisons)
  1008	    {
  1009	        if (!diagnostics.empty()) return MemberOutcome::Inconsistent;
  1010	        switch (lifecycle)
  1011	        {
  1012	            case Lifecycle::NotTerminal:
  1013	            case Lifecycle::Unknown:
  1014	                return MemberOutcome::NotReady;
  1015	            case Lifecycle::Failed:
  1016	                return MemberOutcome::TerminalFailed;
  1017	            case Lifecycle::Cancelled:
  1018	                return MemberOutcome::TerminalCancelled;
  1019	            case Lifecycle::Succeeded:
  1020	                break;
  1021	        }
  1022	        if (std::any_of(comparisons.begin(), comparisons.end(),
  1023	                [](const Comparison& comparison)
  1024	        {
  1025	            return comparison.classification == Classification::ContextChanged;
  1026	        }))
  1027	            return MemberOutcome::SucceededContextChanged;
  1028	        if (comparisons.empty() ||
  1029	            std::any_of(comparisons.begin(), comparisons.end(),
  1030	                [](const Comparison& comparison)
  1031	        {
  1032	            return comparison.classification != Classification::Comparable;
  1033	        }))
  1034	            return MemberOutcome::SucceededMetricGap;
  1035	        return MemberOutcome::SucceededComparable;
  1036	    }
  1037	
  1038	    static RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment Build(
  1039	        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity&
  1040	            campaignIdentity,
  1041	        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity&
  1042	            materializationIdentity,
  1043	        const std::string& observedAt,
  1044	        const std::vector<MemberEvidence>& members)
  1045	    {
  1046	        RequireText(observedAt,
  1047	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_observed_at_invalid");
  1048	        if (materializationIdentity.campaignApprovalId !=
  1049	                campaignIdentity.campaignApprovalId ||
  1050	            materializationIdentity.campaignIdentityHash !=
  1051	                campaignIdentity.identityHash)
  1052	            throw std::invalid_argument(
  1053	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_materialization_mismatch");
  1054	        if (materializationIdentity.contractVersion !=
  1055	            kRecommendationCampaignMaterializationContractVersion)
  1056	            throw std::invalid_argument(
  1057	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_version_unsupported");
  1058	        if (materializationIdentity.memberCount !=
  1059	            static_cast<int>(members.size()))
  1060	            throw std::invalid_argument(
  1061	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_count_mismatch");
  1062	        if (members.empty() ||
  1063	            members.size() > static_cast<std::size_t>(
  1064	                                 kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers))
  1065	            throw std::invalid_argument(
  1066	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_assessment_member_count_invalid");
  1067	
  1068	        std::vector<const MemberEvidence*> ordered;

    46	void RequireText(const std::string& value, const char* error)
    47	{
    48	    if (value.empty() || value.find('\0') != std::string::npos)
    49	        throw std::invalid_argument(error);

    52	void RequireValidDirection(MetricDirection value)
    53	{
    54	    switch (value)
    55	    {
    56	        case MetricDirection::HigherIsFavorable:
    57	        case MetricDirection::LowerIsFavorable:
    58	            return;
    59	    }
    60	    throw std::invalid_argument(
    61	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_direction_invalid");
    62	}
    63	
    64	void RequireValidMetricJudgment(MetricJudgment value)
    65	{
    66	    switch (value)
    67	    {
    68	        case MetricJudgment::NotEvaluable:
    69	        case MetricJudgment::Favorable:
    70	        case MetricJudgment::Neutral:
    71	        case MetricJudgment::Unfavorable:
    72	            return;
    73	    }
    74	    throw std::invalid_argument(
    75	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_judgment_invalid");
    76	}
    77	
    78	void RequireValidMemberInterpretation(MemberInterpretation value)
    79	{
    80	    switch (value)

    82	        case MemberInterpretation::Inconclusive:
    83	        case MemberInterpretation::Favorable:
    84	        case MemberInterpretation::Neutral:
    85	        case MemberInterpretation::Unfavorable:
    86	        case MemberInterpretation::Mixed:
    87	            return;
    88	    }
    89	    throw std::invalid_argument(
    90	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_member_interpretation_invalid");
    91	}

   117	void AddReason(std::vector<PolicyReason>& reasons, PolicyReason reason)
   118	{
   119	    if (std::find(reasons.begin(), reasons.end(), reason) == reasons.end())
   120	        reasons.push_back(reason);
   121	}
   122	
   123	void CanonicalizeReasons(std::vector<PolicyReason>& reasons)
   124	{
   125	    std::sort(reasons.begin(), reasons.end(),
   126	        [](PolicyReason left, PolicyReason right)
   127	    {
   128	        return static_cast<int>(left) < static_cast<int>(right);
   129	    });
   130	    reasons.erase(std::unique(reasons.begin(), reasons.end()), reasons.end());
   131	}
   132	
   133	std::vector<MetricRule> CanonicalRules(
   134	    const std::vector<RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricRuleInput>&
   135	        inputs)
   136	{
   137	    if (inputs.empty())
   138	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   139	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_rules_missing");
   140	    std::vector<const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricRuleInput*>
   141	        ordered;
   142	    ordered.reserve(inputs.size());
   143	    for (const auto& input : inputs) ordered.push_back(&input);
   144	    std::sort(ordered.begin(), ordered.end(),
   145	        [](const auto* left, const auto* right)
   146	    {
   147	        return left->metricIdentity < right->metricIdentity;
   148	    });
   149	    for (std::size_t index = 1; index < ordered.size(); ++index)
   150	        if (ordered[index - 1]->metricIdentity ==
   151	            ordered[index]->metricIdentity)
   152	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   153	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_rule_duplicate");
   154	    std::vector<MetricRule> rules;
   155	    rules.reserve(inputs.size());
   156	    for (const auto* input : ordered)
   157	        rules.emplace_back(input->metricIdentity, input->direction);
   158	    return rules;
   159	}

   161	std::string PolicyCanonicalText(
   162	    int minimumComparableMemberCount,
   163	    const std::vector<MetricRule>& rules)
   164	{
   165	    std::ostringstream out;
   166	    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
   167	    out << "experiment_recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_v1"
   168	        << ";policy_contract_version="
   169	        << kRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyContractVersion
   170	        << ";minimum_comparable_member_count="
   171	        << minimumComparableMemberCount
   172	        << ";required_metric_rule_count=" << rules.size();
   173	    for (const auto& rule : rules)
   174	        out << ";required_metric=" << LengthPrefixed(rule.metricIdentity)
   175	            << ','
   176	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricDirectionText(
   177	                   rule.direction);
   178	    out << ";all_members_consistent_required=true"
   179	           ";all_members_terminal_required=true"
   180	           ";all_successful_members_comparable_required=true"
   181	           ";follow_up_requires_all_members_comparable_success=true"
   182	           ";follow_up_requires_favorable_campaign_interpretation=true"
   183	           ";follow_up_authorizing=false";
   184	    return out.str();
   185	}

   187	RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyValidationView ValidationView(
   188	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy& policy,
   189	    const Assessment& assessment)
   190	{
   191	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyValidationView view;
   192	    view.policyContractVersion = policy.identity.contractVersion;
   193	    view.policyCanonicalText = policy.identity.canonicalText;
   194	    view.policyIdentityHash = policy.identity.hash;
   195	    view.assessmentContractVersion = assessment.identity.contractVersion;
   196	    view.assessmentCanonicalText = assessment.identity.canonicalText;
   197	    view.assessmentIdentityHash = assessment.identity.hash;
   198	    view.campaignApprovalId = assessment.campaignIdentity.campaignApprovalId;
   199	    view.campaignIdentityCanonical =
   200	        assessment.campaignIdentity.identityCanonical;
   201	    view.campaignIdentityHash = assessment.campaignIdentity.identityHash;
   202	    view.materializationId =
   203	        assessment.materializationIdentity.materializationId;
   204	    view.materializationCampaignApprovalId =
   205	        assessment.materializationIdentity.campaignApprovalId;
   206	    view.materializationCampaignIdentityHash =
   207	        assessment.materializationIdentity.campaignIdentityHash;
   208	    view.materializationContractVersion =
   209	        assessment.materializationIdentity.contractVersion;
   210	    view.materializationMemberCount =
   211	        assessment.materializationIdentity.memberCount;
   212	    view.materializationIdentityCanonical =
   213	        assessment.materializationIdentity.identityCanonical;
   214	    view.materializationIdentityHash =
   215	        assessment.materializationIdentity.identityHash;
   216	    view.assessmentSummaryMemberCount = assessment.summary.memberCount;
   217	    view.members.reserve(assessment.members.size());
   218	    for (const auto& member : assessment.members)
   219	    {
   220	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMemberValidationView memberView;
   221	        memberView.memberOrdinal = member.identity.memberOrdinal;
   222	        memberView.materializationMemberId =
   223	            member.identity.materializationMemberId;
   224	        memberView.rankingMemberId = member.identity.rankingMemberId;
   225	        memberView.recommendationId = member.identity.recommendationId;
   226	        memberView.sourceExperimentId = member.identity.sourceExperimentId;
   227	        memberView.proposalId = member.identity.proposalId;
   228	        memberView.expectedExperimentId =
   229	            member.identity.expectedExperimentId;
   230	        memberView.comparisonMetricIdentities.reserve(
   231	            member.comparisons.size());
   232	        for (const auto& comparison : member.comparisons)
   233	            memberView.comparisonMetricIdentities.push_back(
   234	                comparison.metricIdentity);
   235	        view.members.push_back(std::move(memberView));
   236	    }
   237	    return view;
   238	}
   239	
   240	void ValidateAssessment(
   241	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy& policy,
   242	    const Assessment& assessment)
   243	{
   244	    ValidateRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyEvidence(
   245	        ValidationView(policy, assessment));
   246	
   247	    (void)RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentAggregateOutcomeText(
   248	        assessment.summary.outcome);
   249	    for (const auto& member : assessment.members)
   250	    {
   251	        (void)RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleStateText(
   252	            member.lifecycle);
   253	        (void)RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentConsistencyStateText(
   254	            member.consistency);
   255	        (void)RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberOutcomeText(
   256	            member.outcome);
   257	        for (const auto diagnostic : member.diagnostics)
   258	            (void)RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentDiagnosticCodeText(
   259	                diagnostic);
   260	        for (const auto& comparison : member.comparisons)
   261	        {
   262	            (void)RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonClassificationText(
   263	                comparison.classification);
   264	            for (const auto difference : comparison.contextDifferences)
   265	                (void)RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifferenceText(
   266	                    difference);
   267	        }
   268	    }
   269	}
   270	
   271	EvidenceClassification EvidenceClassificationFrom(
   272	    AssessmentMemberOutcome outcome)
   273	{
   274	    switch (outcome)
   275	    {
   276	        case AssessmentMemberOutcome::Inconsistent:
   277	            return EvidenceClassification::Inconsistent;
   278	        case AssessmentMemberOutcome::NotReady:
   279	            return EvidenceClassification::NotReady;
   280	        case AssessmentMemberOutcome::TerminalFailed:
   281	            return EvidenceClassification::TerminalFailed;
   282	        case AssessmentMemberOutcome::TerminalCancelled:
   283	            return EvidenceClassification::TerminalCancelled;
   284	        case AssessmentMemberOutcome::SucceededContextChanged:
   285	            return EvidenceClassification::SucceededContextChanged;
   286	        case AssessmentMemberOutcome::SucceededMetricGap:
   287	            return EvidenceClassification::SucceededMetricGap;
   288	        case AssessmentMemberOutcome::SucceededComparable:
   289	            return EvidenceClassification::SucceededComparable;
   290	    }
   291	    throw std::invalid_argument(
   292	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_assessment_outcome_invalid");
   293	}
   294	
   295	const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparison* FindComparison(
   296	    const AssessmentMember& member,
   297	    const std::string& metricIdentity)
   298	{
   299	    const auto found = std::lower_bound(member.comparisons.begin(),
   300	        member.comparisons.end(), metricIdentity,
   301	        [](const auto& comparison, const std::string& identity)
   302	    {
   303	        return comparison.metricIdentity < identity;
   304	    });
   305	    return found != member.comparisons.end() &&
   306	            found->metricIdentity == metricIdentity
   307	        ? &*found
   308	        : nullptr;
   309	}
   310	
   311	MetricJudgment JudgeDelta(double delta, MetricDirection direction)
   312	{
   313	    if (delta == 0.0) return MetricJudgment::Neutral;
   314	    const bool favorable = direction == MetricDirection::HigherIsFavorable
   315	        ? delta > 0.0
   316	        : delta < 0.0;
   317	    return favorable ? MetricJudgment::Favorable
   318	                     : MetricJudgment::Unfavorable;
   319	}
   320	
   321	MemberInterpretation InterpretMetricJudgments(
   322	    const std::vector<MetricEvaluation>& evaluations)
   323	{
   324	    bool favorable = false;
   325	    bool unfavorable = false;
   326	    for (const auto& evaluation : evaluations)
   327	    {
   328	        switch (evaluation.judgment)
   329	        {
   330	            case MetricJudgment::NotEvaluable:
   331	                return MemberInterpretation::Inconclusive;
   332	            case MetricJudgment::Favorable:
   333	                favorable = true;
   334	                break;
   335	            case MetricJudgment::Neutral:
   336	                break;
   337	            case MetricJudgment::Unfavorable:
   338	                unfavorable = true;
   339	                break;
   340	        }
   341	    }
   342	    if (favorable && unfavorable) return MemberInterpretation::Mixed;
   343	    if (unfavorable) return MemberInterpretation::Unfavorable;
   344	    if (favorable) return MemberInterpretation::Favorable;
   345	    return MemberInterpretation::Neutral;
   346	}
   347	
   348	void AddMemberReason(
   349	    std::vector<PolicyReason>& reasons,
   350	    EvidenceClassification classification)
   351	{
   352	    switch (classification)
   353	    {
   354	        case EvidenceClassification::Inconsistent:
   355	            AddReason(reasons, PolicyReason::InconsistentMemberPresent);
   356	            break;
   357	        case EvidenceClassification::NotReady:
   358	            AddReason(reasons, PolicyReason::NotReadyMemberPresent);
   359	            break;
   360	        case EvidenceClassification::TerminalFailed:
   361	            AddReason(reasons, PolicyReason::FailedMemberPresent);
   362	            break;
   363	        case EvidenceClassification::TerminalCancelled:
   364	            AddReason(reasons, PolicyReason::CancelledMemberPresent);
   365	            break;
   366	        case EvidenceClassification::SucceededContextChanged:
   367	            AddReason(reasons, PolicyReason::ContextChangedMemberPresent);
   368	            break;
   369	        case EvidenceClassification::SucceededMetricGap:
   370	            AddReason(reasons, PolicyReason::MetricGapMemberPresent);
   371	            break;
   372	        case EvidenceClassification::SucceededComparable:
   373	            break;
   374	    }
   375	}
   376	
   377	void AddComparisonReason(
   378	    std::vector<PolicyReason>& reasons,
   379	    AssessmentClassification classification)
   380	{
   381	    switch (classification)
   382	    {
   383	        case AssessmentClassification::Comparable:
   384	            break;
   385	        case AssessmentClassification::ContextChanged:
   386	            AddReason(reasons,
   387	                PolicyReason::ContextChangedComparisonPresent);
   388	            break;
   389	        case AssessmentClassification::MissingSourceMetric:
   390	            AddReason(reasons, PolicyReason::MissingSourceMetricPresent);
   391	            break;
   392	        case AssessmentClassification::MissingResultMetric:
   393	            AddReason(reasons, PolicyReason::MissingResultMetricPresent);
   394	            break;
   395	        case AssessmentClassification::MetricValueUnavailable:
   396	            AddReason(reasons, PolicyReason::MetricValueUnavailablePresent);
   397	            break;
   398	        case AssessmentClassification::Unsupported:
   399	            AddReason(reasons, PolicyReason::UnsupportedMetricPresent);
   400	            break;
   401	    }
   402	}
   403	
   404	struct MutableCounts
   405	{
   406	    int inconsistent = 0;
   407	    int notReady = 0;
   408	    int failed = 0;
   409	    int cancelled = 0;
   410	    int contextChanged = 0;
   411	    int metricGap = 0;
   412	    int comparable = 0;
   413	    std::size_t notEvaluableMetrics = 0;
   414	    std::size_t favorableMetrics = 0;
   415	    std::size_t neutralMetrics = 0;
   416	    std::size_t unfavorableMetrics = 0;
   417	};
   418	
   419	void CountMember(MutableCounts& counts, const PolicyMember& member)
   420	{
   421	    switch (member.evidenceClassification)
   422	    {
   423	        case EvidenceClassification::Inconsistent:
   424	            ++counts.inconsistent;
   425	            break;
   426	        case EvidenceClassification::NotReady:
   427	            ++counts.notReady;
   428	            break;
   429	        case EvidenceClassification::TerminalFailed:
   430	            ++counts.failed;
   431	            break;
   432	        case EvidenceClassification::TerminalCancelled:
   433	            ++counts.cancelled;
   434	            break;
   435	        case EvidenceClassification::SucceededContextChanged:
   436	            ++counts.contextChanged;
   437	            break;
   438	        case EvidenceClassification::SucceededMetricGap:
   439	            ++counts.metricGap;
   440	            break;
   441	        case EvidenceClassification::SucceededComparable:
   442	            ++counts.comparable;
   443	            break;
   444	    }
   445	    for (const auto& evaluation : member.metricEvaluations)
   446	    {
   447	        switch (evaluation.judgment)
   448	        {
   449	            case MetricJudgment::NotEvaluable:
   450	                ++counts.notEvaluableMetrics;
   451	                break;
   452	            case MetricJudgment::Favorable:
   453	                ++counts.favorableMetrics;
   454	                break;
   455	            case MetricJudgment::Neutral:
   456	                ++counts.neutralMetrics;
   457	                break;
   458	            case MetricJudgment::Unfavorable:
   459	                ++counts.unfavorableMetrics;
   460	                break;
   461	        }
   462	    }
   463	}
   464	
   465	CampaignInterpretation DeriveCampaignInterpretation(
   466	    const MutableCounts& counts,
   467	    int memberCount,
   468	    int minimumComparableMemberCount,
   469	    const std::vector<PolicyMember>& members)
   470	{
   471	    if (counts.inconsistent > 0 || counts.notReady > 0 ||
   472	        counts.cancelled > 0 || counts.contextChanged > 0 ||
   473	        counts.metricGap > 0 ||
   474	        (counts.comparable < minimumComparableMemberCount &&
   475	            counts.failed != memberCount))
   476	        return CampaignInterpretation::Inconclusive;
   477	    if (counts.failed == memberCount)
   478	        return CampaignInterpretation::Unfavorable;
   479	
   480	    bool favorable = false;
   481	    bool unfavorable = counts.failed > 0;
   482	    for (const auto& member : members)
   483	    {
   484	        switch (member.interpretation)
   485	        {
   486	            case MemberInterpretation::Inconclusive:
   487	                return CampaignInterpretation::Inconclusive;
   488	            case MemberInterpretation::Favorable:
   489	                favorable = true;
   490	                break;
   491	            case MemberInterpretation::Neutral:
   492	                break;
   493	            case MemberInterpretation::Unfavorable:
   494	                unfavorable = true;
   495	                break;
   496	            case MemberInterpretation::Mixed:
   497	                favorable = true;
   498	                unfavorable = true;
   499	                break;
   500	        }
   501	    }
   502	    if (favorable && unfavorable) return CampaignInterpretation::Mixed;
   503	    if (unfavorable) return CampaignInterpretation::Unfavorable;
   504	    if (favorable) return CampaignInterpretation::Favorable;
   505	    return CampaignInterpretation::Neutral;
   506	}
   507	
   508	void AppendReasons(
   509	    std::ostringstream& out,
   510	    const std::vector<PolicyReason>& reasons)
   511	{
   512	    out << reasons.size();
   513	    for (const auto reason : reasons)
   514	        out << ',' << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyReasonText(reason);
   515	}
   516	
   517	void AppendMetricEvaluation(
   518	    std::ostringstream& out,
   519	    const MetricEvaluation& evaluation)
   520	{
   521	    out << LengthPrefixed(evaluation.metricIdentity) << ','
   522	        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricDirectionText(
   523	               evaluation.direction)
   524	        << ",assessment_classification="
   525	        << OptionalClassification(evaluation.evidenceClassification)
   526	        << ",assessment_delta="
   527	        << OptionalDouble(evaluation.assessmentDelta)
   528	        << ",policy_judgment="
   529	        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricJudgmentText(
   530	               evaluation.judgment);
   531	}
   532	
   533	std::string DecisionCanonicalText(
   534	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy& policy,
   535	    const Assessment& assessment,
   536	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicySummary& summary,
   537	    const std::vector<PolicyMember>& members)
   538	{
   539	    std::ostringstream out;
   540	    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
   541	    out << "experiment_recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_decision_v1"
   542	        << ";decision_contract_version="
   543	        << kRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyDecisionContractVersion
   544	        << ";policy_contract_version=" << policy.identity.contractVersion
   545	        << ";policy_canonical="
   546	        << LengthPrefixed(policy.identity.canonicalText)
   547	        << ";policy_hash=" << LengthPrefixed(policy.identity.hash)
   548	        << ";assessment_contract_version="
   549	        << assessment.identity.contractVersion
   550	        << ";assessment_canonical="
   551	        << LengthPrefixed(assessment.identity.canonicalText)
   552	        << ";assessment_hash=" << LengthPrefixed(assessment.identity.hash)
   553	        << ";campaign_approval_id="
   554	        << assessment.campaignIdentity.campaignApprovalId
   555	        << ";campaign_identity="
   556	        << LengthPrefixed(assessment.campaignIdentity.identityCanonical)
   557	        << ";campaign_identity_hash="
   558	        << LengthPrefixed(assessment.campaignIdentity.identityHash)
   559	        << ";materialization_id="
   560	        << assessment.materializationIdentity.materializationId
   561	        << ";materialization_campaign_approval_id="
   562	        << assessment.materializationIdentity.campaignApprovalId
   563	        << ";materialization_campaign_identity_hash="
   564	        << LengthPrefixed(
   565	               assessment.materializationIdentity.campaignIdentityHash)
   566	        << ";materialization_contract_version="
   567	        << assessment.materializationIdentity.contractVersion
   568	        << ";materialization_member_count="
   569	        << assessment.materializationIdentity.memberCount
   570	        << ";materialization_identity="
   571	        << LengthPrefixed(
   572	               assessment.materializationIdentity.identityCanonical)
   573	        << ";materialization_identity_hash="
   574	        << LengthPrefixed(assessment.materializationIdentity.identityHash);
   575	    for (const auto& member : members)
   576	    {
   577	        out << ";member=" << member.identity.memberOrdinal << ','
   578	            << member.identity.materializationMemberId << ','
   579	            << member.identity.rankingMemberId << ','
   580	            << member.identity.recommendationId << ','
   581	            << member.identity.sourceExperimentId << ','
   582	            << member.identity.proposalId
   583	            << ",expected_experiment_id="
   584	            << OptionalLongLong(member.identity.expectedExperimentId)
   585	            << ",assessment_lifecycle="
   586	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleStateText(
   587	                   member.lifecycle)
   588	            << ",assessment_consistency="
   589	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentConsistencyStateText(
   590	                   member.consistency)
   591	            << ",assessment_outcome="
   592	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberOutcomeText(
   593	                   member.assessmentOutcome)
   594	            << ",assessment_diagnostic_count="
   595	            << member.assessmentDiagnostics.size();
   596	        for (const auto diagnostic : member.assessmentDiagnostics)
   597	            out << ','
   598	                << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentDiagnosticCodeText(
   599	                       diagnostic);
   600	        out << ",evidence_classification="
   601	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyEvidenceClassificationText(
   602	                   member.evidenceClassification)
   603	            << ",policy_interpretation="
   604	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMemberInterpretationText(
   605	                   member.interpretation)
   606	            << ",metric_evaluation_count="
   607	            << member.metricEvaluations.size();
   608	        for (const auto& evaluation : member.metricEvaluations)
   609	        {
   610	            out << ',';
   611	            AppendMetricEvaluation(out, evaluation);
   612	        }
   613	    }
   614	    out << ";evidence_sufficiency="
   615	        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyEvidenceSufficiencyText(
   616	               summary.evidenceSufficiency)
   617	        << ";campaign_interpretation="
   618	        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyCampaignInterpretationText(
   619	               summary.campaignInterpretation)
   620	        << ";follow_up_eligibility="
   621	        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyFollowUpEligibilityText(
   622	               summary.followUpEligibility)
   623	        << ";follow_up_authorized="
   624	        << (summary.followUpAuthorized ? "true" : "false")
   625	        << ";member_count=" << summary.memberCount
   626	        << ";inconsistent_member_count=" << summary.inconsistentMemberCount
   627	        << ";not_ready_member_count=" << summary.notReadyMemberCount
   628	        << ";failed_member_count=" << summary.failedMemberCount
   629	        << ";cancelled_member_count=" << summary.cancelledMemberCount
   630	        << ";context_changed_member_count="
   631	        << summary.contextChangedMemberCount
   632	        << ";metric_gap_member_count=" << summary.metricGapMemberCount
   633	        << ";comparable_member_count=" << summary.comparableMemberCount
   634	        << ";metric_evaluation_count=" << summary.metricEvaluationCount
   635	        << ";not_evaluable_metric_count="
   636	        << summary.notEvaluableMetricCount
   637	        << ";favorable_metric_count=" << summary.favorableMetricCount
   638	        << ";neutral_metric_count=" << summary.neutralMetricCount
   639	        << ";unfavorable_metric_count=" << summary.unfavorableMetricCount
   640	        << ";evidence_reasons=";
   641	    AppendReasons(out, summary.evidenceReasons);
   642	    out << ";interpretation_reasons=";
   643	    AppendReasons(out, summary.interpretationReasons);
   644	    out << ";follow_up_reasons=";
   645	    AppendReasons(out, summary.followUpReasons);
   646	    return out.str();
   647	}
   648	
   649	} // namespace
   650	
   651	void ValidateRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyEvidence(
   652	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyValidationView& view)
   653	{
   654	    if (view.policyContractVersion !=
   655	        kRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyContractVersion)
   656	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   657	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_version_unsupported");
   658	    RequireText(view.policyCanonicalText,
   659	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_identity_missing");
   660	    if (view.policyIdentityHash !=
   661	        RecommendationCanonicalHash(view.policyCanonicalText))
   662	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   663	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_identity_invalid");
   664	
   665	    if (view.assessmentContractVersion !=
   666	        kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion)
   667	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   668	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_assessment_version_unsupported");
   669	    RequireText(view.assessmentCanonicalText,
   670	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_assessment_identity_missing");
   671	    if (view.assessmentIdentityHash !=
   672	        RecommendationCanonicalHash(view.assessmentCanonicalText))
   673	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   674	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_assessment_identity_invalid");
   675	
   676	    RequireText(view.campaignIdentityCanonical,
   677	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_campaign_identity_missing");
   678	    if (view.campaignApprovalId <= 0 ||
   679	        view.campaignIdentityHash !=
   680	            RecommendationCanonicalHash(view.campaignIdentityCanonical))
   681	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   682	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_campaign_identity_invalid");
   683	
   684	    RequireText(view.materializationIdentityCanonical,
   685	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_materialization_identity_missing");
   686	    if (view.materializationId <= 0 ||
   687	        view.materializationCampaignApprovalId <= 0 ||
   688	        view.materializationContractVersion <= 0 ||
   689	        view.materializationIdentityHash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(
   690	            view.materializationIdentityCanonical))
   691	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   692	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_materialization_identity_invalid");
   693	    if (view.materializationCampaignApprovalId != view.campaignApprovalId ||
   694	        view.materializationCampaignIdentityHash !=
   695	            view.campaignIdentityHash)
   696	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   697	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_campaign_materialization_mismatch");
   698	
   699	    if (view.materializationMemberCount !=
   700	            static_cast<int>(view.members.size()) ||
   701	        view.assessmentSummaryMemberCount !=
   702	            static_cast<int>(view.members.size()) ||
   703	        view.members.empty() ||
   704	        view.members.size() > static_cast<std::size_t>(
   705	            kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers))
   706	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   707	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_assessment_shape_invalid");
   708	
   709	    std::set<long long> materializationMemberIds;
   710	    std::set<long long> rankingMemberIds;
   711	    std::set<long long> recommendationIds;
   712	    std::set<long long> proposalIds;
   713	    for (std::size_t index = 0; index < view.members.size(); ++index)
   714	    {
   715	        const auto& member = view.members[index];
   716	        if (member.memberOrdinal != static_cast<int>(index) + 1 ||
   717	            member.materializationMemberId <= 0 ||
   718	            member.rankingMemberId <= 0 || member.recommendationId <= 0 ||
   719	            member.sourceExperimentId <= 0 || member.proposalId <= 0 ||
   720	            (member.expectedExperimentId &&
   721	                *member.expectedExperimentId <= 0) ||
   722	            !materializationMemberIds
   723	                 .insert(member.materializationMemberId).second ||
   724	            !rankingMemberIds.insert(member.rankingMemberId).second ||
   725	            !recommendationIds.insert(member.recommendationId).second ||
   726	            !proposalIds.insert(member.proposalId).second)
   727	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   728	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_member_identity_set_invalid");
   729	        std::set<std::string> comparisonIdentities;
   730	        for (const auto& identity : member.comparisonMetricIdentities)
   731	        {
   732	            RequireText(identity,
   733	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_comparison_identity_invalid");
   734	            if (!comparisonIdentities.insert(identity).second)
   735	                throw std::invalid_argument(
   736	                    "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_comparison_identity_duplicate");
   737	        }
   738	    }
   739	}
   740	
   741	RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricRule::
   742	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricRule(
   743	        std::string metricIdentityValue,
   744	        MetricDirection directionValue)
   745	    : metricIdentity(std::move(metricIdentityValue)),
   746	      direction(directionValue)
   747	{
   748	    RequireText(metricIdentity,
   749	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_identity_invalid");
   750	    RequireValidDirection(direction);
   751	}
   752	
   753	RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyIdentity::
   754	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyIdentity(
   755	        int contractVersionValue,
   756	        std::string canonicalTextValue,
   757	        std::string hashValue)
   758	    : contractVersion(contractVersionValue),
   759	      canonicalText(std::move(canonicalTextValue)),
   760	      hash(std::move(hashValue))
   740	
   741	RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricRule::
   742	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricRule(
   743	        std::string metricIdentityValue,
   744	        MetricDirection directionValue)
   745	    : metricIdentity(std::move(metricIdentityValue)),
   746	      direction(directionValue)
   747	{
   748	    RequireText(metricIdentity,
   749	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_identity_invalid");
   750	    RequireValidDirection(direction);
   751	}

   785	RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricEvaluation::
   786	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricEvaluation(
   787	        std::string metricIdentityValue,
   788	        MetricDirection directionValue,
   789	        std::optional<AssessmentClassification> evidenceClassificationValue,
   790	        std::optional<double> assessmentDeltaValue,
   791	        MetricJudgment judgmentValue)
   792	    : metricIdentity(std::move(metricIdentityValue)),
   793	      direction(directionValue),
   794	      evidenceClassification(evidenceClassificationValue),
   795	      assessmentDelta(assessmentDeltaValue && *assessmentDeltaValue == 0.0
   796	              ? std::optional<double>{0.0}
   797	              : assessmentDeltaValue),
   798	      judgment(judgmentValue)
   799	{
   800	    RequireText(metricIdentity,
   801	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_evaluation_identity_invalid");
   802	    RequireValidDirection(direction);
   803	    RequireValidMetricJudgment(judgment);
   804	    if (evidenceClassification)
   805	        (void)RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonClassificationText(
   806	            *evidenceClassification);
   807	    if (assessmentDelta && !std::isfinite(*assessmentDelta))
   808	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   809	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_delta_nonfinite");
   810	    const bool evaluable = judgment != MetricJudgment::NotEvaluable;
   811	    if (evaluable !=
   812	            (evidenceClassification ==
   813	                    AssessmentClassification::Comparable &&
   814	                assessmentDelta.has_value()))
   815	        throw std::logic_error(
   816	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_evaluation_invariant_failed");
   817	}

   844	RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicySummary::
   845	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicySummary(
   846	        EvidenceSufficiency evidenceSufficiencyValue,
   847	        CampaignInterpretation campaignInterpretationValue,
   848	        FollowUpEligibility followUpEligibilityValue,
   849	        bool followUpAuthorizedValue,
   850	        int memberCountValue,
   851	        int inconsistentMemberCountValue,
   852	        int notReadyMemberCountValue,
   853	        int failedMemberCountValue,
   854	        int cancelledMemberCountValue,
   855	        int contextChangedMemberCountValue,
   856	        int metricGapMemberCountValue,
   857	        int comparableMemberCountValue,
   858	        std::size_t metricEvaluationCountValue,
   859	        std::size_t notEvaluableMetricCountValue,
   860	        std::size_t favorableMetricCountValue,
   861	        std::size_t neutralMetricCountValue,
   862	        std::size_t unfavorableMetricCountValue,
   863	        std::vector<PolicyReason> evidenceReasonsValue,
   864	        std::vector<PolicyReason> interpretationReasonsValue,
   865	        std::vector<PolicyReason> followUpReasonsValue)
   866	    : evidenceSufficiency(evidenceSufficiencyValue),
   867	      campaignInterpretation(campaignInterpretationValue),
   868	      followUpEligibility(followUpEligibilityValue),
   869	      followUpAuthorized(followUpAuthorizedValue),
   870	      memberCount(memberCountValue),
   871	      inconsistentMemberCount(inconsistentMemberCountValue),
   872	      notReadyMemberCount(notReadyMemberCountValue),
   873	      failedMemberCount(failedMemberCountValue),
   874	      cancelledMemberCount(cancelledMemberCountValue),
   875	      contextChangedMemberCount(contextChangedMemberCountValue),
   876	      metricGapMemberCount(metricGapMemberCountValue),
   877	      comparableMemberCount(comparableMemberCountValue),
   878	      metricEvaluationCount(metricEvaluationCountValue),
   879	      notEvaluableMetricCount(notEvaluableMetricCountValue),
   880	      favorableMetricCount(favorableMetricCountValue),
   881	      neutralMetricCount(neutralMetricCountValue),
   882	      unfavorableMetricCount(unfavorableMetricCountValue),
   883	      evidenceReasons(std::move(evidenceReasonsValue)),
   884	      interpretationReasons(std::move(interpretationReasonsValue)),
   885	      followUpReasons(std::move(followUpReasonsValue))
   886	{
   887	    const int classifiedMembers = inconsistentMemberCount +
   888	        notReadyMemberCount + failedMemberCount + cancelledMemberCount +
   889	        contextChangedMemberCount + metricGapMemberCount +
   890	        comparableMemberCount;
   891	    const std::size_t classifiedMetrics = notEvaluableMetricCount +
   892	        favorableMetricCount + neutralMetricCount + unfavorableMetricCount;
   893	    if (memberCount <= 0 || classifiedMembers != memberCount ||
   894	        classifiedMetrics != metricEvaluationCount || followUpAuthorized)
   895	        throw std::logic_error(
   896	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_summary_invariant_failed");
   897	}

   918	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy policyValue,
   919	        int assessmentContractVersionValue,
   920	        std::string assessmentCanonicalTextValue,

   923	            campaignIdentityValue,
   924	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity
   925	            materializationIdentityValue,

   927	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicySummary summaryValue,
   928	        std::vector<PolicyMember> membersValue)
   929	    : identity(std::move(identityValue)),
   930	      policy(std::move(policyValue)),
   931	      assessmentContractVersion(assessmentContractVersionValue),

   949	struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyBuilder
   950	{
   951	    static RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy Build(
   952	        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyInput& input)
   953	    {
   954	        if (input.contractVersion !=
   955	            kRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyContractVersion)
   956	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   957	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_version_unsupported");
   958	        if (input.minimumComparableMemberCount <= 0 ||
   959	            input.minimumComparableMemberCount >
   960	                kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers)
   961	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   962	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_minimum_evidence_invalid");
   963	        auto rules = CanonicalRules(input.requiredMetricRules);
   964	        std::string canonical = PolicyCanonicalText(
   965	            input.minimumComparableMemberCount, rules);
   966	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyIdentity identity(
   967	            kRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyContractVersion,
   968	            canonical, RecommendationCanonicalHash(canonical));
   969	        return RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy(
   970	            input.minimumComparableMemberCount, std::move(rules),
   971	            std::move(identity));
   972	    }
   973	};
   974	
   975	struct RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyDecisionBuilder
   976	{
   977	    static RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyDecision Build(
   978	        const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy& policy,
   979	        const Assessment& assessment)
   980	    {
   981	        ValidateAssessment(policy, assessment);
   982	
   983	        std::vector<PolicyMember> members;
   984	        members.reserve(assessment.members.size());
   985	        std::vector<PolicyReason> evidenceReasons;
   986	        std::vector<PolicyReason> interpretationReasons;
   987	        bool requiredMetricMissing = false;
   988	        bool requiredMetricNotComparable = false;
   989	
   990	        for (const auto& assessed : assessment.members)
   991	        {
   992	            const auto classification =
   993	                EvidenceClassificationFrom(assessed.outcome);
   994	            AddMemberReason(interpretationReasons, classification);
   995	            switch (classification)
   996	            {
   997	                case EvidenceClassification::Inconsistent:
   998	                case EvidenceClassification::NotReady:
   999	                case EvidenceClassification::TerminalCancelled:
  1000	                case EvidenceClassification::SucceededContextChanged:
  1001	                case EvidenceClassification::SucceededMetricGap:
  1002	                    AddMemberReason(evidenceReasons, classification);
  1003	                    break;
  1004	                case EvidenceClassification::TerminalFailed:
  1005	                case EvidenceClassification::SucceededComparable:
  1006	                    break;
  1007	            }
  1008	            for (const auto& comparison : assessed.comparisons)
  1009	            {
  1010	                AddComparisonReason(
  1011	                    interpretationReasons, comparison.classification);
  1012	                AddComparisonReason(evidenceReasons, comparison.classification);
  1013	            }
  1014	
  1015	            std::vector<MetricEvaluation> evaluations;
  1016	            evaluations.reserve(policy.requiredMetricRules.size());
  1017	            const bool successfulEvidence =
  1018	                classification ==
  1019	                    EvidenceClassification::SucceededContextChanged ||
  1020	                classification ==
  1021	                    EvidenceClassification::SucceededMetricGap ||
  1022	                classification ==
  1023	                    EvidenceClassification::SucceededComparable;
  1024	            for (const auto& rule : policy.requiredMetricRules)
  1025	            {
  1026	                const auto* comparison =
  1027	                    FindComparison(assessed, rule.metricIdentity);
  1028	                if (!comparison)
  1029	                {
  1030	                    if (successfulEvidence) requiredMetricMissing = true;
  1031	                    evaluations.push_back(MetricEvaluation(
  1032	                        rule.metricIdentity, rule.direction, std::nullopt,
  1033	                        std::nullopt, MetricJudgment::NotEvaluable));
  1034	                    continue;
  1035	                }
  1036	                if (comparison->classification !=
  1037	                        AssessmentClassification::Comparable ||
  1038	                    !comparison->delta)
  1039	                {
  1040	                    if (successfulEvidence)
  1041	                        requiredMetricNotComparable = true;
  1042	                    evaluations.push_back(MetricEvaluation(
  1043	                        rule.metricIdentity, rule.direction,
  1044	                        comparison->classification, std::nullopt,
  1045	                        MetricJudgment::NotEvaluable));
  1046	                    continue;
  1047	                }
  1048	                evaluations.push_back(MetricEvaluation(rule.metricIdentity,
  1049	                    rule.direction, comparison->classification,
  1050	                    comparison->delta,
  1051	                    JudgeDelta(*comparison->delta, rule.direction)));
  1052	            }
  1053	
  1054	            MemberInterpretation interpretation =
  1055	                MemberInterpretation::Inconclusive;
  1056	            if (classification == EvidenceClassification::TerminalFailed)
  1057	                interpretation = MemberInterpretation::Unfavorable;
  1058	            else if (classification ==
  1059	                EvidenceClassification::SucceededComparable)
  1060	                interpretation = InterpretMetricJudgments(evaluations);
  1061	
  1062	            members.push_back(PolicyMember(assessed.identity, assessed.lifecycle,
  1063	                assessed.consistency, assessed.outcome,
  1064	                assessed.diagnostics, classification, interpretation,
  1065	                std::move(evaluations)));
  1066	        }
  1067	
  1068	        if (requiredMetricMissing)
  1069	        {
  1070	            AddReason(evidenceReasons, PolicyReason::RequiredMetricMissing);
  1071	            AddReason(interpretationReasons,
  1072	                PolicyReason::RequiredMetricMissing);
  1073	        }
  1074	        if (requiredMetricNotComparable)
  1075	        {
  1076	            AddReason(evidenceReasons,
  1077	                PolicyReason::RequiredMetricNotComparable);
  1078	            AddReason(interpretationReasons,
  1079	                PolicyReason::RequiredMetricNotComparable);
  1080	        }
  1081	
  1082	        MutableCounts counts;
  1083	        for (const auto& member : members) CountMember(counts, member);
  1084	        if (counts.comparable < policy.minimumComparableMemberCount)
  1085	        {
  1086	            AddReason(evidenceReasons,
  1087	                PolicyReason::MinimumComparableMembersNotMet);
  1088	            AddReason(interpretationReasons,
  1089	                PolicyReason::MinimumComparableMembersNotMet);
  1090	        }
  1091	        if (counts.favorableMetrics > 0)
  1092	            AddReason(interpretationReasons,
  1093	                PolicyReason::FavorableMetricEvidencePresent);
  1094	        if (counts.neutralMetrics > 0)
  1095	            AddReason(interpretationReasons,
  1096	                PolicyReason::NeutralMetricEvidencePresent);
  1097	        if (counts.unfavorableMetrics > 0)
  1098	            AddReason(interpretationReasons,
  1099	                PolicyReason::UnfavorableMetricEvidencePresent);
  1100	        CanonicalizeReasons(evidenceReasons);
  1101	        CanonicalizeReasons(interpretationReasons);
  1102	
  1103	        const EvidenceSufficiency sufficiency = evidenceReasons.empty()
  1104	            ? EvidenceSufficiency::Sufficient
  1105	            : EvidenceSufficiency::Insufficient;
  1106	        const CampaignInterpretation campaignInterpretation =
  1107	            DeriveCampaignInterpretation(counts,
  1108	                static_cast<int>(members.size()),
  1109	                policy.minimumComparableMemberCount, members);
  1110	        const bool eligible =
  1111	            sufficiency == EvidenceSufficiency::Sufficient &&
  1112	            counts.comparable == static_cast<int>(members.size()) &&
  1113	            campaignInterpretation == CampaignInterpretation::Favorable;
  1114	        std::vector<PolicyReason> followUpReasons;
  1115	        if (eligible)
  1116	            AddReason(followUpReasons,
  1117	                PolicyReason::AllMembersSucceededComparable);
  1118	        else
  1119	        {
  1120	            if (sufficiency == EvidenceSufficiency::Insufficient)
  1121	                AddReason(followUpReasons,
  1122	                    PolicyReason::EvidenceInsufficient);
  1123	            if (campaignInterpretation != CampaignInterpretation::Favorable)
  1124	                AddReason(followUpReasons,
  1125	                    PolicyReason::FavorableCampaignInterpretationRequired);
  1126	            for (const auto& member : members)
  1127	                AddMemberReason(
  1128	                    followUpReasons, member.evidenceClassification);
  1129	        }
  1130	        CanonicalizeReasons(followUpReasons);
  1131	
  1132	        const std::size_t metricEvaluationCount = members.size() *
  1133	            policy.requiredMetricRules.size();
  1134	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicySummary summary(sufficiency,
  1135	            campaignInterpretation,
  1136	            eligible ? FollowUpEligibility::EligibleForOperatorReview
  1137	                     : FollowUpEligibility::NotEligible,
  1138	            false, static_cast<int>(members.size()), counts.inconsistent,
  1139	            counts.notReady, counts.failed, counts.cancelled,
  1140	            counts.contextChanged, counts.metricGap, counts.comparable,
  1141	            metricEvaluationCount, counts.notEvaluableMetrics,
  1142	            counts.favorableMetrics, counts.neutralMetrics,
  1143	            counts.unfavorableMetrics, std::move(evidenceReasons),
  1144	            std::move(interpretationReasons),
  1145	            std::move(followUpReasons));
  1146	        std::string canonical = DecisionCanonicalText(
  1147	            policy, assessment, summary, members);
  1148	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyDecisionIdentity identity(
  1149	            kRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyDecisionContractVersion,
  1150	            canonical, RecommendationCanonicalHash(canonical));
  1151	        return RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyDecision(
  1152	            std::move(identity), policy, assessment.identity.contractVersion,
  1153	            assessment.identity.canonicalText, assessment.identity.hash,
  1154	            assessment.campaignIdentity,
  1155	            assessment.materializationIdentity, assessment.observedAt,
  1156	            std::move(summary), std::move(members));
  1157	    }
  1158	};
  1159	
  1160	RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy(
  1161	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyInput& input)
  1162	{
  1163	    return RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyBuilder::Build(input);
  1164	}
  1165	
  1166	RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyDecision
  1167	ApplyRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy(
  1168	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy& policy,
  1169	    const Assessment& assessment)
  1170	{
  1171	    return RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyDecisionBuilder::Build(
  1172	        policy, assessment);
  1173	}

  1184	    }

   464	
   465	CampaignInterpretation DeriveCampaignInterpretation(
   466	    const MutableCounts& counts,
   467	    int memberCount,
   468	    int minimumComparableMemberCount,
   469	    const std::vector<PolicyMember>& members)
   470	{
   471	    if (counts.inconsistent > 0 || counts.notReady > 0 ||
   472	        counts.cancelled > 0 || counts.contextChanged > 0 ||
   473	        counts.metricGap > 0 ||
   474	        (counts.comparable < minimumComparableMemberCount &&
   475	            counts.failed != memberCount))
   476	        return CampaignInterpretation::Inconclusive;
   477	    if (counts.failed == memberCount)
   478	        return CampaignInterpretation::Unfavorable;
   479	
   480	    bool favorable = false;
   481	    bool unfavorable = counts.failed > 0;
   482	    for (const auto& member : members)
   483	    {
   484	        switch (member.interpretation)
   485	        {
   486	            case MemberInterpretation::Inconclusive:
   487	                return CampaignInterpretation::Inconclusive;
   488	            case MemberInterpretation::Favorable:
   489	                favorable = true;
   490	                break;
   491	            case MemberInterpretation::Neutral:
   492	                break;
   493	            case MemberInterpretation::Unfavorable:
   494	                unfavorable = true;
   495	                break;
   496	            case MemberInterpretation::Mixed:
   497	                favorable = true;
   498	                unfavorable = true;
   499	                break;
   500	        }
   501	    }
   502	    if (favorable && unfavorable) return CampaignInterpretation::Mixed;
   503	    if (unfavorable) return CampaignInterpretation::Unfavorable;
   504	    if (favorable) return CampaignInterpretation::Favorable;
   505	    return CampaignInterpretation::Neutral;
   506	}

   508	void AppendReasons(
   509	    std::ostringstream& out,
   510	    const std::vector<PolicyReason>& reasons)
   511	{
   512	    out << reasons.size();
   513	    for (const auto reason : reasons)
   514	        out << ',' << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyReasonText(reason);
   515	}

   517	void AppendMetricEvaluation(
   518	    std::ostringstream& out,
   519	    const MetricEvaluation& evaluation)
   520	{
   521	    out << LengthPrefixed(evaluation.metricIdentity) << ','
   522	        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricDirectionText(
   523	               evaluation.direction)
   524	        << ",assessment_classification="
   525	        << OptionalClassification(evaluation.evidenceClassification)
   526	        << ",assessment_delta="
   527	        << OptionalDouble(evaluation.assessmentDelta)
   528	        << ",policy_judgment="
   529	        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricJudgmentText(
   530	               evaluation.judgment);

   532	
   533	std::string DecisionCanonicalText(
   534	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy& policy,
   535	    const Assessment& assessment,
   536	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicySummary& summary,
   537	    const std::vector<PolicyMember>& members)
   538	{
   539	    std::ostringstream out;
   540	    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
   541	    out << "experiment_recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_decision_v1"
   542	        << ";decision_contract_version="
   543	        << kRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyDecisionContractVersion
   544	        << ";policy_contract_version=" << policy.identity.contractVersion
   545	        << ";policy_canonical="
   546	        << LengthPrefixed(policy.identity.canonicalText)
   547	        << ";policy_hash=" << LengthPrefixed(policy.identity.hash)
   548	        << ";assessment_contract_version="
   549	        << assessment.identity.contractVersion
   550	        << ";assessment_canonical="
   551	        << LengthPrefixed(assessment.identity.canonicalText)
   552	        << ";assessment_hash=" << LengthPrefixed(assessment.identity.hash)
   553	        << ";campaign_approval_id="
   554	        << assessment.campaignIdentity.campaignApprovalId
   555	        << ";campaign_identity="
   556	        << LengthPrefixed(assessment.campaignIdentity.identityCanonical)
   557	        << ";campaign_identity_hash="
   558	        << LengthPrefixed(assessment.campaignIdentity.identityHash)
   559	        << ";materialization_id="
   560	        << assessment.materializationIdentity.materializationId
   561	        << ";materialization_campaign_approval_id="
   562	        << assessment.materializationIdentity.campaignApprovalId
   563	        << ";materialization_campaign_identity_hash="
   564	        << LengthPrefixed(
   565	               assessment.materializationIdentity.campaignIdentityHash)
   566	        << ";materialization_contract_version="
   567	        << assessment.materializationIdentity.contractVersion
   568	        << ";materialization_member_count="
   569	        << assessment.materializationIdentity.memberCount
   570	        << ";materialization_identity="
   571	        << LengthPrefixed(
   572	               assessment.materializationIdentity.identityCanonical)
   573	        << ";materialization_identity_hash="
   574	        << LengthPrefixed(assessment.materializationIdentity.identityHash);
   575	    for (const auto& member : members)
   576	    {
   577	        out << ";member=" << member.identity.memberOrdinal << ','
   578	            << member.identity.materializationMemberId << ','
   579	            << member.identity.rankingMemberId << ','
   580	            << member.identity.recommendationId << ','
   581	            << member.identity.sourceExperimentId << ','
   582	            << member.identity.proposalId
   583	            << ",expected_experiment_id="
   584	            << OptionalLongLong(member.identity.expectedExperimentId)
   585	            << ",assessment_lifecycle="
   586	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleStateText(
   587	                   member.lifecycle)
   588	            << ",assessment_consistency="
   589	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentConsistencyStateText(
   590	                   member.consistency)
   591	            << ",assessment_outcome="
   592	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberOutcomeText(
   593	                   member.assessmentOutcome)
   594	            << ",assessment_diagnostic_count="
   595	            << member.assessmentDiagnostics.size();
   596	        for (const auto diagnostic : member.assessmentDiagnostics)
   597	            out << ','
   598	                << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentDiagnosticCodeText(
   599	                       diagnostic);
   600	        out << ",evidence_classification="
   601	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyEvidenceClassificationText(
   602	                   member.evidenceClassification)
   603	            << ",policy_interpretation="
   604	            << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMemberInterpretationText(
   605	                   member.interpretation)
   606	            << ",metric_evaluation_count="
   607	            << member.metricEvaluations.size();
   608	        for (const auto& evaluation : member.metricEvaluations)
   609	        {
   610	            out << ',';
   611	            AppendMetricEvaluation(out, evaluation);
   612	        }
   613	    }
   614	    out << ";evidence_sufficiency="
   615	        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyEvidenceSufficiencyText(
   616	               summary.evidenceSufficiency)
   617	        << ";campaign_interpretation="
   618	        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyCampaignInterpretationText(
   619	               summary.campaignInterpretation)
   620	        << ";follow_up_eligibility="
   621	        << RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyFollowUpEligibilityText(
   622	               summary.followUpEligibility)
   623	        << ";follow_up_authorized="
   624	        << (summary.followUpAuthorized ? "true" : "false")
   625	        << ";member_count=" << summary.memberCount
   626	        << ";inconsistent_member_count=" << summary.inconsistentMemberCount
   627	        << ";not_ready_member_count=" << summary.notReadyMemberCount
   628	        << ";failed_member_count=" << summary.failedMemberCount
   629	        << ";cancelled_member_count=" << summary.cancelledMemberCount
   630	        << ";context_changed_member_count="
   631	        << summary.contextChangedMemberCount
   632	        << ";metric_gap_member_count=" << summary.metricGapMemberCount
   633	        << ";comparable_member_count=" << summary.comparableMemberCount
   634	        << ";metric_evaluation_count=" << summary.metricEvaluationCount
   635	        << ";not_evaluable_metric_count="
   636	        << summary.notEvaluableMetricCount
   637	        << ";favorable_metric_count=" << summary.favorableMetricCount
   638	        << ";neutral_metric_count=" << summary.neutralMetricCount
   639	        << ";unfavorable_metric_count=" << summary.unfavorableMetricCount
   640	        << ";evidence_reasons=";
   641	    AppendReasons(out, summary.evidenceReasons);
   642	    out << ";interpretation_reasons=";
   643	    AppendReasons(out, summary.interpretationReasons);
   644	    out << ";follow_up_reasons=";
   645	    AppendReasons(out, summary.followUpReasons);
   646	    return out.str();
   647	}

   649	} // namespace
   650	
   651	void ValidateRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyEvidence(
   652	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyValidationView& view)
   653	{
   654	    if (view.policyContractVersion !=
   655	        kRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyContractVersion)
   656	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   657	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_version_unsupported");
   658	    RequireText(view.policyCanonicalText,
   659	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_identity_missing");
   660	    if (view.policyIdentityHash !=
   661	        RecommendationCanonicalHash(view.policyCanonicalText))
   662	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   663	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_identity_invalid");
   664	
   665	    if (view.assessmentContractVersion !=
   666	        kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion)
   667	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   668	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_assessment_version_unsupported");
   669	    RequireText(view.assessmentCanonicalText,
   670	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_assessment_identity_missing");
   671	    if (view.assessmentIdentityHash !=
   672	        RecommendationCanonicalHash(view.assessmentCanonicalText))
   673	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   674	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_assessment_identity_invalid");
   675	
   676	    RequireText(view.campaignIdentityCanonical,
   677	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_campaign_identity_missing");
   678	    if (view.campaignApprovalId <= 0 ||
   679	        view.campaignIdentityHash !=
   680	            RecommendationCanonicalHash(view.campaignIdentityCanonical))
   681	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   682	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_campaign_identity_invalid");
   683	
   684	    RequireText(view.materializationIdentityCanonical,
   685	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_materialization_identity_missing");
   686	    if (view.materializationId <= 0 ||
   687	        view.materializationCampaignApprovalId <= 0 ||
   688	        view.materializationContractVersion <= 0 ||
   689	        view.materializationIdentityHash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(
   690	            view.materializationIdentityCanonical))
   691	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   692	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_materialization_identity_invalid");
   693	    if (view.materializationCampaignApprovalId != view.campaignApprovalId ||
   694	        view.materializationCampaignIdentityHash !=
   695	            view.campaignIdentityHash)
   696	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   697	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_campaign_materialization_mismatch");
   698	
   699	    if (view.materializationMemberCount !=
   700	            static_cast<int>(view.members.size()) ||
   701	        view.assessmentSummaryMemberCount !=
   702	            static_cast<int>(view.members.size()) ||
   703	        view.members.empty() ||
   704	        view.members.size() > static_cast<std::size_t>(
   705	            kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers))
   706	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   707	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_assessment_shape_invalid");
   708	
   709	    std::set<long long> materializationMemberIds;
   710	    std::set<long long> rankingMemberIds;
   711	    std::set<long long> recommendationIds;
   712	    std::set<long long> proposalIds;
   713	    for (std::size_t index = 0; index < view.members.size(); ++index)
   714	    {
   715	        const auto& member = view.members[index];
   716	        if (member.memberOrdinal != static_cast<int>(index) + 1 ||
   717	            member.materializationMemberId <= 0 ||
   718	            member.rankingMemberId <= 0 || member.recommendationId <= 0 ||
   719	            member.sourceExperimentId <= 0 || member.proposalId <= 0 ||
   720	            (member.expectedExperimentId &&
   721	                *member.expectedExperimentId <= 0) ||
   722	            !materializationMemberIds
   723	                 .insert(member.materializationMemberId).second ||
   724	            !rankingMemberIds.insert(member.rankingMemberId).second ||
   725	            !recommendationIds.insert(member.recommendationId).second ||
   726	            !proposalIds.insert(member.proposalId).second)
   727	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   728	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_member_identity_set_invalid");
   729	        std::set<std::string> comparisonIdentities;
   730	        for (const auto& identity : member.comparisonMetricIdentities)
   731	        {
   732	            RequireText(identity,
   733	                "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_comparison_identity_invalid");
   734	            if (!comparisonIdentities.insert(identity).second)
   735	                throw std::invalid_argument(
   736	                    "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_comparison_identity_duplicate");
   737	        }
   738	    }
   739	}

   741	RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricRule::
   742	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricRule(
   743	        std::string metricIdentityValue,
   744	        MetricDirection directionValue)
   745	    : metricIdentity(std::move(metricIdentityValue)),
   746	      direction(directionValue)
   747	{
   748	    RequireText(metricIdentity,
   749	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_identity_invalid");

   751	}

   753	RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyIdentity::
   754	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyIdentity(
   755	        int contractVersionValue,
   756	        std::string canonicalTextValue,
   757	        std::string hashValue)
   758	    : contractVersion(contractVersionValue),
   759	      canonicalText(std::move(canonicalTextValue)),
   760	      hash(std::move(hashValue))
   761	{
   762	    if (contractVersion !=
   763	            kRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyContractVersion ||
   764	        hash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(canonicalText))
   765	        throw std::logic_error(
   766	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_identity_invariant_failed");
   767	}
