   769	RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy::RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy(
   770	    int minimumComparableMemberCountValue,
   771	    std::vector<MetricRule> requiredMetricRulesValue,
   772	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyIdentity identityValue)
   773	    : minimumComparableMemberCount(minimumComparableMemberCountValue),
   774	      requiredMetricRules(std::move(requiredMetricRulesValue)),
   775	      identity(std::move(identityValue))
   776	{
   777	    if (minimumComparableMemberCount <= 0 ||
   778	        minimumComparableMemberCount >
   779	            kMaximumRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMembers ||
   780	        requiredMetricRules.empty())
   781	        throw std::logic_error(
   782	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_invariant_failed");
   783	}
   784	
   785	RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricEvaluation::
   786	    RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyMetricEvaluation(
   787	        std::string metricIdentityValue,
   788	        MetricDirection directionValue,
   789	        std::optional<AssessmentClassification> evidenceClassificationValue,
   790	        std::optional<double> assessmentDeltaValue,
   791	        MetricJudgment judgmentValue)
   792	    : metricIdentity(std::move(metricIdentityValue)),
   793	      direction(directionValue),
   794	      evidenceClassification(evidenceClassificationValue),
   795	      assessmentDelta(assessmentDeltaValue && *assessmentDeltaValue == 0.0
   796	              ? std::optional<double>{0.0}
   797	              : assessmentDeltaValue),
   798	      judgment(judgmentValue)
   799	{
   800	    RequireText(metricIdentity,
   801	        "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_evaluation_identity_invalid");
   802	    RequireValidDirection(direction);
   803	    RequireValidMetricJudgment(judgment);
   804	    if (evidenceClassification)
   805	        (void)RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonClassificationText(
   806	            *evidenceClassification);
   807	    if (assessmentDelta && !std::isfinite(*assessmentDelta))
   808	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   809	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_delta_nonfinite");
   810	    const bool evaluable = judgment != MetricJudgment::NotEvaluable;
   811	    if (evaluable !=
   812	            (evidenceClassification ==
   813	                    AssessmentClassification::Comparable &&
   814	                assessmentDelta.has_value()))
   815	        throw std::logic_error(
   816	            "recommendation_campaign_outcome_policy_metric_evaluation_invariant_failed");
   817	}

  3792	long long ResolveAutoResumeModelId(pqxx::work& w,
  3793	                                          const SchedulerOptions& options)
  3794	{
  3795	    const std::vector<AutoResumeCandidate> candidates = LoadAutoResumeCandidates(w, options);
  3796	    if (candidates.empty())
  3797	    {
  3798	        std::cout << "QUEUE_RESUME_AUTO_NO_MATCH"
  3799	                  << ",symbol=" << *options.symbol
  3800	                  << ",prediction_horizon=" << *options.predictionHorizon
  3801	                  << ",threshold=" << FormatDouble(*options.cNextThreshold)
  3802	                  << ",target_epochs=" << *options.targetEpochs
  3803	                  << ",train_start=" << *options.trainStart
  3804	                  << ",train_end=" << *options.trainEnd
  3805	                  << std::endl;
  3806	        throw std::invalid_argument("QUEUE_RESUME_AUTO_NO_MATCH");
  3807	    }
  3808	
  3809	    const int bestCompletedEpochs = candidates.front().completedEpochs;
  3810	    int tiedBestCount = 0;
  3811	    for (const auto& candidate : candidates)
  3812	    {
  3813	        if (candidate.completedEpochs == bestCompletedEpochs)
  3814	            ++tiedBestCount;
  3815	    }
  3816	
  3817	    for (const auto& candidate : candidates)
  3818	    {
  3819	        std::cout << "QUEUE_RESUME_AUTO_CANDIDATE"
  3820	                  << ",model_id=" << candidate.modelId
  3821	                  << ",name=" << candidate.name
  3822	                  << ",completed_epochs=" << candidate.completedEpochs
  3823	                  << std::endl;
  3824	    }
  3825	
  3826	    if (tiedBestCount > 1)
  3827	    {
  3828	        std::cout << "QUEUE_RESUME_AUTO_AMBIGUOUS"
  3829	                  << ",best_completed_epochs=" << bestCompletedEpochs
  3830	                  << ",candidate_count=" << candidates.size()
  3831	                  << std::endl;
  3832	        throw std::invalid_argument("QUEUE_RESUME_AUTO_AMBIGUOUS");
  3833	    }
  3834	
  3835	    std::cout << "QUEUE_RESUME_AUTO_SELECTED"
  3836	              << ",resume_model_id=" << candidates.front().modelId
  3837	              << ",completed_epochs=" << candidates.front().completedEpochs
  3838	              << std::endl;
  3839	    return candidates.front().modelId;
  3840	}

  3858	std::optional<long long> FindLatestModelForExperiment(pqxx::work& w, long long experimentId)
  3859	{
  3860	    if (!ColumnExists(w, "model", "experiment_id"))
  3861	        return std::nullopt;
  3862	
  3863	    pqxx::result rows = w.exec_params(
  3864	        "WITH cfg AS ("
  3865	        "  SELECT model_id, max(value) FILTER (WHERE col_idx = 10) AS completed_epochs "
  3866	        "  FROM matrix "
  3867	        "  WHERE param_name = 'train_config_meta' AND row_idx = 0 "
  3868	        "  GROUP BY model_id"
  3869	        ") "
  3870	        "SELECT m.model_id "
  3871	        "FROM model m "
  3872	        "LEFT JOIN cfg ON cfg.model_id = m.model_id "
  3873	        "WHERE m.experiment_id = $1 "
  3874	        "ORDER BY cfg.completed_epochs DESC NULLS LAST, m.model_id DESC "
  3875	        "LIMIT 1;",
  3876	        experimentId);
  3877	    if (rows.empty())
  3878	        return std::nullopt;
  3879	    return rows[0][0].as<long long>();
  3880	}

  3882	std::optional<long long> FindLatestModelForExperimentSince(
  3883	    pqxx::work& w,
  3884	    long long experimentId,
  3885	    double attemptStartedEpoch)
  3886	{
  3887	    if (!ColumnExists(w, "model", "experiment_id"))

  3891	        "WITH cfg AS ("
  3892	        "  SELECT model_id, max(value) FILTER (WHERE col_idx = 10) AS completed_epochs "
  3893	        "  FROM matrix "
  3894	        "  WHERE param_name = 'train_config_meta' AND row_idx = 0 "
  3895	        "  GROUP BY model_id"
  3896	        ") "

  3898	        "FROM model m "
  3899	        "LEFT JOIN cfg ON cfg.model_id = m.model_id "
  3900	        "WHERE m.experiment_id = $1 "
  3901	        "AND m.created_at >= to_timestamp($2) "
  3902	        "ORDER BY cfg.completed_epochs DESC NULLS LAST, m.model_id DESC "
  3903	        "LIMIT 1;",
  3904	        experimentId,
  3905	        attemptStartedEpoch);
  3906	    if (rows.empty())

  3911	bool ModelWasCreatedForAttempt(pqxx::work& w,
  3912	                               long long modelId,
  3913	                               long long experimentId,
  3914	                               double attemptStartedEpoch)
  3915	{
  3916	    pqxx::result rows = w.exec_params(
  3917	        "SELECT 1 FROM model WHERE model_id = $1 AND experiment_id = $2 "
  3918	        "AND created_at >= to_timestamp($3) LIMIT 1;",
  3919	        modelId,
  3920	        experimentId,
  3921	        attemptStartedEpoch);
  3922	    return !rows.empty();
  3923	}

  3930	bool RequireSchedulerTables(pqxx::work& w)
  3931	{
  3932	    std::vector<std::string> missing;
  3933	    if (!TableExists(w, "experiment"))
  3934	        missing.push_back("experiment");
  3935	    if (!TableExists(w, "experiment_analysis_result"))
  3936	        missing.push_back("experiment_analysis_result");
  3937	    if (TableExists(w, "experiment") &&
  3938	        !ColumnExists(w, "experiment", "worker_started_at"))
  3939	        missing.push_back("experiment.worker_started_at");
  3940	
  3941	    if (missing.empty())

  3954	              << std::endl;
  3955	    return false;
  3956	}

   264	
   265	std::map<long long, SourceRecord> LoadSources(
   266	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
   267	    const std::vector<long long>& recommendationIds)
   268	{
   269	    if (recommendationIds.empty()) return {};
   270	    const auto [placeholders, parameters] =
   271	        IdPlaceholders(recommendationIds);
   272	    const pqxx::result rows = transaction.exec(
   273	        "SELECT recommendation_id,source_experiment_id,source_model_id,"
   274	        "source_analysis_id,source_symbol,source_prediction_horizon,"
   275	        "source_leader_score,source_infer_accuracy "
   276	        "FROM experiment_recommendation WHERE recommendation_id IN (" +
   277	            placeholders + ") ORDER BY recommendation_id;",
   278	        parameters);
   279	    std::map<long long, SourceRecord> result;
   280	    for (const auto& row : rows)
   281	    {
   282	        SourceRecord value;
   283	        value.recommendationId = row["recommendation_id"].as<long long>();
   284	        value.sourceExperimentId =
   285	            row["source_experiment_id"].as<long long>();
   286	        value.sourceModelId = OptionalValue<long long>(row, "source_model_id");
   287	        value.sourceAnalysisId =
   288	            OptionalValue<long long>(row, "source_analysis_id");
   289	        value.sourceSymbol = row["source_symbol"].as<std::string>();
   290	        value.sourcePredictionHorizon =
   291	            row["source_prediction_horizon"].as<int>();
   292	        value.leaderScore =
   293	            OptionalValue<double>(row, "source_leader_score");
   294	        value.inferenceAccuracy =
   295	            OptionalValue<double>(row, "source_infer_accuracy");
   296	        if (!result.emplace(value.recommendationId, std::move(value)).second)
   297	            throw std::runtime_error(
   298	                "campaign_outcome_assessment_duplicate_source_record");
   299	    }
   300	    return result;
   301	}
   302	
   303	std::map<long long, ExperimentRecord> LoadExperiments(
   304	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
   305	    const std::set<long long>& experimentIds)
   306	{
   307	    if (experimentIds.empty()) return {};
   308	    auto [placeholders, parameters] = IdPlaceholders(experimentIds);
   309	    const std::size_t timeZoneParameter = experimentIds.size() + 1;
   310	    parameters.append(kRecommendationDateTimeZone);
   311	    const std::string timeZone = "$" +
   312	        std::to_string(timeZoneParameter);
   313	    const pqxx::result rows = transaction.exec(
   314	        "SELECT experiment_id,symbol,prediction_horizon,c_next_threshold,"
   315	        "CASE WHEN infer_start IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE to_char(infer_start AT "
   316	        "TIME ZONE " + timeZone + ",'YYYY-MM-DD') END AS infer_start,"
   317	        "CASE WHEN infer_end IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE to_char(infer_end AT "
   318	        "TIME ZONE " + timeZone + ",'YYYY-MM-DD') END AS infer_end,"
   319	        "last_model_id FROM experiment WHERE experiment_id IN (" +
   320	        placeholders + ") ORDER BY experiment_id;",
   321	        parameters);
   322	    std::map<long long, ExperimentRecord> result;
   323	    for (const auto& row : rows)
   324	    {
   325	        ExperimentRecord value;
   326	        value.experimentId = row["experiment_id"].as<long long>();
   327	        value.symbol = row["symbol"].as<std::string>();
   328	        value.predictionHorizon =
   329	            row["prediction_horizon"].as<int>();
   330	        value.threshold = row["c_next_threshold"].as<double>();
   331	        value.inferenceRangeStart = OptionalText(row, "infer_start");
   332	        value.inferenceRangeEnd = OptionalText(row, "infer_end");
   333	        value.lastModelId = OptionalValue<long long>(row, "last_model_id");
   334	        if (!result.emplace(value.experimentId, std::move(value)).second)
   335	            throw std::runtime_error(
   336	                "campaign_outcome_assessment_duplicate_experiment_record");
   337	    }
   338	    return result;
   339	}
   340	
   341	std::map<long long, std::optional<long long>> LoadModels(
   342	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
   343	    const std::set<long long>& modelIds)
   344	{
   345	    if (modelIds.empty()) return {};
   346	    const auto [placeholders, parameters] = IdPlaceholders(modelIds);
   347	    const pqxx::result rows = transaction.exec(
   348	        "SELECT model_id,experiment_id FROM model WHERE model_id IN (" +
   349	            placeholders + ") ORDER BY model_id;",
   350	        parameters);
   351	    std::map<long long, std::optional<long long>> result;
   352	    for (const auto& row : rows)
   353	    {
   354	        const long long id = row["model_id"].as<long long>();
   355	        if (!result.emplace(
   356	                id, OptionalValue<long long>(row, "experiment_id")).second)
   357	            throw std::runtime_error(
   358	                "campaign_outcome_assessment_duplicate_model_record");
   359	    }
   360	    return result;
   361	}
   362	
   363	std::vector<InferenceRecord> LoadInferenceRecords(
   364	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
   365	    const std::set<long long>& modelIds)
   366	{
   367	    if (modelIds.empty()) return {};
   368	    const auto [placeholders, parameters] = IdPlaceholders(modelIds);
   369	    const pqxx::result rows = transaction.exec(
   370	        "SELECT id,model_id,symbol,prediction_horizon,threshold_logret,"
   371	        "window_size,label_rule_id,target_type,from_date,to_date,status "
   372	        "FROM inference_eval_result WHERE model_id IN (" + placeholders +
   373	        ") AND inference_scope='final' AND checkpoint_eval_id IS NULL "
   374	        "ORDER BY model_id,id;",
   375	        parameters);
   376	    std::vector<InferenceRecord> result;
   377	    result.reserve(rows.size());
   378	    for (const auto& row : rows)
   379	    {
   380	        InferenceRecord value;
   381	        value.id = row["id"].as<long long>();
   382	        value.modelId = row["model_id"].as<long long>();
   383	        value.symbol = row["symbol"].as<std::string>();
   384	        value.predictionHorizon =
   385	            row["prediction_horizon"].as<int>();
   386	        value.threshold = row["threshold_logret"].as<double>();
   387	        value.windowSize = row["window_size"].as<int>();
   388	        value.labelRuleId = row["label_rule_id"].as<int>();
   389	        value.targetType = row["target_type"].as<int>();
   390	        value.rangeStart = row["from_date"].as<std::string>();
   391	        value.rangeEnd = row["to_date"].as<std::string>();
   392	        value.status = row["status"].as<std::string>();
   393	        result.push_back(std::move(value));
   394	    }
   395	    return result;
   396	}
   397	
   398	std::vector<AnalysisRecord> LoadAnalysisRecords(
   399	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
   400	    const std::set<long long>& experimentIds)
   401	{
   402	    if (experimentIds.empty()) return {};
   403	    const auto [placeholders, parameters] = IdPlaceholders(experimentIds);
   404	    const pqxx::result rows = transaction.exec(
   405	        "SELECT analysis_id,experiment_id,model_id,analysis_status,"
   406	        "analysis_scope,infer_accuracy,leader_score "
   407	        "FROM experiment_analysis_result WHERE experiment_id IN (" +
   408	        placeholders + ") AND analysis_scope='final' ORDER BY "
   409	        "experiment_id,analysis_id;",
   410	        parameters);
   411	    std::vector<AnalysisRecord> result;
   412	    result.reserve(rows.size());
   413	    for (const auto& row : rows)
   414	    {
   415	        AnalysisRecord value;
   416	        value.analysisId = row["analysis_id"].as<long long>();
   417	        value.experimentId = row["experiment_id"].as<long long>();
   418	        value.modelId = OptionalValue<long long>(row, "model_id");
   419	        value.status = row["analysis_status"].as<std::string>();
   420	        value.scope = row["analysis_scope"].as<std::string>();
   421	        value.inferenceAccuracy =
   422	            OptionalValue<double>(row, "infer_accuracy");
   423	        value.leaderScore = OptionalValue<double>(row, "leader_score");
   424	        result.push_back(std::move(value));
   425	    }
   426	    return result;
   427	}
   428	
   429	struct InferenceSelection
   430	{
   429	struct InferenceSelection
   430	{
   431	    std::string symbol;
   432	    int predictionHorizon = 0;
   433	    double threshold = 0.0;
   434	    std::optional<std::string> rangeStart;
   435	    std::optional<std::string> rangeEnd;
   436	};
   437	
   438	InferenceSelection SelectionFrom(const ExperimentRecord& experiment)
   439	{
   440	    return {experiment.symbol, experiment.predictionHorizon,
   441	        experiment.threshold, experiment.inferenceRangeStart,
   442	        experiment.inferenceRangeEnd};
   443	}
   444	
   445	InferenceSelection SelectionFrom(
   446	    const ExperimentInvocationConfiguration& invocation)
   447	{
   448	    return {invocation.configuration.symbol,
   449	        invocation.configuration.predictionHorizon,
   450	        invocation.configuration.labelThreshold,
   451	        invocation.configuration.inferStartDate,
   452	        invocation.configuration.inferEndDate};
   453	}
   454	
   455	std::vector<const InferenceRecord*> ExactInferenceRecords(
   456	    const std::vector<InferenceRecord>& records,
   457	    const InferenceSelection& selection,
   458	    long long modelId,
   459	    bool completedOnly)
   460	{
   461	    std::vector<const InferenceRecord*> exact;
   462	    if (!selection.rangeStart || !selection.rangeEnd)
   463	        return exact;
   464	    for (const auto& record : records)
   465	    {
   466	        if (record.modelId == modelId &&
   467	            record.symbol == selection.symbol &&
   468	            record.predictionHorizon == selection.predictionHorizon &&
   469	            record.threshold == selection.threshold &&
   470	            record.rangeStart == *selection.rangeStart &&
   471	            record.rangeEnd == *selection.rangeEnd &&
   472	            (!completedOnly || record.status == "completed"))
   473	            exact.push_back(&record);
   474	    }
   475	    return exact;
   476	}
   477	
   478	std::vector<const AnalysisRecord*> ExactAnalysisRecords(
   479	    const std::vector<AnalysisRecord>& records,
   480	    long long experimentId,
   481	    long long modelId)
   482	{
   483	    std::vector<const AnalysisRecord*> exact;
   484	    for (const auto& record : records)
   485	        if (record.experimentId == experimentId &&
   486	            record.modelId == std::optional<long long>{modelId} &&
   487	            record.scope == "final")
   488	            exact.push_back(&record);
   489	    return exact;
   490	}
   491	
   492	std::optional<Context> ContextFrom(
   493	    const InferenceRecord& record,
   494	    Consistency& consistency)
   495	{
   496	    const std::string labelDefinition =
   497	        "inference_eval_result_label_v1;label_rule_id=" +
   498	        std::to_string(record.labelRuleId) +
   499	        ";target_type=" + std::to_string(record.targetType);
   500	    if (record.symbol.empty() || record.predictionHorizon <= 0 ||
   501	        record.windowSize <= 0 || !std::isfinite(record.threshold) ||
   502	        record.rangeStart.empty() || record.rangeEnd.empty())
   503	    {
   504	        consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   505	        return std::nullopt;
   506	    }
   507	    try
   508	    {
   509	        return Context(EA::CanonicalSymbol::Normalize(record.symbol),
   510	            record.predictionHorizon, record.threshold, record.windowSize,
   511	            labelDefinition, CanonicalExperimentDateText(record.rangeStart),
   512	            CanonicalExperimentDateText(record.rangeEnd));
   513	    }
   514	    catch (const std::exception&)
   515	    {
   516	        consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   517	        return std::nullopt;
   518	    }
   519	}
   520	
   521	std::optional<double> FiniteMetric(
   522	    std::optional<double> value,
   523	    Consistency& consistency)
   524	{
   525	    if (value && !std::isfinite(*value))
   526	    {
   527	        consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   528	        return std::nullopt;
   529	    }
   530	    return value;
   531	}
   532	
   533	std::vector<Metric> Metrics(
   534	    std::optional<double> inferenceAccuracy,
   535	    std::optional<double> leaderScore,
   536	    bool identitiesPresent,
   537	    Consistency& consistency)
   538	{
   539	    if (!identitiesPresent) return {};
   540	    return {
   541	        Metric("inference_accuracy",
   542	            FiniteMetric(inferenceAccuracy, consistency),
   543	            MetricSupport::NumericDelta),
   544	        Metric("leader_score", FiniteMetric(leaderScore, consistency),
   545	            MetricSupport::NumericDelta)};
   546	}
   547	
   548	std::optional<SourceEvidence> BuildSourceEvidence(
   549	    const RecommendationCampaignStatusMember& member,
   550	    const std::map<long long, SourceRecord>& sources,
   551	    const std::map<long long,
   552	        const PersistedRecommendationConversionProposal*>& proposals,
   553	    const std::map<long long, std::optional<long long>>& models,
   554	    const std::vector<InferenceRecord>& inferenceRecords,
   555	    const std::vector<AnalysisRecord>& analysisRecords,
   556	    Consistency& consistency)
   557	{
   558	    const auto source = sources.find(member.recommendationId);
   559	    const auto proposal = proposals.find(member.proposalId);
   560	    if (source == sources.end() || proposal == proposals.end() ||
   561	        source->second.sourceExperimentId != member.sourceExperimentId)
   562	    {
   563	        consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   564	        return std::nullopt;
   565	    }
   566	
   567	    ExperimentInvocationConfiguration sourceInvocation;
   568	    try
   569	    {
   570	        sourceInvocation = ParseSourceInvocation(
   571	            proposal->second->proposal.sourceInvocationCanonical);
   572	    }
   573	    catch (const std::exception&)
   574	    {
   575	        consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   576	        return std::nullopt;
   577	    }
   578	    try
   579	    {
   580	        if (EA::CanonicalSymbol::Normalize(source->second.sourceSymbol) !=
   581	                sourceInvocation.configuration.symbol ||
   582	            source->second.sourcePredictionHorizon !=
   583	                sourceInvocation.configuration.predictionHorizon)
   584	            consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   585	    }
   586	    catch (const std::exception&)
   587	    {
   588	        consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   589	    }
   590	    if (proposal->second->proposal.recommendationId !=
   591	            member.recommendationId ||
   592	        proposal->second->proposal.sourceExperimentId !=
   593	            member.sourceExperimentId)
   594	    {
   595	        consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   596	        return std::nullopt;
   597	    }
   598	
   599	    if (!source->second.sourceModelId)
   600	    {
   601	        if (source->second.sourceAnalysisId)
   602	            consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   603	        return std::nullopt;
   604	    }
   605	    if (*source->second.sourceModelId <= 0)
   606	    {
   607	        consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   608	        return std::nullopt;
   609	    }
   610	
   611	    const long long modelId = *source->second.sourceModelId;
   612	    const auto model = models.find(modelId);
   613	    if (model == models.end() || !model->second ||
   614	        model->second != std::optional<long long>{member.sourceExperimentId})
   615	        consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   616	
   617	    const auto exactInference = ExactInferenceRecords(
   618	        inferenceRecords, SelectionFrom(sourceInvocation), modelId, true);
   619	    if (exactInference.size() != 1)
   620	    {
   621	        if (exactInference.size() > 1)
   622	            consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   623	        return std::nullopt;
   624	    }
   625	    if (exactInference.front()->id <= 0)
   626	    {
   627	        consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   628	        return std::nullopt;
   629	    }
   630	    const auto context = ContextFrom(*exactInference.front(), consistency);
   631	    if (!context) return std::nullopt;
   632	
   633	    bool analysisIdentityPresent = false;
   634	    std::optional<long long> sourceAnalysisId =
   635	        source->second.sourceAnalysisId;
   636	    if (sourceAnalysisId && *sourceAnalysisId <= 0)
   637	    {
   638	        consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   639	        sourceAnalysisId.reset();
   640	    }
   641	    if (sourceAnalysisId)
   642	    {
   643	        const auto found = std::find_if(analysisRecords.begin(),
   644	            analysisRecords.end(), [&](const AnalysisRecord& record)
   645	        {
   646	            return record.analysisId == *sourceAnalysisId;
   647	        });
   648	        if (found != analysisRecords.end())
   649	        {
   650	            analysisIdentityPresent = found->experimentId ==
   651	                    member.sourceExperimentId &&
   652	                found->modelId == source->second.sourceModelId &&
   653	                found->scope == "final" && found->status == "completed";
   654	            if (!analysisIdentityPresent)
   655	                consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   656	        }
   657	        else
   658	            consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   659	    }
   660	
   661	    const bool metricIdentityPresent =
   662	        analysisIdentityPresent || sourceAnalysisId.has_value();
   663	    return SourceEvidence(member.sourceExperimentId,
   664	        source->second.sourceModelId, sourceAnalysisId, *context,
   665	        MetricCollection(Metrics(source->second.inferenceAccuracy,
   666	            source->second.leaderScore, metricIdentityPresent,
   667	            consistency)));
   668	}
   669	
   670	std::optional<ResultEvidence> BuildResultEvidence(
   671	    const RecommendationCampaignStatusMember& member,
   672	    const std::map<long long, ExperimentRecord>& experiments,
   673	    const std::map<long long, std::optional<long long>>& models,
   674	    const std::vector<InferenceRecord>& inferenceRecords,
   675	    const std::vector<AnalysisRecord>& analysisRecords,
   676	    Consistency& consistency)
   677	{
   678	    std::vector<ResultIdentity> identities;
   679	    std::optional<Context> context;
   680	    std::vector<Metric> metrics;
   681	
   682	    if (member.experimentId && member.modelId)
   683	    {
   684	        const auto experiment = experiments.find(*member.experimentId);
   685	        const auto model = models.find(*member.modelId);
   686	        if (model == models.end() || !model->second ||
   687	            model->second != member.experimentId)
   688	            consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   689	        if (experiment != experiments.end())
   690	        {
   691	            const auto exactInference = ExactInferenceRecords(
   692	                inferenceRecords, SelectionFrom(experiment->second),
   693	                *member.modelId, false);
   694	            for (const auto* record : exactInference)
   695	                if (record->id > 0)
   696	                    identities.emplace_back(
   697	                        "inference_eval_result", record->id);
   698	                else
   699	                    consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   700	            if (exactInference.size() == 1)
   701	            {
   702	                const auto mapped = ContextFrom(
   703	                    *exactInference.front(), consistency);
   704	                if (mapped) context.emplace(*mapped);
   705	            }
   706	            else if (exactInference.size() > 1)
   707	                consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   708	
   709	            const auto exactAnalysis = ExactAnalysisRecords(analysisRecords,
   710	                *member.experimentId, *member.modelId);
   711	            for (const auto* record : exactAnalysis)
   712	                if (record->analysisId > 0)
   713	                    identities.emplace_back(
   714	                        "experiment_analysis_result", record->analysisId);
   715	                else
   716	                    consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   717	            if (exactAnalysis.size() == 1)
   718	                metrics = Metrics(exactAnalysis.front()->inferenceAccuracy,
   719	                    exactAnalysis.front()->leaderScore, true, consistency);
   720	            else if (exactAnalysis.size() > 1)
   721	                consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   722	        }
   723	    }
   724	
   725	    const bool succeeded = member.terminalResult ==
   726	        RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::succeeded;
   727	    if (!succeeded && identities.empty()) return std::nullopt;
   728	    return ResultEvidence(member.experimentId, member.modelId,
   729	        std::move(identities), std::move(context),
   730	        MetricCollection(std::move(metrics)));
   731	}
   732	
   733	std::vector<ScientificMember> LoadScientificMembers(
   734	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
   735	    const RecommendationCampaignStatusSnapshot& status)
   736	{
   737	    std::vector<long long> recommendationIds;
   738	    std::vector<long long> proposalIds;
   739	    std::set<long long> experimentIds;
   740	    std::set<long long> modelIds;
   741	    recommendationIds.reserve(status.members.size());
   742	    proposalIds.reserve(status.members.size());
   743	    for (const auto& member : status.members)
   744	    {
   745	        recommendationIds.push_back(member.recommendationId);
   746	        proposalIds.push_back(member.proposalId);
   747	        experimentIds.insert(member.sourceExperimentId);
   748	        if (member.experimentId) experimentIds.insert(*member.experimentId);
   749	    }
   750	    const auto sources = LoadSources(transaction, recommendationIds);
   751	    const auto proposalValues =
   752	        ListRecommendationConversionProposalsByIds(transaction, proposalIds);
   753	    std::map<long long, const PersistedRecommendationConversionProposal*>
   754	        proposals;
   755	    for (const auto& proposal : proposalValues)
   756	        if (!proposals.emplace(proposal.proposalId, &proposal).second)
   757	            throw std::runtime_error(
   758	                "campaign_outcome_assessment_duplicate_proposal_record");
   759	    for (const auto& [unused, source] : sources)
   760	    {
   761	        static_cast<void>(unused);
   762	        if (source.sourceModelId) modelIds.insert(*source.sourceModelId);
   763	    }
   764	    for (const auto& member : status.members)
   765	        if (member.modelId) modelIds.insert(*member.modelId);
   766	
   767	    const auto experiments = LoadExperiments(transaction, experimentIds);
   768	    const auto models = LoadModels(transaction, modelIds);
   769	    const auto inferenceRecords =
   770	        LoadInferenceRecords(transaction, modelIds);
   771	    const auto analysisRecords =
   772	        LoadAnalysisRecords(transaction, experimentIds);
   773	
   774	    std::vector<ScientificMember> result;
   775	    result.reserve(status.members.size());
   776	    for (const auto& member : status.members)
   777	    {
   778	        Consistency consistency = Consistency::Consistent;
   779	        auto sourceEvidence = BuildSourceEvidence(member, sources, proposals,
   780	            models, inferenceRecords, analysisRecords, consistency);
   781	        auto resultEvidence = BuildResultEvidence(member, experiments,
   782	            models, inferenceRecords, analysisRecords,
   783	            consistency);
   784	        result.push_back({member.memberOrdinal, consistency,
   785	            std::move(sourceEvidence), std::move(resultEvidence)});
   786	    }
   787	    return result;
   788	}
   789	
   790	} // namespace
   791	
   792	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest
   790	} // namespace
   791	
   792	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest
   793	NormalizeRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest(
   794	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest& request)
   795	{
   796	    if (request.materializationId <= 0)
   797	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   798	            "campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_id_invalid");
   799	    return request;
   800	}
   801	
   802	bool RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSchemasExist(
   803	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction)
   804	{
   805	    if (!RecommendationCampaignStatusSchemasExist(transaction) ||
   806	        !RecommendationCampaignApprovalSchemaExists(transaction))
   807	        return false;
   808	    return transaction.exec(R"SQL(
   809	SELECT NOT EXISTS (
   810	  SELECT 1 FROM (VALUES
   811	    ('experiment_recommendation','recommendation_id'),
   812	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_experiment_id'),
   813	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_model_id'),
   814	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_analysis_id'),
   815	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_symbol'),
   816	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_prediction_horizon'),
   817	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_leader_score'),
   818	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_infer_accuracy'),
   819	    ('experiment','c_next_threshold'),('experiment','infer_start'),
   820	    ('experiment','infer_end'),('experiment','last_model_id'),
   821	    ('model','model_id'),('model','experiment_id'),
   822	    ('inference_eval_result','id'),('inference_eval_result','model_id'),
   823	    ('inference_eval_result','symbol'),
   824	    ('inference_eval_result','prediction_horizon'),
   825	    ('inference_eval_result','threshold_logret'),
   826	    ('inference_eval_result','window_size'),
   827	    ('inference_eval_result','label_rule_id'),
   828	    ('inference_eval_result','target_type'),
   829	    ('inference_eval_result','from_date'),
   830	    ('inference_eval_result','to_date'),
   831	    ('inference_eval_result','status'),
   832	    ('inference_eval_result','inference_scope'),
   833	    ('inference_eval_result','checkpoint_eval_id'),
   834	    ('experiment_analysis_result','analysis_id'),
   835	    ('experiment_analysis_result','experiment_id'),
   836	    ('experiment_analysis_result','model_id'),
   837	    ('experiment_analysis_result','analysis_status'),
   838	    ('experiment_analysis_result','analysis_scope'),
   839	    ('experiment_analysis_result','infer_accuracy'),
   840	    ('experiment_analysis_result','leader_score')
   841	  ) required(table_name,column_name)
   842	  WHERE NOT EXISTS (
   843	    SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns c
   844	    WHERE c.table_schema=current_schema()
   845	      AND c.table_name=required.table_name
   846	      AND c.column_name=required.column_name));
   847	)SQL").one_row()[0].as<bool>();
   848	}
   849	
   850	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidenceSnapshot
   851	LoadRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidence(
   852	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
   853	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest& request)
   854	{
   855	    const auto normalized =
   856	        NormalizeRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest(request);
   857	    if (!RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSchemasExist(transaction))
   858	        throw std::runtime_error(
   859	            "campaign_outcome_assessment_schemas_required");
   860	
   861	    const auto materialization = FindRecommendationCampaignMaterialization(
   862	        transaction, normalized.materializationId);
   863	    if (!materialization)
   864	        throw std::runtime_error(
   865	            "campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_not_found");
   866	    const auto approval = FindRecommendationCampaignApproval(
   867	        transaction, materialization->campaignApprovalId);
   868	    if (!approval)
   869	        throw std::runtime_error(
   870	            "campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_approval_not_found");
   871	    if (approval->evidence.approvalIdentityHash !=
   872	            materialization->approvalIdentityHash)
   873	        throw std::runtime_error(
   874	            "campaign_outcome_assessment_approval_materialization_mismatch");
   875	
   876	    auto status = LoadRecommendationCampaignStatusSnapshot(
   877	        transaction, {normalized.materializationId});
   878	    auto scientific = LoadScientificMembers(transaction, status);
   879	    return {
   880	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity(
   881	            approval->campaignApprovalId,
   882	            approval->evidence.approvalIdentityCanonical,
   883	            approval->evidence.approvalIdentityHash),
   884	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity(
   885	            materialization->materializationId,
   886	            materialization->campaignApprovalId,
   887	            materialization->approvalIdentityHash,
   888	            materialization->contractVersion,
   889	            materialization->selectedMemberCount,
   890	            materialization->identityCanonical,
   891	            materialization->identityHash),
   892	        std::move(status), std::move(scientific)};
   893	}
   894	
   895	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidenceSnapshot
   896	ReadRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidence(
   897	    pqxx::connection& connection,
   898	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest& request)
   899	{
   900	    pqxx::read_transaction transaction{connection};
   901	    transaction.exec(
   902	        "SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ, READ ONLY;");
   903	    return LoadRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidence(
   904	        transaction, request);
   905	}

    30	class StreamStateGuard
    31	{
    32	public:
    33	    explicit StreamStateGuard(std::ostream& stream)
    34	        : stream_(stream), flags_(stream.flags()),
    35	          precision_(stream.precision()), locale_(stream.getloc()) {}
    36	
    37	    ~StreamStateGuard()
    38	    {
    39	        try
    40	        {
    41	            stream_.flags(flags_);
    42	            stream_.precision(precision_);
    43	            stream_.imbue(locale_);
    44	        }
    45	        catch (...)
    46	        {
    47	        }
    48	    }
    49	
    50	private:
    51	    std::ostream& stream_;
    52	    const std::ios_base::fmtflags flags_;
    53	    const std::streamsize precision_;
    54	    const std::locale locale_;
    55	};
    56	
    57	template <typename Value>
    58	void PrintOptional(std::ostream& output, const std::optional<Value>& value)
    59	{
    60	    if (value) output << *value;
    61	    else output << "null";
    62	}
    63	
    64	void PrintOptionalNumber(
    65	    std::ostream& output,
    66	    const std::optional<double>& value)
    67	{
    68	    if (value) output << CanonicalRecommendationDouble(*value);
    69	    else output << "null";
    70	}
    71	
    72	void PrintSafety(std::ostream& output)
    73	{
    74	    output << "transaction_read_only=true,snapshot_isolation=repeatable_read,"
    75	              "advisory_locks_acquired=0,row_locks_acquired=0,rows_inserted=0,"
    76	              "rows_updated=0,rows_deleted=0,sequences_advanced=0,"
    77	              "scheduler_started=false,scheduler_signaled=false,"
    78	              "scheduler_polled=false,workers_launched=false,"
    79	              "automatic_follow_up=false,campaign_state_row_created=false,"
    80	              "assessment_persisted=false,assessment_advisory=true,"
    81	              "campaign_success_declared=false,follow_up_authorized=false";
    82	}
    83	
    84	Consistency InputConsistency(
    85	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMember& member)
    86	{
    87	    return std::find(member.diagnostics.begin(), member.diagnostics.end(),
    88	               RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentDiagnosticCode::
    89	                   InputEvidenceInconsistent) == member.diagnostics.end()
    90	        ? Consistency::Consistent
    91	        : Consistency::Inconsistent;
    92	}
    93	
    94	void PrintDiagnostics(
    95	    std::ostream& output,
    96	    const std::vector<
    97	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentDiagnosticCode>& diagnostics)
    98	{
    99	    if (diagnostics.empty())
   100	    {
   101	        output << "none";
   102	        return;
   103	    }
   104	    for (std::size_t index = 0; index < diagnostics.size(); ++index)
   105	    {
   106	        if (index != 0) output << '|';
   107	        output << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentDiagnosticCodeText(
   108	            diagnostics[index]);
   109	    }
   110	}
   111	
   112	void PrintContext(
   113	    std::ostream& output,
   114	    const Context* context,
   115	    const char* prefix)
   116	{
   117	    output << ',' << prefix << "_symbol=";
   118	    if (context) output << RecommendationMachineText(context->symbol);
   119	    else output << "null";
   120	    output << ',' << prefix << "_prediction_horizon=";
   121	    if (context) output << context->predictionHorizon;
   122	    else output << "null";
   123	    output << ',' << prefix << "_threshold=";
   124	    if (context) output << CanonicalRecommendationDouble(context->threshold);
   125	    else output << "null";
   126	    output << ',' << prefix << "_window_size=";
   127	    if (context) output << context->windowSize;
   128	    else output << "null";
   129	    output << ',' << prefix << "_label_definition=";
   130	    if (context)
   131	        output << RecommendationMachineText(context->labelDefinition);
   132	    else output << "null";
   133	    output << ',' << prefix << "_inference_range_start=";
   134	    if (context)
   135	        output << RecommendationMachineText(context->inferenceRangeStart);
   136	    else output << "null";
   137	    output << ',' << prefix << "_inference_range_end=";
   138	    if (context)
   139	        output << RecommendationMachineText(context->inferenceRangeEnd);
   140	    else output << "null";
   141	}
   142	
   143	std::optional<double> MetricValue(
   144	    const MetricCollection* collection,
   145	    const std::string& identity)
   146	{
   147	    if (!collection) return std::nullopt;
   148	    const auto found = std::find_if(collection->metrics.begin(),
   149	        collection->metrics.end(), [&](const auto& metric)
   150	    {
   151	        return metric.identity == identity;
   152	    });
   153	    return found == collection->metrics.end()
   154	        ? std::nullopt
   155	        : found->value;
   156	}
   157	
   158	void PrintMetrics(
   159	    std::ostream& output,
   160	    const MetricCollection* metrics)
   161	{
   162	    if (!metrics || metrics->metrics.empty())
   163	    {
   164	        output << "none";
   165	        return;
   166	    }
   167	    for (std::size_t index = 0; index < metrics->metrics.size(); ++index)
   168	    {
   169	        const auto& metric = metrics->metrics[index];
   170	        if (index != 0) output << '|';
   171	        output << RecommendationMachineText(metric.identity) << ':';
   172	        PrintOptionalNumber(output, metric.value);
   173	        output << ':'
   174	               << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonSupportText(
   175	                      metric.comparisonSupport);
   176	    }
   177	}
   178	
   179	void PrintResultIdentities(
   180	    std::ostream& output,
   181	    const std::vector<
   182	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentResultIdentity>* identities)
   183	{
   184	    if (!identities || identities->empty())
   185	    {
   186	        output << "none";
   187	        return;
   188	    }
   189	    for (std::size_t index = 0; index < identities->size(); ++index)
   190	    {
   191	        if (index != 0) output << '|';
   192	        output << RecommendationMachineText((*identities)[index].kind)
   193	               << ':' << (*identities)[index].id;
   194	    }
   195	}
   196	
   197	void PrintComparisons(
   198	    std::ostream& output,
   199	    const std::vector<
   200	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparison>& comparisons)
   201	{
   202	    if (comparisons.empty())
   203	    {
   204	        output << "none";
   205	        return;
   206	    }
   207	    for (std::size_t index = 0; index < comparisons.size(); ++index)
   208	    {
   209	        const auto& comparison = comparisons[index];
   210	        if (index != 0) output << '|';
   211	        output << RecommendationMachineText(comparison.metricIdentity) << ':'
   212	               << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMetricComparisonClassificationText(
   213	                      comparison.classification)
   214	               << ':';
   215	        PrintOptionalNumber(output, comparison.sourceValue);
   216	        output << ':';
   217	        PrintOptionalNumber(output, comparison.resultValue);
   218	        output << ':';
   219	        PrintOptionalNumber(output, comparison.delta);
   220	        output << ':';
   221	        if (comparison.contextDifferences.empty()) output << "none";
   222	        else
   223	        {
   224	            for (std::size_t difference = 0;
   225	                 difference < comparison.contextDifferences.size();
   226	                 ++difference)
   227	            {
   228	                if (difference != 0) output << '+';
   229	                output << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContextDifferenceText(
   230	                    comparison.contextDifferences[difference]);
   231	            }
   232	        }
   233	    }
   234	}
   235	
   236	void PrintAssessment(std::ostream& output, const Assessment& assessment)
   237	{
   238	    const auto& summary = assessment.summary;
   239	    output << "RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_OUTCOME_ASSESSMENT"
   240	           << ",campaign_approval_id="
   241	           << assessment.campaignIdentity.campaignApprovalId
   242	           << ",campaign_approval_contract_version="
   243	           << kRecommendationCampaignApprovalContractVersion
   244	           << ",campaign_approval_identity_hash="
   245	           << RecommendationMachineText(
   246	                  assessment.campaignIdentity.identityHash)
   247	           << ",materialization_id="
   248	           << assessment.materializationIdentity.materializationId
   249	           << ",materialization_contract_version="
   250	           << assessment.materializationIdentity.contractVersion
   251	           << ",materialization_campaign_approval_identity_hash="
   252	           << RecommendationMachineText(
   253	                  assessment.materializationIdentity.campaignIdentityHash)
   254	           << ",materialization_identity_hash="
   255	           << RecommendationMachineText(
   256	                  assessment.materializationIdentity.identityHash)
   257	           << ",campaign_status_contract_version="
   258	           << kRecommendationCampaignStatusContractVersion
   259	           << ",assessment_contract_version="
   260	           << assessment.identity.contractVersion
   261	           << ",assessment_identity_hash="
   262	           << RecommendationMachineText(assessment.identity.hash)
   263	           << ",observed_at="
   264	           << RecommendationMachineText(assessment.observedAt)
   265	           << ",aggregate_outcome="
   266	           << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentAggregateOutcomeText(
   267	                  summary.outcome)
   268	           << ",member_count=" << summary.memberCount
   269	           << ",lifecycle_not_terminal_count="
   270	           << summary.notTerminalLifecycleCount
   271	           << ",lifecycle_succeeded_count="
   272	           << summary.succeededLifecycleCount
   273	           << ",lifecycle_failed_count=" << summary.failedLifecycleCount
   274	           << ",lifecycle_cancelled_count="
   275	           << summary.cancelledLifecycleCount
   276	           << ",lifecycle_unknown_count=" << summary.unknownLifecycleCount
   277	           << ",consistent_member_count=" << summary.consistentMemberCount
   278	           << ",inconsistent_member_count="
   279	           << summary.inconsistentMemberCount
   280	           << ",not_ready_member_count=" << summary.notReadyMemberCount
   281	           << ",succeeded_comparable_member_count="
   282	           << summary.succeededComparableMemberCount
   283	           << ",succeeded_context_changed_member_count="
   284	           << summary.succeededContextChangedMemberCount
   285	           << ",succeeded_metric_gap_member_count="
   286	           << summary.succeededMetricGapMemberCount
   287	           << ",terminal_failed_member_count="
   288	           << summary.terminalFailedMemberCount
   289	           << ",terminal_cancelled_member_count="
   290	           << summary.terminalCancelledMemberCount
   291	           << ",inconsistent_outcome_member_count="
   292	           << summary.inconsistentOutcomeMemberCount
   293	           << ",metric_comparison_count=" << summary.metricComparisonCount
   294	           << ",comparable_metric_count=" << summary.comparableMetricCount
   295	           << ",context_changed_metric_count="
   296	           << summary.contextChangedMetricCount
   297	           << ",missing_source_metric_count="
   298	           << summary.missingSourceMetricCount
   299	           << ",missing_result_metric_count="
   300	           << summary.missingResultMetricCount
   301	           << ",metric_value_unavailable_count="
   302	           << summary.metricValueUnavailableCount
   303	           << ",unsupported_metric_count=" << summary.unsupportedMetricCount
   304	           << ",positive_delta_count=" << summary.positiveDeltaCount
   305	           << ",zero_delta_count=" << summary.zeroDeltaCount
   306	           << ",negative_delta_count=" << summary.negativeDeltaCount << ',';
   307	    PrintSafety(output);
   308	    output << '\n';
   309	
   310	    for (const auto& member : assessment.members)
   311	    {
   312	        const auto* source = member.sourceEvidence
   313	            ? &*member.sourceEvidence : nullptr;
   314	        const auto* result = member.resultEvidence
   315	            ? &*member.resultEvidence : nullptr;
   316	        output << "RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_OUTCOME_ASSESSMENT_MEMBER"
   317	               << ",materialization_id="
   318	               << assessment.materializationIdentity.materializationId
   319	               << ",member_ordinal=" << member.identity.memberOrdinal
   320	               << ",materialization_member_id="
   321	               << member.identity.materializationMemberId
   322	               << ",ranking_member_id=" << member.identity.rankingMemberId
   323	               << ",recommendation_id=" << member.identity.recommendationId
   324	               << ",source_experiment_id="
   325	               << member.identity.sourceExperimentId
   326	               << ",proposal_id=" << member.identity.proposalId
   327	               << ",expected_experiment_id=";
   328	        PrintOptional(output, member.identity.expectedExperimentId);
   329	        output << ",lifecycle="
   330	               << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleStateText(
   331	                      member.lifecycle)
   332	               << ",input_consistency="
   333	               << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentConsistencyStateText(
   334	                      InputConsistency(member))
   335	               << ",final_consistency="
   336	               << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentConsistencyStateText(
   337	                      member.consistency)
   338	               << ",outcome="
   339	               << RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberOutcomeText(
   340	                      member.outcome)
   341	               << ",diagnostic=";
   342	        PrintDiagnostics(output, member.diagnostics);
   343	        output << ",source_evidence_present=" << (source ? "true" : "false")
   344	               << ",source_model_id=";
   345	        if (source) PrintOptional(output, source->sourceModelId);
   346	        else output << "null";
   347	        output << ",source_analysis_id=";
   348	        if (source) PrintOptional(output, source->sourceAnalysisId);
   349	        else output << "null";
   350	        PrintContext(output, source ? &source->context : nullptr, "source");
   351	        output << ",source_inference_accuracy=";
   352	        PrintOptionalNumber(output, MetricValue(
   353	            source ? &source->metrics : nullptr, "inference_accuracy"));
   354	        output << ",source_leader_score=";
   355	        PrintOptionalNumber(output, MetricValue(
   356	            source ? &source->metrics : nullptr, "leader_score"));
   357	        output << ",source_metrics=";
   358	        PrintMetrics(output, source ? &source->metrics : nullptr);
   359	        output << ",result_evidence_present=" << (result ? "true" : "false")
   360	               << ",result_experiment_id=";
   361	        if (result) PrintOptional(output, result->experimentId);
   362	        else output << "null";
   363	        output << ",result_model_id=";
   364	        if (result) PrintOptional(output, result->modelId);
   365	        else output << "null";
   366	        output << ",result_identity_count="
   367	               << (result ? result->resultIdentities.size() : 0)
   368	               << ",result_identities=";
   369	        PrintResultIdentities(output,
   370	            result ? &result->resultIdentities : nullptr);
   371	        output << ",result_context_present="
   372	               << (result && result->context ? "true" : "false");
   373	        PrintContext(output,
   374	            result && result->context ? &*result->context : nullptr,
   375	            "result");
   376	        output << ",result_inference_accuracy=";
   377	        PrintOptionalNumber(output, MetricValue(
   378	            result ? &result->metrics : nullptr, "inference_accuracy"));
   379	        output << ",result_leader_score=";
   380	        PrintOptionalNumber(output, MetricValue(
   381	            result ? &result->metrics : nullptr, "leader_score"));
   382	        output << ",result_metrics=";
   383	        PrintMetrics(output, result ? &result->metrics : nullptr);
   384	        output << ",comparison_count=" << member.comparisons.size()
   385	               << ",comparisons=";
   386	        PrintComparisons(output, member.comparisons);
   387	        output << ',';
   388	        PrintSafety(output);
   389	        output << '\n';
   390	    }
   391	}
   392	
   393	} // namespace
   394	
   395	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleState
   396	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleFromCampaignStatus(
   397	    RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult terminalResult)
   398	{
   399	    switch (terminalResult)
   400	    {
   401	        case RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::notTerminal:
   402	            return Lifecycle::NotTerminal;
   403	        case RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::succeeded:
   404	            return Lifecycle::Succeeded;
   405	        case RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::failed:
   406	            return Lifecycle::Failed;
   407	        case RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::cancelled:
   408	            return Lifecycle::Cancelled;
   409	        case RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::unknown:
   410	            return Lifecycle::Unknown;
   411	    }
   412	    throw std::invalid_argument(
   413	        "campaign_outcome_assessment_status_lifecycle_invalid");
   414	}
   415	
   416	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment
   417	BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentFromEvidence(
   418	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidenceSnapshot& evidence)
   419	{
   420	    if (evidence.statusSnapshot.request.materializationId !=
   421	            evidence.materializationIdentity.materializationId ||
   422	        evidence.statusSnapshot.materializationContractVersion !=
   423	            evidence.materializationIdentity.contractVersion ||
   424	        evidence.statusSnapshot.materializationIdentityHash !=
   425	            evidence.materializationIdentity.identityHash ||
   426	        evidence.statusSnapshot.members.size() !=
   427	            evidence.scientificMembers.size())
   428	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   429	            "campaign_outcome_assessment_evidence_snapshot_mismatch");
   430	
   431	    std::map<int, const
   432	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentScientificMemberEvidence*>
   433	        scientificByOrdinal;
   434	    for (const auto& member : evidence.scientificMembers)
   435	        if (member.memberOrdinal <= 0 ||
   436	            !scientificByOrdinal.emplace(
   437	                member.memberOrdinal, &member).second)
   438	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   439	                "campaign_outcome_assessment_scientific_member_invalid");
   440	
   441	    std::vector<MemberEvidence> members;
   442	    members.reserve(evidence.statusSnapshot.members.size());
   443	    for (const auto& member : evidence.statusSnapshot.members)
   444	    {
   445	        const auto scientific = scientificByOrdinal.find(
   446	            member.memberOrdinal);
   447	        if (scientific == scientificByOrdinal.end())
   448	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   449	                "campaign_outcome_assessment_scientific_member_missing");
   450	        const bool inconsistent = member.consistency ==
   451	                RecommendationCampaignStatusConsistency::inconsistent ||
   452	            scientific->second->inputConsistency ==
   453	                Consistency::Inconsistent;
   454	        members.emplace_back(
   455	            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberIdentity(
   456	                member.memberOrdinal, member.materializationMemberId,
   457	                member.rankingMemberId, member.recommendationId,
   458	                member.sourceExperimentId, member.proposalId,
   459	                member.experimentId),
   460	            RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleFromCampaignStatus(
   461	                member.terminalResult),
   462	            scientific->second->sourceEvidence,
   463	            scientific->second->resultEvidence,
   464	            inconsistent ? Consistency::Inconsistent
   465	                         : Consistency::Consistent);
   466	    }
   467	    return BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
   468	        evidence.campaignIdentity, evidence.materializationIdentity,
   469	        evidence.statusSnapshot.observedAt, members);
   470	}
   471	
   472	void WriteRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
   473	    std::ostream& output,
   474	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment& assessment)
   475	{
   476	    StreamStateGuard state{output};
   477	    std::ostringstream defaults;
   478	    output.flags(defaults.flags());
   479	    output.precision(defaults.precision());
   480	    output.imbue(std::locale::classic());
   481	    PrintAssessment(output, assessment);
   482	}
   483	
   484	int RunRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCommand(
   485	    const std::string& connectionString,
   486	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest& request,
   487	    std::ostream& output,
   488	    std::ostream& errors)
   489	{
   490	    try
   491	    {
   492	        const auto normalized =
   493	            NormalizeRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest(request);
   494	        pqxx::connection connection{connectionString};
   495	        const auto evidence =
   496	            ReadRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidence(
   497	                connection, normalized);
   498	        WriteRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(output,
   499	            BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentFromEvidence(
   500	                evidence));
   501	        return 0;
   502	    }
   503	    catch (const std::exception& error)
   504	    {
   505	        errors << "RECOMMENDATION_CAMPAIGN_OUTCOME_ASSESSMENT_FAILED"
   506	               << ",campaign_approval_id=null,"
   507	                  "campaign_approval_contract_version="
   508	               << kRecommendationCampaignApprovalContractVersion
   509	               << ",campaign_approval_identity_hash=null,materialization_id="
   510	               << request.materializationId
   511	               << ",materialization_contract_version=null,"
   512	                  "materialization_campaign_approval_identity_hash=null,"
   513	                  "materialization_identity_hash=null,"
   514	                  "campaign_status_contract_version="
   515	               << kRecommendationCampaignStatusContractVersion
   516	               << ","
   517	                  "assessment_contract_version="
   518	               << kRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentContractVersion
   519	               << ",assessment_identity_hash=null,observed_at=null,"
   520	                  "aggregate_outcome=inconsistent,diagnostic="
   521	               << RecommendationMachineText(error.what())
   522	               << ",member_count=0,";
   523	        PrintSafety(errors);
   524	        errors << '\n';
   525	        return 1;
   526	    }
   527	}

   670	std::optional<ResultEvidence> BuildResultEvidence(
   671	    const RecommendationCampaignStatusMember& member,
   672	    const std::map<long long, ExperimentRecord>& experiments,
   673	    const std::map<long long, std::optional<long long>>& models,
   674	    const std::vector<InferenceRecord>& inferenceRecords,
   675	    const std::vector<AnalysisRecord>& analysisRecords,
   676	    Consistency& consistency)
   677	{
   678	    std::vector<ResultIdentity> identities;
   679	    std::optional<Context> context;
   680	    std::vector<Metric> metrics;
   681	
   682	    if (member.experimentId && member.modelId)
   683	    {
   684	        const auto experiment = experiments.find(*member.experimentId);
   685	        const auto model = models.find(*member.modelId);
   686	        if (model == models.end() || !model->second ||
   687	            model->second != member.experimentId)
   688	            consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   689	        if (experiment != experiments.end())
   690	        {
   691	            const auto exactInference = ExactInferenceRecords(
   692	                inferenceRecords, SelectionFrom(experiment->second),
   693	                *member.modelId, false);
   694	            for (const auto* record : exactInference)
   695	                if (record->id > 0)
   696	                    identities.emplace_back(
   697	                        "inference_eval_result", record->id);
   698	                else
   699	                    consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   700	            if (exactInference.size() == 1)
   701	            {
   702	                const auto mapped = ContextFrom(
   703	                    *exactInference.front(), consistency);
   704	                if (mapped) context.emplace(*mapped);
   705	            }
   706	            else if (exactInference.size() > 1)
   707	                consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   708	
   709	            const auto exactAnalysis = ExactAnalysisRecords(analysisRecords,
   710	                *member.experimentId, *member.modelId);
   711	            for (const auto* record : exactAnalysis)
   712	                if (record->analysisId > 0)
   713	                    identities.emplace_back(
   714	                        "experiment_analysis_result", record->analysisId);
   715	                else
   716	                    consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   717	            if (exactAnalysis.size() == 1)
   718	                metrics = Metrics(exactAnalysis.front()->inferenceAccuracy,
   719	                    exactAnalysis.front()->leaderScore, true, consistency);
   720	            else if (exactAnalysis.size() > 1)
   721	                consistency = Consistency::Inconsistent;
   722	        }
   723	    }
   724	
   725	    const bool succeeded = member.terminalResult ==
   726	        RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::succeeded;
   727	    if (!succeeded && identities.empty()) return std::nullopt;
   728	    return ResultEvidence(member.experimentId, member.modelId,
   729	        std::move(identities), std::move(context),
   730	        MetricCollection(std::move(metrics)));
   731	}
   732	
   733	std::vector<ScientificMember> LoadScientificMembers(
   734	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
   735	    const RecommendationCampaignStatusSnapshot& status)
   736	{
   737	    std::vector<long long> recommendationIds;
   738	    std::vector<long long> proposalIds;
   739	    std::set<long long> experimentIds;
   740	    std::set<long long> modelIds;
   741	    recommendationIds.reserve(status.members.size());
   742	    proposalIds.reserve(status.members.size());
   743	    for (const auto& member : status.members)
   744	    {
   745	        recommendationIds.push_back(member.recommendationId);
   746	        proposalIds.push_back(member.proposalId);
   747	        experimentIds.insert(member.sourceExperimentId);
   748	        if (member.experimentId) experimentIds.insert(*member.experimentId);
   749	    }
   750	    const auto sources = LoadSources(transaction, recommendationIds);
   751	    const auto proposalValues =
   752	        ListRecommendationConversionProposalsByIds(transaction, proposalIds);
   753	    std::map<long long, const PersistedRecommendationConversionProposal*>
   754	        proposals;
   755	    for (const auto& proposal : proposalValues)
   756	        if (!proposals.emplace(proposal.proposalId, &proposal).second)
   757	            throw std::runtime_error(
   758	                "campaign_outcome_assessment_duplicate_proposal_record");
   759	    for (const auto& [unused, source] : sources)
   760	    {
   761	        static_cast<void>(unused);
   762	        if (source.sourceModelId) modelIds.insert(*source.sourceModelId);
   763	    }
   764	    for (const auto& member : status.members)
   765	        if (member.modelId) modelIds.insert(*member.modelId);
   766	
   767	    const auto experiments = LoadExperiments(transaction, experimentIds);
   768	    const auto models = LoadModels(transaction, modelIds);
   769	    const auto inferenceRecords =
   770	        LoadInferenceRecords(transaction, modelIds);
   771	    const auto analysisRecords =
   772	        LoadAnalysisRecords(transaction, experimentIds);
   773	
   774	    std::vector<ScientificMember> result;
   775	    result.reserve(status.members.size());
   776	    for (const auto& member : status.members)
   777	    {
   778	        Consistency consistency = Consistency::Consistent;
   779	        auto sourceEvidence = BuildSourceEvidence(member, sources, proposals,
   780	            models, inferenceRecords, analysisRecords, consistency);
   781	        auto resultEvidence = BuildResultEvidence(member, experiments,
   782	            models, inferenceRecords, analysisRecords,
   783	            consistency);
   784	        result.push_back({member.memberOrdinal, consistency,
   785	            std::move(sourceEvidence), std::move(resultEvidence)});
   786	    }
   787	    return result;
   788	}

   794	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest& request)
   795	{

   799	    return request;

   802	bool RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSchemasExist(
   803	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction)
   804	{
   805	    if (!RecommendationCampaignStatusSchemasExist(transaction) ||
   806	        !RecommendationCampaignApprovalSchemaExists(transaction))
   807	        return false;
   808	    return transaction.exec(R"SQL(
   809	SELECT NOT EXISTS (
   810	  SELECT 1 FROM (VALUES
   811	    ('experiment_recommendation','recommendation_id'),
   812	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_experiment_id'),
   813	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_model_id'),
   814	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_analysis_id'),
   815	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_symbol'),
   816	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_prediction_horizon'),
   817	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_leader_score'),
   818	    ('experiment_recommendation','source_infer_accuracy'),
   819	    ('experiment','c_next_threshold'),('experiment','infer_start'),
   820	    ('experiment','infer_end'),('experiment','last_model_id'),
   821	    ('model','model_id'),('model','experiment_id'),
   822	    ('inference_eval_result','id'),('inference_eval_result','model_id'),
   823	    ('inference_eval_result','symbol'),
   824	    ('inference_eval_result','prediction_horizon'),
   825	    ('inference_eval_result','threshold_logret'),
   826	    ('inference_eval_result','window_size'),
   827	    ('inference_eval_result','label_rule_id'),
   828	    ('inference_eval_result','target_type'),
   829	    ('inference_eval_result','from_date'),
   830	    ('inference_eval_result','to_date'),
   831	    ('inference_eval_result','status'),
   832	    ('inference_eval_result','inference_scope'),
   833	    ('inference_eval_result','checkpoint_eval_id'),
   834	    ('experiment_analysis_result','analysis_id'),
   835	    ('experiment_analysis_result','experiment_id'),
   836	    ('experiment_analysis_result','model_id'),
   837	    ('experiment_analysis_result','analysis_status'),
   838	    ('experiment_analysis_result','analysis_scope'),
   839	    ('experiment_analysis_result','infer_accuracy'),
   840	    ('experiment_analysis_result','leader_score')
   841	  ) required(table_name,column_name)
   842	  WHERE NOT EXISTS (
   843	    SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns c
   844	    WHERE c.table_schema=current_schema()
   845	      AND c.table_name=required.table_name
   846	      AND c.column_name=required.column_name));
   847	)SQL").one_row()[0].as<bool>();
   848	}
   849	
   850	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidenceSnapshot
   851	LoadRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidence(
   852	    pqxx::transaction_base& transaction,
   853	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest& request)
   854	{
   855	    const auto normalized =
   856	        NormalizeRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest(request);
   857	    if (!RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentSchemasExist(transaction))
   858	        throw std::runtime_error(
   859	            "campaign_outcome_assessment_schemas_required");
   860	
   861	    const auto materialization = FindRecommendationCampaignMaterialization(
   862	        transaction, normalized.materializationId);
   863	    if (!materialization)
   864	        throw std::runtime_error(
   865	            "campaign_outcome_assessment_materialization_not_found");
   866	    const auto approval = FindRecommendationCampaignApproval(
   867	        transaction, materialization->campaignApprovalId);
   868	    if (!approval)
   869	        throw std::runtime_error(
   870	            "campaign_outcome_assessment_campaign_approval_not_found");
   871	    if (approval->evidence.approvalIdentityHash !=
   872	            materialization->approvalIdentityHash)
   873	        throw std::runtime_error(
   874	            "campaign_outcome_assessment_approval_materialization_mismatch");
   875	
   876	    auto status = LoadRecommendationCampaignStatusSnapshot(
   877	        transaction, {normalized.materializationId});
   878	    auto scientific = LoadScientificMembers(transaction, status);
   879	    return {
   880	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentCampaignIdentity(
   881	            approval->campaignApprovalId,
   882	            approval->evidence.approvalIdentityCanonical,
   883	            approval->evidence.approvalIdentityHash),
   884	        RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMaterializationIdentity(
   885	            materialization->materializationId,
   886	            materialization->campaignApprovalId,
   887	            materialization->approvalIdentityHash,
   888	            materialization->contractVersion,
   889	            materialization->selectedMemberCount,
   890	            materialization->identityCanonical,
   891	            materialization->identityHash),
   892	        std::move(status), std::move(scientific)};
   893	}
   894	
   895	RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidenceSnapshot
   896	ReadRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidence(
   897	    pqxx::connection& connection,
   898	    const RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRequest& request)
   899	{
   900	    pqxx::read_transaction transaction{connection};
   901	    transaction.exec(
   902	        "SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ, READ ONLY;");
   903	    return LoadRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentEvidence(
   904	        transaction, request);
   905	}

    16	std::string LengthText(const std::string& value)
    17	{
    18	    return std::to_string(value.size()) + ":" + value;
    19	}
    20	
    21	bool HasControlOrNul(const std::string& value)
    22	{
    23	    return std::any_of(value.begin(), value.end(), [](unsigned char value) {
    24	        return value == 0 || value < 0x20 || value == 0x7f;
    25	    });
    26	}
    27	
    28	std::string TrimAscii(const std::string& value)
    29	{
    30	    const auto blank = [](unsigned char character) {
    31	        return character == ' ' || character == '\t' || character == '\n' ||
    32	               character == '\r' || character == '\f' || character == '\v';
    33	    };
    34	    std::size_t begin = 0;
    35	    while (begin < value.size() && blank(value[begin])) ++begin;
    36	    std::size_t end = value.size();
    37	    while (end > begin && blank(value[end - 1])) --end;
    38	    return value.substr(begin, end - begin);
    39	}

    51	        << ";ranking_position=" << candidate.input.rankingPosition

    53	        << ";source_experiment_id=" << candidate.input.sourceExperimentId
