    57	        << LengthText(candidate.input.recommendationSemanticHash)
    58	        << ";recommendation_invocation="
    59	        << LengthText(candidate.input.recommendationInvocationCanonical)
    60	        << ";recommendation_invocation_hash="
    61	        << LengthText(candidate.input.recommendationInvocationHash)
    62	        << ";proposal_identity="
    63	        << LengthText(proposal.conversionIdentityCanonical)
    64	        << ";proposal_identity_hash="
    65	        << LengthText(proposal.conversionIdentityHash);
    66	    return out.str();
    67	}
    68	
    69	std::string MaterializationCanonical(
    70	    const RecommendationCampaignMaterializationEvidence& evidence)
    71	{
    72	    std::ostringstream out;
    73	    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
    74	    out << "experiment_recommendation_campaign_materialization_v1"
    75	        << ";contract_version=" << evidence.materializationContractVersion
    76	        << ";campaign_approval_id=" << evidence.campaignApprovalId
    77	        << ";campaign_approval_identity="
    78	        << LengthText(evidence.approval.approvalIdentityCanonical)
    79	        << ";campaign_approval_identity_hash="
    80	        << LengthText(evidence.approval.approvalIdentityHash)
    81	        << ";ranking_snapshot_id=" << evidence.approval.rankingSnapshotId
    82	        << ";ranking_snapshot_identity="
    83	        << LengthText(evidence.approval.rankingSnapshotIdentityCanonical)
    84	        << ";ranking_snapshot_identity_hash="
    85	        << LengthText(evidence.approval.rankingSnapshotIdentityHash)
    86	        << ";planning_policy="
    87	        << LengthText(evidence.approval.planningPolicyCanonical)
    88	        << ";planning_policy_hash="
    89	        << LengthText(evidence.approval.planningPolicyHash)
    90	        << ";planning_scope="
    91	        << LengthText(evidence.approval.planningScopeCanonical)
    92	        << ";campaign_plan_identity="
    93	        << LengthText(evidence.approval.campaignPlanIdentityCanonical)
    94	        << ";campaign_plan_identity_hash="
    95	        << LengthText(evidence.approval.campaignPlanIdentityHash)
    96	        << ";campaign_review_identity="
    97	        << LengthText(evidence.approval.campaignReviewIdentityCanonical)
    98	        << ";campaign_review_identity_hash="
    99	        << LengthText(evidence.approval.campaignReviewIdentityHash)
   100	        << ";approval_decision="
   101	        << RecommendationCampaignApprovalDecisionText(
   102	               evidence.approval.decision)
   103	        << ";approval_reviewer="
   104	        << LengthText(evidence.approval.reviewerIdentity)
   105	        << ";approval_reason=" << LengthText(evidence.approval.reasonText)
   106	        << ";materialized_by=" << LengthText(evidence.operatorIdentity)
   107	        << ";materialization_reason=" << LengthText(evidence.reasonText)
   108	        << ";selected_member_count=" << evidence.members.size();
   109	    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < evidence.members.size(); ++i)
   110	    {
   111	        const auto& member = evidence.members[i];
   112	        out << ";member[" << i << "].identity="
   113	            << LengthText(member.selectedMemberIdentityCanonical)
   114	            << ";member[" << i << "].identity_hash="
   115	            << LengthText(member.selectedMemberIdentityHash)
   116	            << ";member[" << i << "].proposal_identity="
   117	            << LengthText(member.proposal.conversionIdentityCanonical)
   118	            << ";member[" << i << "].proposal_identity_hash="
   119	            << LengthText(member.proposal.conversionIdentityHash);
   120	    }
   121	    return out.str();
   122	}
   123	
   124	} // namespace
   125	
   126	RecommendationCampaignMaterializationRequest
   127	NormalizeRecommendationCampaignMaterializationRequest(
   128	    const RecommendationCampaignMaterializationRequest& request)
   129	{
   130	    RecommendationCampaignMaterializationRequest normalized = request;
   131	    normalized.operatorIdentity = TrimAscii(request.operatorIdentity);
   132	    normalized.reasonText = TrimAscii(request.reasonText);
   133	    if (normalized.campaignApprovalId <= 0 ||
   134	        normalized.operatorIdentity.empty() || normalized.reasonText.empty() ||
   135	        normalized.operatorIdentity.size() >
   136	            kRecommendationCampaignMaterializationOperatorMaximum ||
   137	        normalized.reasonText.size() >
   138	            kRecommendationCampaignMaterializationReasonMaximum ||
   139	        HasControlOrNul(normalized.operatorIdentity) ||
   140	        HasControlOrNul(normalized.reasonText))
   141	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   142	            "recommendation_campaign_materialization_request_invalid");
   143	    return normalized;
   144	}
   145	
   146	RecommendationCampaignMaterializationEvidence
   147	BuildRecommendationCampaignMaterializationEvidence(
   148	    const RecommendationCampaignMaterializationRequest& request,
   149	    const RecommendationCampaignApprovalEvidence& approval,
   150	    const RecommendationCampaignPlan& plan,
   151	    const std::vector<ProposedExperimentSpecification>& proposals)
   152	{
   153	    const auto normalized =
   154	        NormalizeRecommendationCampaignMaterializationRequest(request);
   155	    ValidateRecommendationCampaignApprovalEvidence(approval);
   156	    if (approval.decision != RecommendationCampaignApprovalDecision::approved)
   157	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   158	            "recommendation_campaign_materialization_approval_rejected");
   159	    if (approval.campaignPlanIdentityCanonical != plan.identityCanonical ||
   160	        approval.campaignPlanIdentityHash != plan.identityHash ||
   161	        approval.summary.selectedCount <= 0 ||
   162	        approval.summary.selectedCount != plan.summary.selectedCount)
   163	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   164	            "recommendation_campaign_materialization_approval_stale");
   165	
   166	    std::vector<const RecommendationCampaignPlanCandidate*> selected;
   167	    for (const auto& candidate : plan.candidates)
   168	        if (candidate.decision == RecommendationCampaignDecision::include)
   169	            selected.push_back(&candidate);
   170	    if (selected.size() != proposals.size() ||
   171	        selected.size() != static_cast<std::size_t>(approval.summary.selectedCount))
   172	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   173	            "recommendation_campaign_materialization_member_count_mismatch");
   174	
   175	    RecommendationCampaignMaterializationEvidence evidence;
   176	    evidence.campaignApprovalId = normalized.campaignApprovalId;
   177	    evidence.approval = approval;
   178	    evidence.operatorIdentity = normalized.operatorIdentity;
   179	    evidence.reasonText = normalized.reasonText;
   180	    evidence.selectedMemberCount = static_cast<int>(selected.size());
   181	    std::set<long long> rankingMembers;
   182	    std::set<std::string> proposalCanonicals;
   183	    for (std::size_t index = 0; index < selected.size(); ++index)
   184	    {
   185	        const auto& candidate = *selected[index];
   186	        const auto& proposal = proposals[index];
   187	        if (candidate.input.recommendationId != proposal.recommendationId ||
   188	            candidate.input.sourceExperimentId != proposal.sourceExperimentId ||
   189	            !rankingMembers.insert(candidate.input.rankingMemberId).second ||
   190	            !proposalCanonicals.insert(
   191	                proposal.conversionIdentityCanonical).second)
   192	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   193	                "recommendation_campaign_materialization_member_invalid");
   194	        RecommendationCampaignMaterializationSelectedMember member;
   195	        member.memberOrdinal = static_cast<int>(index) + 1;
   196	        member.rankingMemberId = candidate.input.rankingMemberId;
   197	        member.recommendationId = candidate.input.recommendationId;
   198	        member.sourceExperimentId = candidate.input.sourceExperimentId;
   199	        member.rankingPosition = candidate.input.rankingPosition;
   200	        member.proposal = proposal;
   201	        member.selectedMemberIdentityCanonical = SelectedMemberCanonical(
   202	            member.memberOrdinal, candidate, proposal);
   203	        member.selectedMemberIdentityHash = RecommendationCanonicalHash(
   204	            member.selectedMemberIdentityCanonical);
   205	        evidence.members.push_back(std::move(member));
   206	    }
   207	    evidence.materializationIdentityCanonical =
   208	        MaterializationCanonical(evidence);
   209	    evidence.materializationIdentityHash = RecommendationCanonicalHash(
   210	        evidence.materializationIdentityCanonical);
   211	    ValidateRecommendationCampaignMaterializationEvidence(evidence);
   212	    return evidence;
   213	}
   214	
   215	void ValidateRecommendationCampaignMaterializationEvidence(
   216	    const RecommendationCampaignMaterializationEvidence& evidence)
   217	{
   218	    if (evidence.materializationContractVersion !=
   219	            kRecommendationCampaignMaterializationContractVersion ||
   220	        evidence.campaignApprovalId <= 0 ||
   221	        evidence.approval.decision !=
   222	            RecommendationCampaignApprovalDecision::approved ||
   223	        evidence.selectedMemberCount <= 0 ||
   224	        evidence.selectedMemberCount !=
   225	            static_cast<int>(evidence.members.size()) ||
   226	        evidence.operatorIdentity.empty() || evidence.reasonText.empty() ||
   227	        evidence.operatorIdentity.size() >
   228	            kRecommendationCampaignMaterializationOperatorMaximum ||
   229	        evidence.reasonText.size() >
   230	            kRecommendationCampaignMaterializationReasonMaximum ||
   231	        HasControlOrNul(evidence.operatorIdentity) ||
   232	        HasControlOrNul(evidence.reasonText) ||
   233	        evidence.materializationIdentityCanonical.empty() ||
   234	        evidence.materializationIdentityCanonical.size() >
   235	            kRecommendationCampaignMaterializationIdentityCanonicalMaximum ||
   236	        evidence.materializationIdentityHash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(
   237	            evidence.materializationIdentityCanonical) ||
   238	        MaterializationCanonical(evidence) !=
   239	            evidence.materializationIdentityCanonical)
   240	        throw std::invalid_argument(
   241	            "recommendation_campaign_materialization_evidence_invalid");
   242	    ValidateRecommendationCampaignApprovalEvidence(evidence.approval);
   243	    std::set<int> ordinals;
   244	    std::set<long long> rankingMembers;
   245	    std::set<std::string> proposalCanonicals;
   246	    for (const auto& member : evidence.members)
   247	    {
   248	        if (member.memberOrdinal <= 0 ||
   249	            member.memberOrdinal > evidence.selectedMemberCount ||
   250	            member.rankingMemberId <= 0 || member.recommendationId <= 0 ||
   251	            member.sourceExperimentId <= 0 || member.rankingPosition <= 0 ||
   252	            member.proposal.recommendationId != member.recommendationId ||
   253	            member.proposal.sourceExperimentId != member.sourceExperimentId ||
   254	            member.selectedMemberIdentityCanonical.empty() ||
   255	            member.selectedMemberIdentityHash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(
   256	                member.selectedMemberIdentityCanonical) ||
   257	            member.proposal.conversionIdentityHash != RecommendationCanonicalHash(
   258	                member.proposal.conversionIdentityCanonical) ||
   259	            !ordinals.insert(member.memberOrdinal).second ||
   260	            !rankingMembers.insert(member.rankingMemberId).second ||
   261	            !proposalCanonicals.insert(
   262	                member.proposal.conversionIdentityCanonical).second)
   263	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   264	                "recommendation_campaign_materialization_member_invalid");
   265	    }
   266	    for (int ordinal = 1; ordinal <= evidence.selectedMemberCount; ++ordinal)
   267	        if (!ordinals.contains(ordinal))
   268	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   269	                "recommendation_campaign_materialization_member_ordinal_invalid");
   270	}
   271	
   272	} // namespace EA::ExperimentRecommendation

The main extension seam is viable: Phase 6 can compose the public transaction-bound Step 5B loader, the pure Step 5A builder, and `ApplyRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy` without changing any of them. One constraint is important for the plan: persisted Phase 6 records should treat the upstream canonical texts as opaque, versioned evidence and compare full canonical text—not attempt to hydrate or reimplement private Step 5A/5C objects from hashes.

template <std::size_t... Indices>
consteval bool SummaryConstructorIsPublic(std::index_sequence<Indices...>)
{
    return std::is_constructible_v<Summary, AggregateOutcome,
        decltype((void)Indices, int{})...>;
}

static_assert(!SummaryConstructorIsPublic(std::make_index_sequence<25>{}));

template <typename Function>
    AssertInvalidArgument([]
    {
        (void)BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(Campaign(),
            Materialization(1), "", {Succeeded(1)});
    });
    AssertInvalidArgument([]
    {
        (void)MemberEvidence(Identity(1, std::nullopt), Lifecycle::Unknown,
            std::nullopt, std::nullopt,
            static_cast<Consistency>(999));
    });

    const MemberEvidence overflowingDelta(
        Identity(1, ResultExperiment(1)), Lifecycle::Succeeded,
        Source(1, {NumericMetric("wide",-
            std::numeric_limits<double>::max())}),
        Result(1, {NumericMetric("wide",
            std::numeric_limits<double>::max())}));
    const auto overflowAssessment = Assess({overflowingDelta});
    assert(overflowAssessment.members[0].diagnostics ==
        std::vector<Diagnostic>{Diagnostic::NumericDeltaNonFinite});
    assert(overflowAssessment.members[0].comparisons.empty());
    assert(overflowAssessment.members[0].outcome == MemberOutcome::Inconsistent);

    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleStateText(
               Lifecycle::NotTerminal) == "not_terminal");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleStateText(
               Lifecycle::Succeeded) == "succeeded");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleStateText(
               Lifecycle::Failed) == "failed");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleStateText(
               Lifecycle::Cancelled) == "cancelled");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentLifecycleStateText(
               Lifecycle::Unknown) == "unknown");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentConsistencyStateText(
               Consistency::Consistent) == "consistent");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentConsistencyStateText(
               Consistency::Inconsistent) == "inconsistent");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentMemberOutcomeText(
               MemberOutcome::SucceededMetricGap) == "succeeded_metric_gap");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentAggregateOutcomeText(
               AggregateOutcome::HasFailures) == "has_failures");

    return 0;

RecommendationCampaignStatusMemberInput StatusMember(
    int ordinal,
    RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult terminal =
        RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::succeeded,
    std::optional<long long> experimentId = std::nullopt)
{
    RecommendationCampaignStatusMemberInput member;
    member.memberOrdinal = ordinal;
    member.materializationMemberId = 100 + ordinal;
    member.rankingMemberId = 200 + ordinal;
    member.recommendationId = 300 + ordinal;
    member.sourceExperimentId = 400 + ordinal;
    member.proposalId = 500 + ordinal;
    member.currentReviewDecisionId = 600 + ordinal;
    member.currentReviewDecision = "approve";
    if (terminal == RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::notTerminal)
        return member;

    member.authorizationReviewDecisionId = 600 + ordinal;
    member.executionId = 700 + ordinal;
    member.activationId = 800 + ordinal;
    member.experimentId = experimentId.value_or(900 + ordinal);
    member.executionCount = 1;
    member.activationCount = 1;
    RecommendationCampaignStatusExperimentEvidence experiment;
    experiment.experimentId = *member.experimentId;
    experiment.symbol = "eurusd";
    experiment.predictionHorizon = 12;
    experiment.targetEpochs = 120;
    experiment.invocationMode = "recommendation_conversion";
    experiment.invocationIdentityCanonical = "integration-invocation";
    experiment.invocationProvenanceValid = true;
    if (terminal == RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::succeeded)
    {
        experiment.status = "completed";
        experiment.phase = "done";
        experiment.completedAt = "2026-07-19 12:00:00+00";
        experiment.modelId = 1000 + ordinal;
        experiment.modelLinkCount = 1;
        experiment.inferStartPresent = true;
        experiment.inferEndPresent = true;
        experiment.inferConfigured = true;
        experiment.inferenceResultCount = 1;
        experiment.completedInferenceResultCount = 1;
        experiment.analysisResultCount = 1;
        experiment.completedAnalysisResultCount = 1;
    }
    else if (terminal == RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::failed)
    {
        experiment.status = "failed";
        experiment.phase = "train";
        experiment.completedAt = "2026-07-19 12:00:00+00";
        experiment.errorMessage = "worker_failed";
    }
    else if (terminal == RecommendationCampaignStatusTerminalResult::cancelled)
    {
        experiment.status = "cancelled";
        experiment.phase = "train";
        experiment.completedAt = "2026-07-19 12:00:00+00";
    }
    else
    {
        experiment.status = "malformed";
        experiment.phase = "unknown";
    }
    member.experiment = std::move(experiment);
    return member;
}

RecommendationCampaignStatusSnapshot StatusSnapshot(
    std::vector<RecommendationCampaignStatusMemberInput> members)
{
    RecommendationCampaignStatusInput input;
    input.materializationId = 71;
    input.materializationContractVersion =
        kRecommendationCampaignMaterializationContractVersion;
    input.materializationIdentityCanonical =
        "integration-materialization-canonical";
    input.materializationIdentityHash = RecommendationCanonicalHash(
        input.materializationIdentityCanonical);
    input.selectedMemberCount = static_cast<int>(members.size());
    input.observedAt = "2026-07-19 12:00:00+00";
    input.members = std::move(members);
    return BuildRecommendationCampaignStatusSnapshot({71}, std::move(input));
}

Context ComparisonContext(
    std::string symbol = "eurusd",
    double threshold = 0.001,
    int windowSize = 64,
        std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find("result_model_id=1001") !=
        std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find(
        "materialization_campaign_approval_identity_hash=" +
        comparable.materializationIdentity.campaignIdentityHash) !=
        std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find(
        "campaign_approval_contract_version=1") != std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find(
        "campaign_status_contract_version=1") != std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find(
        "input_consistency=consistent,final_consistency=consistent") !=
        std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find("source_evidence_present=true") !=
        std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find("result_evidence_present=true") !=
        std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find("result_context_present=true") !=
        std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find("transaction_read_only=true") !=
        std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find("assessment_persisted=false") !=
        std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find("automatic_follow_up=false") !=
        std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find("campaign_success_declared=false") !=
        std::string::npos);
    assert(firstOutput.str().find("follow_up_authorized=false") !=
        std::string::npos);

    std::ostringstream inconsistentOutput;
    WriteRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
        inconsistentOutput, scientificInconsistent);
    assert(inconsistentOutput.str().find(
        "input_consistency=inconsistent,final_consistency=inconsistent") !=
        std::string::npos);

    std::ostringstream proposalOnlyOutput;
    WriteRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment(
        proposalOnlyOutput, proposalOnly);
    assert(proposalOnlyOutput.str().find("source_evidence_present=false") !=
        std::string::npos);
    assert(proposalOnlyOutput.str().find("result_evidence_present=false") !=
        std::string::npos);
    assert(proposalOnlyOutput.str().find("result_context_present=false") !=
        std::string::npos);

    return 0;
}

    assert(ordered.summary == shuffled.summary);
    assert(ordered.identity == shuffled.identity);

    const Assessment laterObservation = Assess({Comparable(1)},
        "2026-07-20 12:05:00+00");
    const Decision later = ApplyRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy(
        defaultPolicy, laterObservation);
    assert(favorable.observedAt != later.observedAt);
    assert(favorable.assessmentIdentityHash == later.assessmentIdentityHash);
    assert(favorable.identity == later.identity);

    // Canonical identities are independent of the global locale.
    const std::locale originalLocale = std::locale();
    std::locale::global(std::locale(originalLocale, new CommaNumpunct));
    const Policy localizedPolicy =
        BuildRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy();
    const Decision localized = Decide({Comparable(1)}, localizedPolicy);
    std::locale::global(originalLocale);
    assert(localizedPolicy.identity == defaultPolicy.identity);
    assert(localized.identity == favorable.identity);

    const Decision changedEvidence = Decide({Succeeded(1, SourceMetrics(),
        {Numeric("inference_accuracy", 0.76),
            Numeric("leader_score", 0.82)})});
    assert(changedEvidence.assessmentIdentityHash !=
        favorable.assessmentIdentityHash);
    assert(changedEvidence.identity.hash != favorable.identity.hash);
    assert(minimumNotMet.policy.identity.hash != defaultPolicy.identity.hash);
    assert(minimumNotMet.identity.hash != favorable.identity.hash);

    // Ambiguous top-level membership is rejected by the immutable Step 5a
    // boundary before policy can interpret it.
    AssertInvalidArgument([]
    {
        (void)Assess({Comparable(1), Comparable(1)});
    });

    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyEvidenceSufficiencyText(
        EvidenceSufficiency::Sufficient) == "sufficient");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyCampaignInterpretationText(
        CampaignInterpretation::Mixed) == "mixed");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyFollowUpEligibilityText(
        FollowUpEligibility::EligibleForOperatorReview) ==
        "eligible_for_operator_review");
    assert(RecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicyReasonText(
        PolicyReason::EvidenceInsufficient) == "evidence_insufficient");

    return 0;

   435	std::string CanonicalRecommendationDouble(double value)
   436	{
   437	    if (!std::isfinite(value))
   438	        throw std::invalid_argument("recommendation_identity_nonfinite_number");
   439	    if (value == 0.0) value = 0.0; // Canonicalize IEEE negative zero.
   440	    std::array<char, 128> buffer{};
   441	    const auto result = std::to_chars(
   442	        buffer.data(), buffer.data() + buffer.size(), value,
   443	        std::chars_format::general);
   444	    if (result.ec != std::errc{})
   445	        throw std::runtime_error("recommendation_identity_number_format_failed");
   446	    return std::string(buffer.data(), result.ptr);
   447	}

    22	std::vector<std::string> Split(const std::string& text, char delimiter)
    23	{
    24	    std::vector<std::string> values;
    25	    std::string current;
    26	    std::istringstream input{text};
    27	    while (std::getline(input, current, delimiter))
    28	        values.push_back(current);
    29	    if (!text.empty() && text.back() == delimiter)
    30	        values.emplace_back();
    31	    return values;
    32	}
    33	
    34	bool IsAsciiWhitespace(char value)
    35	{
    36	    switch (value)
    37	    {
    38	        case ' ': case '\t': case '\n': case '\r': case '\f': case '\v':
    39	            return true;
    40	        default:
    41	            return false;
    42	    }
    43	}
    44	
    45	std::string TrimAsciiWhitespace(const std::string& value)
    46	{
    47	    size_t begin = 0;
    48	    while (begin < value.size() && IsAsciiWhitespace(value[begin])) ++begin;
    49	    size_t end = value.size();
    50	    while (end > begin && IsAsciiWhitespace(value[end - 1])) --end;
    51	    return value.substr(begin, end - begin);
    52	}
    53	
    54	bool ParseBoolean(const std::string& key, const std::string& value)
    55	{
    56	    if (value == "true" || value == "1") return true;
    57	    if (value == "false" || value == "0") return false;
    58	    throw std::invalid_argument(
    59	        "invalid_recommendation_policy_boolean:key=" + key);
    60	}
    61	
    62	int ParsePositiveInteger(const std::string& key, const std::string& value)
    63	{
    64	    size_t consumed = 0;
    65	    long long parsed = 0;
    66	    try { parsed = std::stoll(value, &consumed, 10); }
    67	    catch (...) {
    68	        throw std::invalid_argument(
    69	            "invalid_recommendation_policy_integer:key=" + key);
    70	    }
    71	    if (consumed != value.size() || parsed <= 0 ||
    72	        parsed > std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
    73	        throw std::invalid_argument(
    74	            "invalid_recommendation_policy_integer:key=" + key);
    75	    return static_cast<int>(parsed);
    76	}
    77	
    78	double ParseFiniteDouble(const std::string& key, const std::string& value)
    79	{
    80	    double parsed = 0.0;
    80	    double parsed = 0.0;
    81	    const auto result = std::from_chars(
    82	        value.data(), value.data() + value.size(), parsed,
    83	        std::chars_format::general);
    84	    if (result.ec != std::errc{} ||
    85	        result.ptr != value.data() + value.size() ||
    86	        !std::isfinite(parsed))
    87	        throw std::invalid_argument(
    88	            "invalid_recommendation_policy_number:key=" + key);
    89	    return parsed;
    90	}
    91	
    92	std::vector<double> ParseDoubleList(
    93	    const std::string& key,
    94	    const std::string& value)
    95	{
    96	    if (value.empty()) return {};
    97	    std::vector<double> result;
    98	    for (const std::string& rawItem : Split(value, ':'))
    99	    {
   100	        const std::string item = TrimAsciiWhitespace(rawItem);
   101	        if (item.empty())
   102	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   103	                "invalid_recommendation_policy_list:key=" + key);
   104	        result.push_back(ParseFiniteDouble(key, item));
   105	    }
   106	    return result;
   107	}
   108	
   109	std::vector<int> ParseIntegerList(
   110	    const std::string& key,
   111	    const std::string& value)
   112	{
   113	    if (value.empty()) return {};
   114	    std::vector<int> result;
   115	    for (const std::string& rawItem : Split(value, ':'))
   116	    {
   117	        const std::string item = TrimAsciiWhitespace(rawItem);
   118	        if (item.empty())
   119	            throw std::invalid_argument(
   120	                "invalid_recommendation_policy_list:key=" + key);

   147	std::string SortedStringSetText(const std::set<std::string>& values)
   148	{
   149	    std::ostringstream out;
   150	    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
   151	    bool first = true;
   152	    for (const std::string& value : values)
   153	    {
   154	        if (!first) out << ':';
   155	        first = false;
   156	        out << value;
   157	    }
   158	    return out.str();
   159	}
   160	
   161	std::string StableRecommendationHash(const std::string& canonicalText)
   162	{
   163	    // Canonical text, not this compact accelerator, is authoritative identity.
   164	    // Future persistence must store and compare both after a hash match.
   165	    std::uint64_t hash = 14695981039346656037ULL;
   166	    for (const unsigned char byte : canonicalText)
   167	    {
   168	        hash ^= byte;
   169	        hash *= 1099511628211ULL;
   170	    }
   171	    constexpr char digits[] = "0123456789abcdef";
   172	    std::array<char, 16> encoded{};
   173	    for (size_t index = 0; index < encoded.size(); ++index)
   174	    {
   175	        const unsigned shift = static_cast<unsigned>(
   176	            (encoded.size() - 1 - index) * 4);
   177	        encoded[index] = digits[(hash >> shift) & 0x0fU];
   178	    }
   179	    return "fnv1a64:" + std::string(encoded.begin(), encoded.end());
   180	}

codex
## 1. Overall readiness

The architecture is ready to begin Phase 6. No architectural defect was found, and no files were modified.

The dependency direction remains valid:

```text
Phase 4 immutable evidence/materialization
    ↓
Step 5A assessment
    ↓
Step 5C policy decision
    ↓
future Phase 6 operator workflow
```

Step 5B is correctly an infrastructure/reporting adapter beside Step 5A: it loads authoritative evidence and builds the assessment without introducing policy. Step 5C includes only the Step 5A contract. Nothing in Phase 4 or Step 5A depends on Step 5C or a future workflow.

The transaction-bound Step 5B loader, Step 5A builder, and Step 5C application function provide sufficient extension points for a new Phase 6 module without modifying completed phases:

- [Step 5B loader](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRepository.cpp:850>)
- [Step 5A evidence builder](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentService.cpp:416>)
- [Step 5C policy application](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy.cpp:1166>)

## 2. Architecture strengths

- Assessment and policy are immutable, privately constructed domain values with explicit non-persistent and non-authorizing semantics: [assessment contract](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.hpp:466>) and [policy decision contract](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy.hpp:376>).
- Step 5B reuses the existing campaign-status classification rather than rebuilding lifecycle logic.
- Evidence is loaded through one repeatable-read, read-only snapshot.
- Canonical identities bind complete provenance, ordered membership, comparisons, policy, interpretations, and summaries.
- Collections and member ordering are deterministic; numeric formatting is locale-independent.
- Canonical text—not the FNV-1a hash—is explicitly authoritative: [hash contract](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendation.cpp:161>).
- `observed_at` is correctly treated as display metadata, allowing unchanged evidence to retain the same identity.
- Approval, materialization, execution, and activation are already modeled as distinct operations, providing a strong precedent for Phase 6.
- The recommendation command dispatcher remains outside scheduler polling, despite sharing its translation unit: [standalone dispatch](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentScheduler.cpp:17075>).

## 3. Architecture weaknesses

None are blockers.

- CLI parsing and scheduler implementation are physically co-located in `ExperimentScheduler.cpp`. Their control flow is separated, but every new command carries a small scheduler-regression risk.
- The Step 5B service header exposes its repository and therefore `pqxx`. This is infrastructure coupling, not contamination of the Step 5A domain.
- Step 5C deliberately has no formatter, service, repository, or deserializer. Phase 6 must add its own presentation and persistence types rather than treating the validation view as a hydration API.
- Assessment and decision canonical texts may be large because they embed upstream canonical evidence. Phase 6 should define its own explicit persistence-size bounds.
- “Campaign materialization” is already a Phase 4D term. A Phase 6 outcome/follow-up materialization needs an unambiguous name to prevent repository and CLI confusion.

## 4. Mandatory work before Phase 6

No remedial work is required in Phase 4 or Steps 5A–5C.

The first Phase 6 task should nevertheless freeze its own boundary before any migration:

- Define the Phase 6 entity name and authority semantics.
- State that `eligible_for_operator_review` permits review only; it is not approval, activation, execution, or scheduler authority.
- Define idempotency using full canonical equality after a hash match.
- Define whether Phase 6 materialization is produced in the same repeatable-read transaction as any additional typed Phase 4 evidence it needs.
- Record assessment v2, policy v1, and decision v1 as immutable upstream contracts.

These are Phase 6 design tasks, not prerequisites requiring changes to completed phases.

## 5. Optional improvements

- Add a new Phase 6 composition service returning assessment, policy decision, and typed immutable materialization together.
- Put Phase 6 formatting in a separate reporting component.
- Add explicit maximum sizes for Phase 6 canonical and operator text.
- Eventually separate recommendation CLI dispatch from the scheduler translation unit. This is desirable but unrelated to Phase 6 readiness.
- Splitting the combined Phase 5 documentation into 5A/5B/5C documents would be cosmetic.

## Responsibility boundaries

| Responsibility | Boundary |
|---|---|
| Assessment | Step 5A derives scientific facts only. |
| Policy | Step 5C interprets an immutable assessment only. |
| Reporting | Formats existing values; never derives policy or authority. |
| Operator workflow | Orchestrates typed assessment, decision, and immutable Phase 6 materialization. |
| Recommendation persistence | Stores the Phase 6 artifact and exact upstream canonical evidence. |
| Approval | Separate append-only human decision. |
| Activation | Explicitly consumes an effective approval but performs no execution. |
| Execution | Uses an accepted lifecycle/conversion boundary; never embeds policy. |
| Scheduler | Sees only its existing eligible lifecycle work unless separately authorized by an ADR. |
| Repository | Owns SQL, transactions, collision handling, and row mapping only. |
| CLI | Parses, confirms, invokes one service, and presents results. |

## 6. Recommended Phase 6 decomposition

1. **6A — Contract and pure materialization:** Build an immutable outcome/follow-up recommendation from the exact assessment, policy decision, and required typed member material. Preserve `followUpAuthorized=false`.
2. **6B — Read-only preview/reporting:** Present the materialization and its complete provenance without persistence or recomputation.
3. **6C — Persistence:** Add append-only storage, full canonical comparison, immutable members, runtime permissions, and retrieval types.
4. **6D — Operator approval/rejection:** Record an append-only decision against an expected materialization identity.
5. **6E — Activation:** Separate explicit activation referencing the effective approval; no experiment creation or scheduler action.
6. **6F — Execution adapter:** Invoke the accepted lifecycle/conversion boundary and record its exact provenance.
7. **Scheduler integration:** Exclude unless Phase 6 explicitly requires a new work class and separately defines eligibility, claims, capacity, recovery, and shutdown.

Downstream workflow code should consume typed immutable values. It must not parse Step 5B machine output, reopen scientific evidence, recompute deltas, or reproduce Step 5C eligibility logic.

## 7. Smallest independently testable first milestone

A database-free Phase 6 materialization builder with:

- Exact assessment/decision identity-alignment validation.
- Deterministic canonical text and hash.
- Immutable ordered membership.
- One eligible case and conservative refusal cases.
- Explicit advisory/non-authorizing flags.
- Golden identity, locale, ordering, changed-input, and `observed_at` invariance tests.

This milestone requires no migration, repository, CLI, scheduler, or completed-phase modification.

## 8. Suggested implementation order and minimum testing

Implementation order:

1. Phase 6 contract/ADR and identity grammar.
2. Pure materialization builder.
3. Read-only formatter/preview.
4. Additive migration and repository.
5. Persistence service and CLI.
6. Approval/rejection.
7. Activation.
8. Execution adapter.
9. Scheduler work only under a separate explicit decision.

Minimum testing:

- Reuse existing Step 5 fixtures; do not duplicate the assessment or policy truth tables.
- Pure tests for Phase 6 identity, mismatches, non-authorization, and deterministic ordering.
- Migration/repository tests for constraints, permissions, append-only behavior, exact retry, hash collision, rollback, and one same-identity concurrency race.
- One database-backed happy-path service test and one refusal path.
- CLI confirmation/mutual-exclusion tests plus one scheduler-isolation regression.
- Before/after experiment and scheduler-state digests for all pre-execution slices.

The targeted Step 5A assessment, Step 5B integration/reporting, and Step 5C policy test executables all passed. The `phase5` worktree remains clean.

The dependency direction remains valid:

```text
Phase 4 immutable evidence/materialization
    ↓
Step 5A assessment
    ↓
Step 5C policy decision
    ↓
future Phase 6 operator workflow
```

Step 5B is correctly an infrastructure/reporting adapter beside Step 5A: it loads authoritative evidence and builds the assessment without introducing policy. Step 5C includes only the Step 5A contract. Nothing in Phase 4 or Step 5A depends on Step 5C or a future workflow.

The transaction-bound Step 5B loader, Step 5A builder, and Step 5C application function provide sufficient extension points for a new Phase 6 module without modifying completed phases:

- [Step 5B loader](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentRepository.cpp:850>)
- [Step 5A evidence builder](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessmentService.cpp:416>)
- [Step 5C policy application](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy.cpp:1166>)

## 2. Architecture strengths

- Assessment and policy are immutable, privately constructed domain values with explicit non-persistent and non-authorizing semantics: [assessment contract](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomeAssessment.hpp:466>) and [policy decision contract](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendationCampaignOutcomePolicy.hpp:376>).
- Step 5B reuses the existing campaign-status classification rather than rebuilding lifecycle logic.
- Evidence is loaded through one repeatable-read, read-only snapshot.
- Canonical identities bind complete provenance, ordered membership, comparisons, policy, interpretations, and summaries.
- Collections and member ordering are deterministic; numeric formatting is locale-independent.
- Canonical text—not the FNV-1a hash—is explicitly authoritative: [hash contract](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentRecommendation.cpp:161>).
- `observed_at` is correctly treated as display metadata, allowing unchanged evidence to retain the same identity.
- Approval, materialization, execution, and activation are already modeled as distinct operations, providing a strong precedent for Phase 6.
- The recommendation command dispatcher remains outside scheduler polling, despite sharing its translation unit: [standalone dispatch](</Volumes/Developer SSD/ExpertAdvisor/Sources/ExperimentScheduler.cpp:17075>).

## 3. Architecture weaknesses

None are blockers.

- CLI parsing and scheduler implementation are physically co-located in `ExperimentScheduler.cpp`. Their control flow is separated, but every new command carries a small scheduler-regression risk.
- The Step 5B service header exposes its repository and therefore `pqxx`. This is infrastructure coupling, not contamination of the Step 5A domain.
- Step 5C deliberately has no formatter, service, repository, or deserializer. Phase 6 must add its own presentation and persistence types rather than treating the validation view as a hydration API.
- Assessment and decision canonical texts may be large because they embed upstream canonical evidence. Phase 6 should define its own explicit persistence-size bounds.
- “Campaign materialization” is already a Phase 4D term. A Phase 6 outcome/follow-up materialization needs an unambiguous name to prevent repository and CLI confusion.

## 4. Mandatory work before Phase 6

No remedial work is required in Phase 4 or Steps 5A–5C.

The first Phase 6 task should nevertheless freeze its own boundary before any migration:

- Define the Phase 6 entity name and authority semantics.
- State that `eligible_for_operator_review` permits review only; it is not approval, activation, execution, or scheduler authority.
- Define idempotency using full canonical equality after a hash match.
- Define whether Phase 6 materialization is produced in the same repeatable-read transaction as any additional typed Phase 4 evidence it needs.
- Record assessment v2, policy v1, and decision v1 as immutable upstream contracts.

These are Phase 6 design tasks, not prerequisites requiring changes to completed phases.

## 5. Optional improvements

- Add a new Phase 6 composition service returning assessment, policy decision, and typed immutable materialization together.
- Put Phase 6 formatting in a separate reporting component.
- Add explicit maximum sizes for Phase 6 canonical and operator text.
- Eventually separate recommendation CLI dispatch from the scheduler translation unit. This is desirable but unrelated to Phase 6 readiness.
- Splitting the combined Phase 5 documentation into 5A/5B/5C documents would be cosmetic.

## Responsibility boundaries

| Responsibility | Boundary |
|---|---|
| Assessment | Step 5A derives scientific facts only. |
| Policy | Step 5C interprets an immutable assessment only. |
| Reporting | Formats existing values; never derives policy or authority. |
| Operator workflow | Orchestrates typed assessment, decision, and immutable Phase 6 materialization. |
| Recommendation persistence | Stores the Phase 6 artifact and exact upstream canonical evidence. |
| Approval | Separate append-only human decision. |
| Activation | Explicitly consumes an effective approval but performs no execution. |
| Execution | Uses an accepted lifecycle/conversion boundary; never embeds policy. |
| Scheduler | Sees only its existing eligible lifecycle work unless separately authorized by an ADR. |
| Repository | Owns SQL, transactions, collision handling, and row mapping only. |
| CLI | Parses, confirms, invokes one service, and presents results. |

## 6. Recommended Phase 6 decomposition

1. **6A — Contract and pure materialization:** Build an immutable outcome/follow-up recommendation from the exact assessment, policy decision, and required typed member material. Preserve `followUpAuthorized=false`.
2. **6B — Read-only preview/reporting:** Present the materialization and its complete provenance without persistence or recomputation.
3. **6C — Persistence:** Add append-only storage, full canonical comparison, immutable members, runtime permissions, and retrieval types.
4. **6D — Operator approval/rejection:** Record an append-only decision against an expected materialization identity.
5. **6E — Activation:** Separate explicit activation referencing the effective approval; no experiment creation or scheduler action.
6. **6F — Execution adapter:** Invoke the accepted lifecycle/conversion boundary and record its exact provenance.
7. **Scheduler integration:** Exclude unless Phase 6 explicitly requires a new work class and separately defines eligibility, claims, capacity, recovery, and shutdown.

Downstream workflow code should consume typed immutable values. It must not parse Step 5B machine output, reopen scientific evidence, recompute deltas, or reproduce Step 5C eligibility logic.

## 7. Smallest independently testable first milestone

A database-free Phase 6 materialization builder with:

- Exact assessment/decision identity-alignment validation.
- Deterministic canonical text and hash.
- Immutable ordered membership.
- One eligible case and conservative refusal cases.
- Explicit advisory/non-authorizing flags.
- Golden identity, locale, ordering, changed-input, and `observed_at` invariance tests.

This milestone requires no migration, repository, CLI, scheduler, or completed-phase modification.

## 8. Suggested implementation order and minimum testing

Implementation order:

1. Phase 6 contract/ADR and identity grammar.
2. Pure materialization builder.
3. Read-only formatter/preview.
4. Additive migration and repository.
5. Persistence service and CLI.
6. Approval/rejection.
7. Activation.
8. Execution adapter.
9. Scheduler work only under a separate explicit decision.

Minimum testing:

- Reuse existing Step 5 fixtures; do not duplicate the assessment or policy truth tables.
- Pure tests for Phase 6 identity, mismatches, non-authorization, and deterministic ordering.
- Migration/repository tests for constraints, permissions, append-only behavior, exact retry, hash collision, rollback, and one same-identity concurrency race.
- One database-backed happy-path service test and one refusal path.
- CLI confirmation/mutual-exclusion tests plus one scheduler-isolation regression.
- Before/after experiment and scheduler-state digests for all pre-execution slices.

The targeted Step 5A assessment, Step 5B integration/reporting, and Step 5C policy test executables all passed. The `phase5` worktree remains clean.
